### [1] [RAIWAY: A Large-Wheeled Bipedal Robot with Contact-Informed Blind Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33172)
*Kyungmin Nam,Jiseong Lee,Donghyuk Choi,Jeongsoo Park,Woojin Kim,Jemin Hwangbo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出轮足机器人RAIWAY（20英寸轮）及一种接触信息引导的控制方法，通过将仿真中的多接触事件蒸馏为隐表示，在真实部署时仅用本体感知在线推断该表示，实现无需接触传感的非规则/不连续地形滚动运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮式滚动运动本质上是接触主导的，局部轮-地相互作用难以直接测量，且不仅取决于地形几何；因此需要一种不依赖接触传感、又能利用接触信息的控制框架。

Method: 构建RAIWAY轮足平台；在仿真中获取多接触事件并蒸馏为紧凑的隐表示（latent representation）；实际部署时控制器从本体感知信号在线推断该隐表示，从而间接“感知”接触状态，无需安装接触传感器。

Result: 在真实实验中穿越陡坡、砾石地、凸起表面和岩石地形，并在室外楼梯上实现连续上行（踏步高度达15 cm）；在室内外多类挑战场景中，所提控制器优于无接触引导机制的基线方法。

Conclusion: 利用仿真接触信息蒸馏+本体感知在线推断的范式，可在无接触传感条件下有效提升轮足机器人在复杂不连续地形上的滚动运动能力。

Abstract: We present RAIWAY, an in-house wheeled bipedal robot with 20-inch wheels, together with a contact-informed control approach for rolling locomotion across irregular and discontinuous terrain. Such locomotion is fundamentally contact-dominated, governed by local wheel--terrain interaction that is impractical to instrument directly and depends on more than terrain geometry alone. The proposed framework exploits the contact information available in simulation, distilling multi-contact events into a latent representation. At deployment, the controller infers this representation online from proprioception, requiring no contact sensing. In real-world experiments, the proposed system traverses diverse environments including steep slopes, gravel beds, studded surfaces, and rocky terrain, and demonstrates continuous ascent on the tested outdoor staircases with riser heights up to 15~cm. On our platform, the proposed controller further outperforms baselines without the contact-informed mechanism across challenging indoor and outdoor settings. The video can be found at https://youtu.be/Ch0divLg64w.

</details>


### [2] [Bracketing Shows the Way: Adaptive Multi-Exposure Policy as Input for Robust Visual SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33174)
*Olivier Gamache,Anthony Lavertu,David-Alexandre Duclos,Jean-Michel Fortin,François Pomerleau,Philippe Giguère*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出B-Adapt自适应曝光包围与BVSLAM多曝光因子图估计方法，缓解快速光照变化对视觉SLAM的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速光照变化（如隧道出口）会使自动曝光策略尚未适应前图像过饱和，破坏视觉对应关系，导致跟踪失效。

Method: B-Adapt采集自适应曝光包围以覆盖场景动态范围并保证光度重叠；BVSLAM将多曝光序列直接作为因子图状态估计中的约束，无需HDR融合。

Result: 在标称环境和32 m/s模拟车速下重复隧道出口转换场景中评估；结合B-Adapt与BVSLAM相比AE图像降低状态估计误差78%。

Conclusion: 包围曝光能有效提升视觉位姿估计对快速光照干扰的鲁棒性，同时在标称条件下保持低误差。

Abstract: Rapid illumination transitions can disturb Visual SLAM (VSLAM) by saturating images before the Automatic Exposure (AE) policy adapts, removing the visual correspondences needed to maintain tracking. Our proposed methods mitigate this disturbance at two levels: B-Adapt acquires adaptive exposure brackets to cover the scene dynamic range while promoting photometric overlap, and BVSLAM directly uses the multi-exposure sequence as constraints in a factor graph state estimator without relying on High Dynamic Range (HDR) merging. We evaluate both methods against state-of-the-art approaches in nominal environments and during a repeated tunnel-exit transition at an emulated vehicle speed of 32 m s-1. In the tunnel, combining our B-Adapt with BVSLAM reduces the state estimation error by 78 % compared with AE images. These results suggest that bracketing efficiently improves visual pose estimation robustness to rapid illumination disturbances while retaining low error under nominal conditions. Our code is available at https://github.com/norlab-ulaval/bracketing-shows-the-way.

</details>


### [3] [Interpretable Collision Avoidance and Feasibility Guarantees through an Uncertainty Aware Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33186)
*Doris Xu,Jin Hyun Park,Guadalupe Alvarado Fernandez,Adhyan Prasad,Mark Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出模块化感知-预测-规划流水线，将不确定性以结构化可解释量跨模块传递，在上游消解碰撞概率并在可行域内构建凸安全集，从而把安全性保证与求解器可行性解耦，实现0碰撞、0不可行、12.7ms/周期的实时避撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶在交叉口、环岛等动态复杂场景下，既要保证不确定性下的无碰撞，又要保证实时可行。现有方法难以兼顾：机会约束方法把安全约束嵌入优化，随不确定性累积会变得不可行；确定性规划器虽有可行解却不处理导致碰撞的不确定性；端到端学习策略无法给出可解释的双重保证。

Method: 采用模块化感知-预测-规划架构，将不确定性作为结构化、可解释的量在各模块边界传播；不把概率风险直接放入优化问题，而是在上游先消解碰撞概率，规划器再在由此构造的凸安全集内求解，保证每个周期都存在可行解，从而实现安全保证与求解器可行性的解耦。

Result: 在四路无信号交叉口的蒙特卡洛试验中，流水线达到0%碰撞率与0%求解不可行率，单周期耗时12.7ms；相比之下，确定性基线与机会约束基线的碰撞率分别为36%和10%。消融实验验证了各不确定性来源的必要性，并在Clearpath Jackal无人地面车上的硬件部署验证了实时可行性。

Conclusion: 通过不确定性结构化传播与安全保证—可行性解耦的设计，该流水线在保持实时性的同时提供了强安全保证，并在物理平台上验证了部署可行性，为动态复杂场景下的AV规划提供了可解释且可保证的替代方案。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) operating in dynamic, challenging conditions such as intersections and roundabouts must guarantee collision avoidance under uncertainty while maintaining real-time feasibility. Most existing planners struggle to simultaneously guarantee both requirements. Chance-constrained formulations embed safety constraints directly in the optimization but become infeasible as uncertainty compounds; deterministic planners provide feasible solutions but fail to address the uncertainty that causes collisions; end-to-end learned policies provide neither guarantee in an interpretable form.
  We present a modular perception-prediction-planning pipeline that closes this gap by propagating uncertainty as structured, interpretable quantities across module boundaries. Rather than embedding probabilistic risk directly in the optimization, our pipeline resolves collision probabilities upstream and constructs convex safe sets within which the planner provides a guaranteed feasible solution at every cycle -- decoupling the safety guarantee from solver feasibility.
  Across Monte Carlo trials in a four-way unsignalized intersection, our pipeline achieves 0% collisions and 0% solver infeasibility at 12.7ms per cycle, compared to 36% and 10% collision rates for deterministic and chance-constrained baselines respectively. The ablation study confirms the necessity of each uncertainty source and the hardware deployment on Clearpath Jackal UGVs validates the pipeline's real-time viability on physical platforms.

</details>


### [4] [Causal-based approaches to explain and learn from self-extension - A review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33227)
*Rebeca Marfil,Pablo Bustos,Antonio Bandera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文指出，单纯依赖深度学习的自主机器人缺乏对行为原因的内部理解，限制了其性能、可靠性与可信度；因此需要一种以深层推理为核心的自省式架构，使机器人能够生成解释、与人类共享并验证解释，进而把解释融入知识库以自我进化，从而提升能力并获得人类信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习赋予机器人强大的感知能力，自动规划使其能自主完成复杂任务，但机器人往往只是对外部状态做出即时反应，并未内化和理解某个感知或动作背后的原因。这一缺口影响了机器人的性能、可靠性和可信度。仅靠深度学习无法弥合，因为其世界模型是黑箱，行为背后的理由不可获取。因此需要一种基于深层推理的底层架构。

Method: 提出（综述/展望）一种以深层推理为基础的自省式、自进化机器人架构设想。该架构应使机器人能够：1）为所执行或已执行的动作生成解释；2）说明可能导致规划失败或感知异常的缘由；3）将源于认知过程并与人类共享、经人类验证的解释纳入自身知识库；4）利用这种理解改进未来行为。文章回顾了自意识、自进化机器人方面的近期进展。

Result: 文章梳理了近期在自意识、自进化机器人开发方面的进展，指出这类机器人被设计为能向人类提供必要的解释，从而增强其功能能力，并致力于赢得人类信任。

Conclusion: 要实现可靠、可信的自主机器人，必须超越黑箱式深度学习，构建支持深层推理与解释生成的底层架构；通过解释的生成、共享、人类验证与知识吸收形成闭环，机器人可实现自我进化并提升功能能力，最终获得人类信任。

Abstract: The last decades have seen a revolution in autonomous robotics. Deep learning approaches and their hardware implementations have made it possible to endow robots with extraordinary perceptual capabilities. In addition, they can also benefit from advances in Automated Planning, allowing them to autonomously solve complex tasks. However, on many occasions, the robot still acts without internalising and understanding the reasons behind a perception or an action, beyond an immediate response to a current state of the context. This gap results in limitations that affect its performance, reliability and trustworthiness. Deep learning alone cannot bridge this gap, because the reasons behind behaviour, when it emanates from a model in which the world is a black-box, are not accessible. What is really needed is an underlying architecture based on deeper reasoning. Among other issues, this architecture should enable the robot to generate explanations, allowing people to know why the robot is performing, or has performed, a certain action, or the reasons that may have caused a certain plan failure or perceptual anomaly. Furthermore, when these explanations arise from a cognitive process and are shared, and thus validated, with people, the robot should be able to incorporate these explanations into its knowledge base, and thus use this understanding to improve future behaviour. Our article looks at recent advances in the development of self-aware, self-evolving robots. These robots are designed to provide the necessary explanations to their human counterparts, thereby enhancing their functional capabilities, in the quest to gain their trust.

</details>


### [5] [ORBIT: A Modular Multi-finger Robotic Hand Platform with Rotational Finger Reconfiguration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33241)
*Benito Ribadeneira,Siqi Shang,Lillian Chin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ORBIT——一种带旋转指节机构的模块化多指手平台，可在1秒内快速重构手指布局，用于系统比较不同手指构型（形态学）对抓取与操作任务的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指灵巧手虽被广泛研究，但缺乏可对不同手部形态进行"同条件对比"的系统化平台，导致难以判断何种形态适合何种任务。

Method: 设计ORBIT平台：核心为皮带驱动的旋转指节机构，独立手指可绕圆柱形手掌运动，实现无需拆装的快速手指布局重构；再用三指实例进行试点抓取实验，比较拟人型、等距型与Barrett型三种布局在11个尺寸/形状/纹理各异物体上的表现，并演示轨道自由度带来的新能力。

Result: 手指平均角速度1.57 rad/s，指根抗外力50 N，重构时间<1 s；三指实验完成三种布局的对比抓取；借助非拟人轨道自由度完成立方体面重定向、拧瓶盖和亚厘米级精密捏取等固定指节设计难以完成的任务。

Conclusion: ORBIT可作为针对特定操作任务直接、公平比较多种多指手形态的可行平台。

Abstract: Despite the popularity of multi-fingered robotic hands for dexterous manipulation, there is no systematic platform to compare different hand morphologies against one another. We present ORBIT (Orbital Reconfigurable Base-actuated Integrated Theta-motion Hand), a modular platform designed to enable principled exploration of multi-fingered hand morphology. The core contribution of ORBIT is a rotating knuckle mechanism, where independent fingers travel around a cylindrical palm via a belt drive. This enables ORBIT to perform quick recon- figuration (< 1 s) of finger layout without hand disassembly. ORBITs a robust system, with finger average angular velocity of 1.57 rad / s1 and a root finger force resistance of 50 N. Using a three fingers instantiation of ORBIT, we demonstrate an initial pilot grasping study, comparing anthropomorphic, isometric, and Barrett-style finger layouts across eleven objects of varied size, shape, and texture. We further demonstrate how ORBITs nonanthropomorphic orbital DOF enables the achievement of tasks that would be difficult to perform with fixed-knuckle designs, such as cube face reorientation, lid threading, and precision pinching of sub-centimeter objects. These results establish ORBIT as a viable platform for direct apples to apples comparison of multi-fingered hand morphologies for specific manipulation tasks

</details>


### [6] [Neuromorphic Decision Dynamics for Deadlock-free Safe Navigation in Congested Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33271)
*Himani Sinhmar,Vaibhav Srivastava,Naomi Ehrich Leonard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出神经形态决策动力学（NDD）——一种去中心化、事件驱动的控制器，在拥堵空间中实现兼顾避碰与协调通行的导航，并从理论上保证安全性、活性、鲁棒性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拥堵环境中的安全导航同时需要碰撞避免与协调有序的通行。现有方法通常依赖通信、预分配优先级、对邻居目标或未来动作的建模，或对群体规模与全局网络性质的估计，去中心化条件下的安全与活性保障难以兼得。

Method: NDD 将内部决策状态与注意状态的动力学同变化的物理状态及局部观测到的相对运动耦合；每个智能体沿既定路径持续调制速度，按冲突消解需求尽快、尽可能多地响应。智能体自组织为局部交互、彼此不相交的组并并发消解冲突；当某组冲突进入安全临界时，决策—动作反馈使集体犹豫失稳，涌现出协调的冲突消解并稳定组内的安全通行顺序。作者推导了传感范围、反馈增益、响应时间尺度与执行器限制的显式条件，以保证有限异构、规模任意且可变的智能体群体在有限时间内避碰并形成通行顺序，且无死锁或活锁。

Result: 理论分析给出上述显式条件与有限时间保障；计算代价随每个智能体所感知的局部交互数量扩展。大规模仿真、与现有方法的对比以及硬件实验在复杂拥堵场景中验证了 NDD 的有效性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: NDD 将安全且保活的冲突消解转化为局部智能体交互中可控、可局部调参的涌现性质，无需通信、预分配优先级、邻居模型或全局信息，为拥堵场景下的可扩展去中心化导航提供了有理论保障的方案。

Abstract: Safe navigation in congested environments requires both collision avoidance and coordinated passage. We introduce neuromorphic decision dynamics (NDD), a decentralized, event-based controller, that provides navigation in congested spaces with guarantees on safety, liveness, robustness, and scalability. NDD makes safe-and-live deconfliction a controllable, locally tunable emergent property of local agent interactions, without requiring communication, preassigned priorities, models of neighbors' objectives or future actions, or estimates of group size or global network properties. By design, NDD couples the dynamics of internal decision and attention states with changing physical states and locally observed relative motion. Each agent continuously modulates its speed along its prescribed path, responding as soon and as much as conflict resolution requires. Agents self-organize into disjoint, locally engaged groups that resolve conflicts concurrently. When any group's conflict becomes safety-critical, decision-action feedback destabilizes collective indecision, and coordinated conflict-resolution emerges that stabilizes a safe traversal order within the group. Order persists as conflicts clear, but groups may split or merge as agents autonomously adapt to new conflicts and those with resolved conflicts resume their nominal motion. We derive explicit conditions on sensing range, feedback gains, response time scales, and actuation limits that guarantee collision avoidance and traversal-order formation in finite time with no deadlock or livelock for finite, heterogeneous agent populations of arbitrary, changing size. Computational cost scales with the number of local interactions sensed by each agent. Large-scale simulations, comparisons with existing approaches, and hardware experiments demonstrate NDD in complex congested scenarios and validate its scalability.

</details>


### [7] [Tiny Recursive Policy: Recursive Latent Refinement for Visuomotor Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33288)
*Hakan Aktas,Cengiz Oztireli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Tiny Recursive Policy（TRP），一种参数高效的行为克隆策略，将持久时间状态与递归潜变量精炼相结合，在长时程与上下文依赖任务上显著优于大型生成式基线，且参数量更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行为克隆策略在每个预测步只从固定的观测窗口重建内部表示，执行过程中不维护持久的潜变量上下文，难以应对长时程和上下文依赖任务；同时大型生成式基线参数量庞大、效率低下。

Method: 在每个控制步，TRP使用共享计算模块反复更新高层与低层潜状态，再解码动作序列，并将更新后的状态传递到后续预测，从而同时具备持久时间状态与步内递归精炼；另外在训练中引入carry扰动以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在LIBERO-100、MetaWorld ML45和LIBERO-Mem上评测：多任务与少样本设置下表现具竞争力，长时程和上下文依赖任务提升明显，且参数量远少于大型生成式基线；执行时扰动实验表明对持久carry的依赖随上下文需求增强，训练中加入carry扰动显著提升对carry与视觉损坏的鲁棒性；消融显示持久状态与递归精炼存在强交互，carry收益强烈依赖步内计算量，性能无法仅用参数量解释。

Conclusion: 持久时间状态与步内递归精炼相辅相成，是实现高效、鲁棒且长时程能力强的行为克隆策略的关键设计，二者结合的效果不能由参数量单独解释。

Abstract: Behaviour cloning has become a powerful approach for robotic manipulation, but many modern policies reconstruct their internal representation from a fixed observation window at each prediction and do not maintain persistent latent context throughout execution. We introduce Tiny Recursive Policy (TRP), a parameter-efficient behaviour-cloning policy that combines persistent temporal state with recursive latent refinement. At each control step, TRP repeatedly updates high- and low-level latent states using a shared computation module before decoding an action sequence, and propagates the resulting state to subsequent predictions. We evaluate TRP across LIBERO-100, MetaWorld ML45, and the LIBERO-Mem benchmarks. TRP performs competitively across multi-task and few-shot settings and shows substantial gains on long-horizon and context-dependent tasks, while using significantly fewer parameters than large generative baselines. Execution-time perturbations show that reliance on the persistent carry increases with temporal-context demand, while training with carry perturbations substantially improves robustness to both carry and visual corruptions. Ablations reveal a strong interaction between persistent state and recursive refinement: the benefit of carry depends strongly on the amount of within-step computation, and the resulting performance cannot be explained by parameter count alone.

</details>


### [8] [Active Reachability Learning for Humanoid Recoverability Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33317)
*Zeyuan Feng,Baljeet Singh,Sarthak Ranjeet Kaingade,Somil Bansal,Bryan Trinh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种统一框架，通过价值学习与主动探索交替迭代，学习回退策略的随机有限时域可恢复性包络，从而在真实人形机器人上实现及时、可靠的跌倒预判监控器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人部署任务策略时需要运行时监控器，能够在不可恢复跌倒发生前及时触发回退。现有方法假设训练数据天然覆盖可恢复性包络，但对接触丰富的系统而言获取此类数据是主要瓶颈，导致只恢复部分真实包络，并在分布外输入上产生过于乐观的幻觉判断。

Method: 将数据探索重构为reach-avoid问题：由不确定性引导的探索器系统性发现未见状态，同时避开高置信度不可恢复区域；外层采用价值学习与主动探索交替迭代的统一框架；价值学习结合时序差分与蒙特卡洛目标，兼顾分布内价值预测精度与可靠的不确定性估计；并将剩余高不确定性区域显式视为不可恢复。

Result: 在随机基准测试和29自由度Unitree G1人形机器人上评估，监控器的覆盖率和准确率大幅提升，并可零样本迁移到真实硬件。

Conclusion: 将可恢复性监控建模为有限时域随机包络学习，并以"主动探索+不确定性保守处理"闭环解决数据覆盖瓶颈，是提升接触丰富系统跌倒预判可靠性与真实部署可行性的有效途径。

Abstract: Deploying humanoid task policies requires reliable runtime monitors that anticipate unrecoverable falls in time to trigger a fallback. We formulate this task as learning the stochastic, finite-horizon recoverability envelope of a given fallback policy. Synthesizing this monitor is challenging: existing methods assume training data naturally covers the recoverability envelope, yet acquiring such data remains a major bottleneck for contact-rich systems. Consequently, they recover only a fraction of the true envelope, leaving them prone to optimistic hallucinations for out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs. We resolve this via a unified framework that iterates between value learning and active exploration. Data exploration is reframed as a reach-avoid problem, where an uncertainty-guided explorer systematically uncovers unseen states while avoiding confidently unrecoverable regions. This loop is further supported by a combined Temporal Difference and Monte Carlo objective for accurate in-distribution value prediction and reliable uncertainty estimation. By explicitly treating the remaining high-uncertainty regions as unrecoverable, the monitor mitigates optimistic OOD hallucinations. Evaluated on stochastic benchmarks and a 29-DoF Unitree G1 humanoid, our approach drastically improves monitor coverage and accuracy and successfully transfers zero-shot to real-world hardware.

</details>


### [9] [TinyFlow: Breaking the Warping Bottleneck for Edge Optical Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33338)
*Xiao'ao Song,Chahat Deep Singh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TinyFlow 是一个 1.26M 参数、免 warp（warp-free）的光流网络，通过用固定窗口、常量偏移的相关性替代依赖当前光流的重采样，使整个网络可编译为单一静态 INT8 图，在保持迭代式由粗到细精化的同时适配资源受限的边缘加速器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能光流网络难以部署到资源受限的边缘加速器，因为其迭代精化需要用当前光流对特征进行 warp（重采样），这依赖流相关的重采样与数据相关的内存访问，无法被静态编译/量化部署。

Method: 1) 用基于常量偏移的固定窗口相关性（fixed-window correlation over constant offsets）替代 warp 操作，从而保留迭代式由粗到细精化结构，但整网可编译为单一静态 INT8 计算图；2) 采用量化感知微调（QAT）、warp 教师蒸馏（warp-teacher distillation）与模型汤（model souping）来压缩量化损失。

Result: 在 MPI-Sintel 上，Float32 到 INT8 的精度损失被压缩到 EPE 小于 0.2；在 Sintel Clean、Sintel Final 与 FlyingChairs 上，其在 Float32 和 INT8 下均优于 EdgeFlowNet 与 NanoFlowNet，且参数量比 EdgeFlowNet 少 54%；INT8 模型在 Sintel Clean/Final 上达到 6.29/7.13 EPE。在 TartanAir 与 UZH-FPV 的纯光流单目视觉里程计任务中，同一 INT8 模型在 11 条序列中的 7 条上取得最低轨迹误差。

Conclusion: 通过消除 warp 依赖并全静态 INT8 化，TinyFlow 在极低参数量下实现了边缘可部署的高精度光流估计，并可直接用于视觉里程计等下游任务，兼具精度与部署可行性。

Abstract: High-performance optical flow networks are difficult to deploy on resource-constrained edge accelerators because their iterative refinement warps features by the current flow, which relies on flow-dependent resampling and data-dependent memory access. We present TinyFlow, a 1.26M-parameter warp-free optical flow network that preserves iterative coarse-to-fine refinement by replacing the warp with a fixed-window correlation over constant offsets, so the whole network compiles as a single static INT8 graph. Quantization-aware fine-tuning, warp-teacher distillation, and model souping reduce the Float32-to-INT8 penalty to below 0.2 end-point error (EPE) on MPI-Sintel. TinyFlow outperforms EdgeFlowNet and NanoFlowNet on Sintel Clean, Sintel Final, and FlyingChairs in both Float32 and INT8, while using 54% fewer parameters than EdgeFlowNet, and our INT8 model achieves 6.29/7.13 EPE on Sintel Clean/Final. In flow-only monocular visual odometry on TartanAir and UZH-FPV, the same INT8 model yields the lowest trajectory error on seven of eleven sequences.

</details>


### [10] [MOTIO: Agentic Skill Engineering for Reality-Grounded Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33446)
*SpatialtemporalAI*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MOTIO 代理式技能工程系统，将人类动作视频、3D 资产、运动参考与可复用机器人技能转化为可执行的人形 loco-manipulation 程序，无需任务专属遥操作或机器人示教采集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人全身移动操作技能获取通常依赖任务专属遥操作与机器人演示采集，成本高、难以扩展；作者希望直接从普通人类动作视频与真实感 3D 资产等易得数据出发，自动构建机器人可执行程序。

Method: 通过上下文任务与接触推理，代理构建分阶段的机器人原生交互，并借助物理执行、多帧反馈与人工确认迭代改进；Agentic 3D 提供度量尺度的产品与环境资产，复用全身控制、逆运动学与灵巧手接口执行程序；另将代理生成技能用于 BC/DAgger 蒸馏为单一共享参考策略。

Result: 33 次带时间戳的首次审批中，代理单轮中位耗时 97.0 分钟，请求到审批时长 2.80 小时；仿真侧布局与物体变化支撑长时程瓶子丢弃技能在扫描办公室中连续 101.75 秒真机 G1 执行；物理细化使拧螺丝的螺纹残差降低 76.0%；蒸馏得到的共享策略在相同 checkpoint 下完成 18 个仿真任务，推理时无需专家介入。

Conclusion: MOTIO 证明了以人类视频与 3D 资产为起点、由代理驱动技能工程并引入物理反馈与人工验证的流水线，可生成可执行且可复用的类人 loco-manipulation 技能，并能蒸馏为单一通用策略，具备可扩展性。

Abstract: We present MOTIO, an agentic skill-engineering system that turns ordinary human-action videos, reality-grounded 3D assets, motion references, and reusable robot skills into executable humanoid loco-manipulation programs. Through in-context task and contact reasoning, an agent constructs staged robot-native interactions and improves them using physics execution, multi-frame feedback, and human validation---without task-specific teleoperation or robot demonstration collection. Agentic 3D supplies metric product and environment assets, while existing whole-body, inverse-kinematics, and dexterous-hand interfaces realize the programs. Across 33 timestamped first approvals, the median recorded agent-turn time is 97.0 minutes and request-to-approval duration is 2.80 hours. Simulation-side layout and object variation prepare a long-horizon bottle-disposal skill for a continuous 101.75-second physical G1 execution in a scanned office; physical refinement reduces screw-tightening thread residual by 76.0%. Agent-generated skills additionally supervise BC/DAgger distillation into one shared reference policy, completing 18 simulated tasks under the same checkpoint without inference-time expert intervention.
Project page: https://iiiiqiiii.github.io/motio/

</details>


### [11] [SCET-CBF: Speed-Calibrated Soft Guidance for One-Step Dynamic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33471)
*Xiaoyu Han,Dongkun Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SCET-CBF：一种速度校准的软制导律，用于单步动态避障导航，通过"短时横向逃逸机动"给出允许接近速度，并把刹车对接近速度与横向运动的双重影响纳入二次规划，在2500组配对任务上把完成率从49.8%（CBF/速度障碍）提升到72.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集动态障碍场景下，控制障碍函数（CBF）常导致可行指令集为空，车辆无解可行；几何修正与软约束虽能保留运动选项，但还需考虑车辆转向响应随速度变化的问题。对转向受限的单车模型而言，刹车既降低接近速度，也减慢障碍到达前所需的横向运动，二者存在权衡，需要显式建模。

Method: 1）设计一段短时横向逃逸机动，据此推导出允许的接近速度；2）由该速度的导数解析得到刹车对"接近速度"与"所需横向运动"的双重影响，使二次规划（QP）在选择加速度与转向时能同时权衡两者；3）采用软惩罚项，在由航段约束与制动约束共同限定的输入集内协调多个竞争性障碍的偏好；4）给出制导律推导，并证明每个被接受运动段上的保护性，以及单障碍情形下的条件制动不变性。

Result: 在2500组配对任务（60 s 时限）中，SCET 完成率72.8%，CBF/速度障碍控制为49.8%，增强型1 s CBF-MPC 为69.8%；在100个障碍场景下，SCET 与增强型 MPC 分别为52.4%和33.6%。配对消融识别出速度反馈与提前激活两项设计的贡献；进一步实验考察了横向加速度限制、有界运动误差与执行误差下的工作范围。

Conclusion: SCET-CBF 通过在速度层面校准制动效应、并结合软约束保留可行运动选择，显著缓解了密集动态遭遇中的可行性缺失问题，在完成率与高障碍密度场景上优于基线；其保护性与条件制动不变性具备理论支撑，并在横向加速度受限与存在有界误差时仍具一定适用性。

Abstract: Control barrier functions (CBFs) support collision avoidance, yet dense dynamic encounters can leave a vehicle with no feasible command. Geometric refinements and soft constraints help preserve motion choices. Using these choices also requires accounting for the vehicle's changing turning response. For a steering-limited bicycle, braking can lower closing speed while slowing the lateral motion needed before an obstacle arrives. We propose SCET-CBF, a speed-calibrated soft guidance law for one-step dynamic navigation. A short lateral escape maneuver gives an allowable approach speed. Its speed derivative allows a quadratic program to weigh both effects of braking when choosing acceleration and steering. Soft penalties accommodate competing obstacle preferences within the input set permitted by segment and braking constraints. We derive the guidance and establish protection over each accepted motion segment, together with conditional braking invariance for a single obstacle. On 2500 paired tasks with a 60 s deadline, SCET achieves 72.8% completion, compared with 49.8% for CBF/velocity-obstacle control and 69.8% for an enhanced 1 s CBF-MPC. At 100 obstacles, SCET and the enhanced MPC complete 52.4% and 33.6% of tasks. Paired studies identify the contributions of speed feedback and early activation. Further evaluations examine the operating range under lateral acceleration limits and bounded motion and execution errors.

</details>


### [12] [A Differentiable Chassis Safety Layer for Intelligent Vehicle Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33476)
*Lu Xiong,Xuge Hu,Yufei Song,Weidong Liu,Chen Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可微底盘安全层，将任务导向控制与显式物理约束模块化结合，通过SQP与隐式微分实现时序梯度耦合，并配合两阶段学习实现安全与性能的协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的车辆运动控制需要协调多样任务目标与状态依赖的底盘安全约束；传统级联架构中安全滤波器独立于策略学习修改标称指令，会损害任务性能。

Method: 构建可微分的底盘安全层，将执行器限制、单胎力约束与车辆操控包络纳入序列二次规划；每个投影后的指令传播预测的车辆动力学并更新后续约束，借助隐式微分实现时序梯度耦合；采用两阶段学习策略，结合专家模仿、风险引导监督与条件干预引导。

Result: 在未见场景、紧急机动的闭环评估及消融实验中，所提方法提升了安全性与性能的协调，并在保持操控稳定性的同时挖掘了车辆动力学潜力。

Conclusion: 可微安全层与两阶段学习相结合，可使标称控制决策与底盘安全约束协同优化，优于传统级联的安全滤波方案。

Abstract: Learning-based vehicle motion control requires coordination between diverse task objectives and state-dependent chassis safety constraints. Conventional cascaded architectures may compromise task performance when safety filters modify nominal commands independently of policy learning. This article proposes a differentiable chassis safety layer that modularly integrates task-oriented control with explicit physical constraints. Actuator limits, individual-tire force constraints, and vehicle handling envelopes are incorporated into sequential quadratic programs. Each projected command propagates the predicted vehicle dynamics and updates subsequent constraints, enabling temporal gradient coupling through implicit differentiation. A two-stage learning strategy combines expert imitation with risk-guided supervision and conditional intervention guidance to coordinate nominal control decisions with chassis safety constraints. Closed-loop evaluations under unseen scenarios, emergency maneuvers, and ablation studies demonstrate that the proposed method improves safety-performance coordination and exploits vehicle dynamic potential while maintaining handling stability.

</details>


### [13] [MDPO: Multi-constraint Dexterous Policy Optimization with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33480)
*Ao Zhou,Zongyou Yu,Ke Wu,Tao Xu,Junqiao Zhao,Wenchao Ding,Alois Knoll,Guang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多约束灵巧策略优化方法MDPO，将累积成本约束与逐状态成本约束统一建模，通过约束特定的策略投影实现安全灵巧操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作需要高维控制策略完成复杂接触任务，同时满足关节限位、不安全手指构型、机械臂碰撞、物体交互稳定性等多重安全要求。现有灵巧操作约束强化学习方法主要关注累积成本约束，难以同时处理多种异构约束，因此需要一种能同时兼顾累积与逐状态约束的策略学习方法。

Method: 首先将灵巧操作建模为同时包含累积成本约束和逐状态成本约束的多约束策略优化问题；然后在该建模基础上，将多约束投影集成到策略优化过程中，采用约束特定的策略投影，把最大化任务性能的中间策略映射到由累积成本约束和逐状态成本约束共同定义的可行域内。

Result: 在ReDMan平台的实验（包括安全关节控制、手指安全、抓取任务）表明，与基线方法相比，MDPO在保持有竞争力任务性能的同时实现了更优的约束满足效果。

Conclusion: MDPO为灵巧操作提供了一种可同时处理累积与逐状态异构安全约束的统一策略优化框架，验证了多约束投影在安全灵巧操作中的有效性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation requires high-dimensional control policies to accomplish complex contact-rich tasks while simultaneously satisfying multiple safety requirements, such as joint limits, unsafe finger configurations, arm collisions, and unstable object interactions. Constrained reinforcement learning (RL) provides a principled framework for incorporating these requirements into dexterous manipulation policy learning. However, existing constrained RL methods for dexterous manipulation primarily focus on cumulative cost constraints and struggle to simultaneously handle multiple heterogeneous constraints during dexterous policy learning. In this work, we propose Multi-constraint Dexterous Policy Optimization (MDPO) for safe dexterous manipulation. We first formulate dexterous manipulation as a multi-constraint policy optimization problem involving both cumulative cost constraints and state-wise cost constraints. Based on this formulation, we integrate multi-constraint projection into the dexterous manipulation policy optimization process. Specifically, we employ constraint-specific policy projections to map the intermediate policy that maximizes task performance onto the feasible regions defined by the cumulative cost constraint and the state-wise cost constraint. Extensive experiments on ReDMan, including safe joint control, finger safety, and grasping tasks, demonstrate that MDPO achieves superior constraint satisfaction while maintaining competitive task performance compared with the baselines. Our code is available at this link.

</details>


### [14] [Learning Dexterous Manipulation: Hands, Simulators, Training, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33486)
*Luai Abuelsamen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: This survey reviews 113 learning-based methods for in-hand object pose reorientation, mapping the dominant sim-to-real RL recipe, the rise of demonstration-driven policies, and the hardware, simulator, and reporting gaps it documents via released code and vendor specifications.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The subfield of changing an object's pose without setting it down has accumulated many papers but no consolidated picture of what hardware they run on, which simulators they train in, and how results are reported; the authors also suspect that released code and vendor specs contradict or supplement what method papers state, and that key physical quantities go unmeasured.

Method: A systematic survey of 113 learning-based papers, tabulating hands, simulators, task families, training paradigms, and evaluation practice. The authors additionally read released code and vendor specifications alongside the papers, and count which commercially or openly buildable hands appear in the literature.

Result: The field has converged on one recipe: RL on privileged state in a GPU simulator, distilled to a vision-only student for transfer. Almost half the corpus trains this way, nearly all in a single simulator whose successors are barely used. A second, faster-growing branch learns from teleoperated demonstrations via VLA models, diffusion policies, and rig-centric systems. Publications tripled since 2022. Grasping is the largest task family, bimanual coordination the fastest-rising, and nearly all experiments use one of four hands. Eight purchasable or openly buildable hands appear in no method paper. Penetration depth, when reported, is not measured in the physics that produced the motion.

Conclusion: The survey provides counts and a consolidated map of the field, and proposes in Section VII a protocol for the quantities currently left unreported, with the body supplying the evidence behind each claim.

Abstract: A dexterous hand can change an object's pose without setting it down. This survey covers 113 learning-based methods that attempt it, the hands they run on, the simulators they train in, and how they report results. The field has largely converged on one recipe: reinforcement learning on privileged state in a GPU simulator, distilled into a vision-only student for transfer to hardware. Almost half the corpus trains that way, nearly all of it in one simulator, whose own successors are barely used. A second and faster-growing branch learns from teleoperated demonstration instead, through vision-language-action models, diffusion policies, and systems whose contribution is the collection rig itself. Published work has tripled since 2022. Grasping remains the largest task family and bimanual coordination the fastest-rising, and almost every experiment runs on one of four hands. What the survey adds comes from reading released code and vendor specifications beside the papers. Eight hands that can be bought or built from published designs appear in no method paper here. Where the depth a hand sinks into the object it holds is reported at all, it is not measured in the physics that produced the motion. Section VII proposes a protocol for the unreported quantities; the body gives the counts behind every claim above.

</details>


### [15] [ReSight: Task-Driven Self-Improving Memory for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33487)
*Lirong Che,Yang Chen,Yimeng Su,Daqi Liu,Zhenyu Huang,Jiong Wang,Yuzhe Yang,Xu Cao,Chuang Wang,Jian Su,Xueqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReSight，一个面向具身智能体的任务驱动自改进记忆系统，通过 Explore-Distill-Mount（EDM）机制在无参考答案条件下发现能力缺口，将探索所得解转化为可复用的记忆构建器、查询工具和技能，从而随任务需求扩展对历史观测的处理与访问能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人可利用历史观测回答熟悉环境中的时空查询，但"存在相关证据"并不等于"现有工具支持任务所需操作"。即记忆访问能力与证据本身是解耦的，现有系统缺乏随任务需求动态扩展记忆处理与访问能力的机制。

Method: ReSight 保留关键帧及其时空关联，将观测证据与可扩展的派生字段分离，使存储的观测可按新需求重新处理。执行智能体与编码智能体通过不同接口共享该记忆：前者在有界在线预算内调用已注册工具，后者使用可编程工作区与更大探索预算来检视证据、编写程序并按需获取模型。EDM 从执行与探索记录中识别能力缺口（无需任务参考答案），将探索所得解蒸馏为可复用的记忆构建器、查询工具与技能。

Result: 在三个公开基准上验证了基础记忆访问与能力扩展的有效性。在每个基准内固定执行模型与在线预算的条件下，EDM 将 OC-NaVQA 的平均位置误差从 33.10 m 降至 24.72 m，将 FindingDory 的高层成功率从 38.3% 提升至 63.8%。冻结工具与技能后，在未参与探索与蒸馏的 FindingDory 片段上成功率仍提升 28.3 个百分点。真实机器人巡逻记忆实验显示这些能力可复用于后续查询与新的记忆，且平均在线工具调用次数更少。

Conclusion: 记忆系统的能力扩展可以与执行阶段解耦：通过探索—蒸馏—挂载的闭环，智能体能在无监督信号下自主补齐记忆处理与访问能力，并将成果固化为可复用资产，在后续任务与新建记忆中持续受益。

Abstract: Robots can use historical observations to answer spatiotemporal queries in familiar environments, but the presence of relevant evidence does not imply that existing tools support the operations a task requires. We introduce ReSight, a task-driven self-improving memory system for embodied agents that expands the capabilities for processing and accessing historical observations as task demands arise. ReSight retains keyframes and their spatiotemporal associations, separating observation evidence from extensible derived fields so that stored observations can be reprocessed for new needs. An execution agent and a coding agent share this memory through different interfaces: the former calls registered tools within a bounded online budget, while the latter uses a programmable workspace and a larger exploration budget to inspect evidence, write programs, and acquire models as needed. To realize capability expansion in ReSight, we develop Explore-Distill-Mount (EDM), which identifies capability gaps from execution and exploration records without task reference answers and converts exploration-derived solutions into reusable memory builders, query tools, and skills, extending memory representations and access capabilities as needed. Experiments on three public benchmarks demonstrate effective base memory access and capability expansion. With the execution model and online budget held fixed within each benchmark, EDM reduces mean position error on OC-NaVQA from 33.10 m to 24.72 m and improves high-level success on FindingDory from 38.3% to 63.8%. With the tools and skill frozen, success still improves by 28.3 percentage points on FindingDory episodes excluded from exploration and distillation. Experiments on real robot patrol memories further demonstrate reuse of these capabilities for subsequent queries and new memories, with fewer online tool calls on average.

</details>


### [16] [MimicAll: Structured Embodiment Conditioning for Cross-Humanoid Whole-Body Motion Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33508)
*Jia Li,Lingyun Xu,Yitang Li,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 MimicAll，一个支持跨异构人形机器人共享全身运动跟踪的强化学习框架，通过结构化具身描述符与物理一致的具身随机化，在 4096 种形态变体上训练，并在 464 种机体的基准上验证泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形运动跟踪器虽能复现多样行为，但要让同一策略在多种机体间共享，必须处理结构、形态和动力学上的差异。现有方法多为单机体专用策略，缺乏跨机体泛化与系统评测手段，因此需要构建可共享的全身运动跟踪框架及相应基准。

Method: 将结构化具身描述符编码为全局条件与关节对齐条件，并与观测历史和与机体匹配的运动参考结合，用于预测关节目标偏移；采用物理一致的具身随机化、机体校准的重定向和异构仿真，在 16 个人形家族、4,096 个变体上训练；构建覆盖五个分布层的 464 种机体基准，评估运动泛化、家族内具身变化和未见家族迁移，并做受控消融比较简单拼接与自注意力编码。

Result: 消融显示具身信息能提升运动完成度，简单拼接即可生效，而自注意力带来最低的全局位置误差；在相同训练转移量下，100STYLE 随机 G1 机体上共享训练 30 秒成功率达 99.4%，单机体专家仅为 42.9%；使用 Flat-MLP 编码与拼接、在剔除障碍类别的运动子集上继续训练后，采样训练机体与已见家族内分布外变体的成功率分别为 98.3% 与 93.5%；在 LAFAN1 八动作子集、464 种机体上，错配机体条件使自注意力 30 秒成功率从 61.7% 降至 47.0%，体现具身信息的闭环收益。

Conclusion: 具身信息对跨异构人形的共享全身运动跟踪至关重要，简单拼接已能带来收益，自注意力在全局位置精度上最优；共享训练显著优于单机体专家策略，且能泛化到家族内分布外变体和未见家族，验证了该框架与基准的有效性。

Abstract: Humanoid motion trackers reproduce diverse behaviors, but sharing a policy across bodies requires accounting for differences in structure, morphology and dynamics. We present MimicAll, a reinforcement-learning framework for shared whole-body motion tracking across heterogeneous humanoids. A structured embodiment descriptor is encoded into global and joint-aligned conditions, which are combined with observation history and body-matched motion references to predict joint-target offsets. Physics-consistent embodiment randomization, body-calibrated retargeting and heterogeneous simulation support training across 4,096 variants from 16 humanoid families. We introduce a benchmark of 464 bodies across five distribution strata to evaluate motion generalization, within-family embodiment shifts and unseen-family transfer. Controlled ablations show that embodiment information improves motion completion with simple concatenation, while self-attention yields the lowest global position errors. With equal training transitions, shared training achieves 99.4% 30-second success on randomized G1 bodies in 100STYLE, versus 42.9% for a single-body specialist. With Flat-MLP encoding and concatenation, continued training on a motion subset excluding obstacle categories achieves 98.3% and 93.5% 30-second success on 100STYLE for sampled training bodies and out-of-distribution variants within seen families, respectively. On an eight-motion LAFAN1 subset excluding ground and get-up sequences across 464 bodies, mismatched body conditions reduce self-attention's 30-second success from 61.7% to 47.0%, demonstrating a closed-loop benefit from embodiment information.

</details>


### [17] [MOON: Learning When to Forget and How to Move On in Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33546)
*Jingmeng Zhou,Seson Sun,Binyao Xu,Lingming Zhang,Hanshi Wang,Fudong Ge,Weiming Hu,Jin Gao,Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MOON框架，为冻结的VLN策略添加"遗忘时机"判断（deviation judge）与恢复策略（GRPO训练），通过清除视觉历史来缓解"行为惯性"，在R2R-CE/RxR-CE上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言导航（VLN）策略基于视觉历史决策，一旦出现错误动作便可能偏离指令路线。论文通过四项策略上的配对干预实验发现：偏离初期清空历史可使合并成功率翻倍，但在路线上清空反而有害；偏离后期的部分清帧收益很小。核心洞察是偏离后残留历史会阻碍策略在应转弯处转弯，即"行为惯性"，因此恢复的关键在于知道"何时遗忘"。

Method: MOON在冻结的VLN策略上外挂两个轻量模块（参数增量<1%）：1）deviation judge——判断智能体是否已偏离路线，触发告警并清空历史（决定何时遗忘）；2）recovery policy——以告警状态为起点用GRPO训练，学习偏离后如何继续前进，将原本只能靠采样才获得的成功转化为单次贪心 rollout 即可实现。

Result: 在R2R-CE与RxR-CE上，MOON在对比方法中取得最高的R2R-CE成功率，并使其冻结基座的成功率分别提升5.2和6.0个百分点；随机触发消融表明该增益依赖于"何时"清空历史。无需重训练，MOON还提升了HM3D-OVON上的零样本物体目标导航表现。

Conclusion: 偏离恢复取决于"知道何时遗忘"而非仅"如何行动"；通过轻量判官+恢复策略的即插即用设计，可在不重训基座的前提下有效提升导航鲁棒性，并具备跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent vision-and-language navigation (VLN) policies act on their visual history, and an incorrect action can take them off the instructed route. Paired interventions on four VLN policies show that clearing the history doubles pooled success at the onset of a deviation but lowers it on route, while later in a deviation, removing only the off-route frames helps much less. After a deviation, the retained history keeps the policy from turning where a turn is needed, which we call behavioral inertia. We propose MOON, a framework that equips a frozen VLN policy with two lightweight modules, adding less than 1% of its parameters. A deviation judge decides when to forget, raising an alarm that clears the history when it predicts that the agent is off route. A recovery policy, trained with GRPO from alarm states, learns how to move on, turning more of the successes found only by sampling into a single greedy rollout. Experiments on R2R-CE and RxR-CE show that MOON reaches the highest R2R-CE success rate among the compared methods and raises the success rate of its frozen base by 5.2 and 6.0 points, and an ablation with a random trigger indicates that this gain depends on when the history is cleared. Without retraining, MOON also improves zero-shot object-goal navigation on HM3D-OVON. Together, these results suggest that deviation recovery depends on knowing when to forget, not only how to act. Code is available at https://github.com/AutoLab-SAI-SJTU/MOON.

</details>


### [18] [WorldFailBench: Do World Models Understand Policy Failures?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33565)
*Zheng Zhang,Xiaoyang Tang,Shuaiqi Cheng,Jiakai Huang,Zhongbo Wu,Ziyi Zhang,Yuan Si,Zeyu Liu,Weibo Gao,Shunyu Liu,Shan You,Fei Wang,Xiaogang Wang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 WorldFailBench，用三类任务（失败原因、失败阶段、失败边界）检验世界模型是否忠实模拟策略的失败方式，而非仅判断成败；在 LIBERO、RoboTwin 仿真环境和 G1 人形机器人真实操作任务上评测七个世界模型，发现它们在理解策略失败上存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略评估对部署决策与策略改进至关重要，但真实测试与仿真环境搭建成本很高。世界模型通过生成环境中的闭环交互提供了一条补充路径，已有基准已验证其作为策略评估器的潜力，但多停留在基于结果的成功/失败判断。这类评估无法确认世界模型是否忠实模拟了策略"如何失败"。若失败被错误呈现，会导致对策略弱点的误诊，甚至在场景轻微变化即失效时仍让策略显得可以部署。因此需要回答：世界模型是否忠实模拟了策略如何失败、在哪个阶段失败，以及在何种条件下由成功转为失败。

Method: 提出 WorldFailBench，包含三个评估任务：失败原因测试模型是否复现策略产生的可观察错误；失败阶段评估失败是否集中在全任务执行的相同阶段；失败边界检验模型是否复现仿真中受控场景变化下策略由成功转为失败的条件。基准覆盖 LIBERO 与 RoboTwin 仿真环境中的操作任务，以及 G1 人形机器人在真实世界执行的操作任务。在七个世界模型上进行实验对比。

Result: 跨七个世界模型的实验显示，它们在理解策略失败方面存在显著差距，即难以忠实复现失败原因、失败阶段与失败边界；这些发现为开发更可靠的世界模型用于策略评估提供了指导。

Conclusion: 现有世界模型作为策略评估器仍不可靠：仅凭结果层面的成功/失败判断不足以支撑部署决策，需要从失败原因、失败阶段和失败边界等过程层面评估其忠实性。WorldFailBench 提供了此类评测框架，并指明了改进方向。

Abstract: Robot policy evaluation informs deployment decisions and policy improvement, but physical testing and simulator preparation require substantial effort. World models offer a complementary route through closed-loop interaction in generated environments. Recent benchmarks have demonstrated their potential as policy evaluators through outcome-based evaluation (i.e., assessing task success or failure). However, such evaluation does not establish whether world models faithfully simulate how policies fail. Misrepresenting failures can lead to incorrect diagnoses of policy weaknesses and make policies appear ready for deployment even when small scene changes cause them to fail. We therefore ask: do world models faithfully simulate how and at which stages policies fail, and under what conditions they transition from success to failure? To answer this question, we introduce WorldFailBench with three evaluation tasks. Failure reason tests whether models reproduce the observable errors policies make. Failure stage assesses whether failures concentrate at the same stages of full-task execution. Failure boundary examines whether models reproduce the conditions under which policies transition from success to failure under controlled scene changes in simulation. The benchmark covers manipulation tasks in the LIBERO and RoboTwin simulation environments and real-world manipulation tasks performed by the G1 humanoid robot. Experiments across seven world models reveal substantial gaps in their understanding of policy failures, providing guidance for developing more reliable world models for policy evaluation.

</details>


### [19] [Memory-Aware Keyframe Management for Dense Maps on Memory-Constrained Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33574)
*Huichang Yun,Jaeyeul Kim,Seungho Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种以语义空间单元（房间、走廊）为粒度、直接按驻留内存量约束的稠密3D地图管理方法，使语言模型与地图能在同一设备上共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在同一设备上同时运行语言模型和稠密3D地图时会共享内存，超限即触发OOM。现有地图系统的有界工作集按关键帧数量限定，但单帧内存占用差异大，数量无法控制实际占用；且以位姿半径、固定栅格或图邻居为准入单元会移动不需要的地图部分。

Method: 做出两个决策：（1）直接对驻留地图的内存做上界约束，每次准入的开销在读取数据之前即可由离线计算并随地图携带的单帧驻留大小得知；（2）以语义空间单元（建筑房间与走廊）为粒度进行关键帧准入与释放，机器人保留当前所在区域与即将进入的区域并释放已通过区域。每次准入先测量驻留总量、腾出空间后再读取，保证预算不被突破。

Result: 在两块NVIDIA Jetson板卡上，所有运行中预算均被守住，模型吞吐损失至多0.8%；若无此机制，操作系统会杀死模型或地图。在该预算下，按语义区域准入比等匹配区域数的几何划分多定位6–9个百分点的查询帧，约为RTAB-Map检索效果的两倍，且ATE更低。

Conclusion: 内存有界化使地图与语言模型得以共存，而以语义区域为单位移动地图则使有界地图真正可用。

Abstract: Running a language model and a dense 3D map on one device means the two share one memory, and exceeding its limit is an out-of-memory failure. Map systems that keep a bounded working set bound it by a count of keyframes, but the memory one keyframe occupies varies widely with what it carries, so a count does not control what the working set occupies. The unit of admission is as poorly matched, since a radius around the pose, a fixed cell, or the graph neighbors of the last matched keyframe all move parts of the map that are not needed. We therefore propose two decisions. First, the memory the resident map uses is bounded directly, and the cost of every admission is known before any data is read, from per-keyframe resident sizes computed offline and carried with the map. Second, keyframes are admitted and released at a semantic spatial unit, the rooms and corridors of the building, so that the robot holds the zone it occupies together with the zone it is about to enter while the zones it has passed are released. Every admission measures the resident total, makes room, and only then reads, so the budget is never overshot. On two NVIDIA Jetson boards the budget holds in every run and the model gives up at most 0.8 percent of its throughput, whereas without it the operating system kills the model or the map. Under that budget, and over the environments evaluated, admission by semantic zone localizes 6-9 points more of the query frames than a geometric partition of matched zone count and about twice as much as the retrieval of RTAB-Map, at a lower ATE. Bounding memory lets the map and the model coexist, and moving the map in semantic zones makes the bounded map usable.

</details>


### [20] [HA-DiPO: Diffusion Policy Optimization for Heterogeneous Cooperative Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33584)
*Xuebin Li,Jinpeng Xiao,Zong Chen,Pierluigi Arpenti,Bruno Siciliano,Jiebao Sun,Yiqun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层多智能体强化学习框架HA-DiPO，实现异构腿式机器人协同搬运未固定共享载荷，高层用集中式critic与扩散策略协调、低层用本体特定鲁棒运动策略跟踪，仿真与真机实验均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构腿式机器人协同搬运需要协调不同运动能力，同时承载未固定的共享载荷，团队级协调与本体特定运动控制耦合难度大；且希望执行时各机器人仅依赖局部观测、无需通信。

Method: HA-DiPO采用CTDE下的分层多智能体RL：高层由集中式critic基于team-value估计联合训练各机器人独立的协调策略，将本体相关动作响应对齐到共享搬运目标，并采用扩散策略以表达多模态协调响应，无需示教、纯奖励驱动从零学习；低层为各本体定制的鲁棒运动策略，跟踪高层协调指令。执行阶段各机器人仅用自身与载荷的局部观测生成指令，不使用伙伴状态或机器人间消息。

Result: 仿真对比中，HA-DiPO在最终平均回报、速度跟踪成功率上更高，速度误差更低；真机实验验证了四足-轮腿机器人及人-机器人协同搬运。

Conclusion: 分层解耦CTDE+扩散策略的HA-DiPO可在无示教、无通信条件下实现异构腿式机器人协同搬运，具备仿真与真机可行性。

Abstract: Cooperative transport with heterogeneous legged robots requires coordinating different motion capabilities while supporting an unfixed shared payload. We present HA-DiPO (Heterogeneous-Agent Diffusion Policy Optimization), a hierarchical multi-agent reinforcement learning framework under centralized training with decentralized execution (CTDE). The framework decouples team-level coordination from embodiment-specific motion control: at the high level, a centralized critic uses team-value estimates to jointly train independent coordination policies, aligning robot-specific action responses with a shared transport objective; at the low level, embodiment-specific robust motion policies track the coordination commands. The high-level diffusion policies offer more flexible action distributions than single-Gaussian parameterizations, allowing potentially multimodal coordination responses to be represented. The coordination policies learn from scratch through reward-driven simulator interaction without demonstrations. At execution, each robot generates coordination commands using only local robot and payload observations, without partner-state inputs or inter-robot messages. Simulation comparisons show that HA-DiPO achieves higher final mean returns and velocity-tracking success rates, as well as lower velocity errors, than the evaluated baselines. Real-world experiments further demonstrate quadrupedal-wheeled-legged and human-robot cooperative transport.

</details>


### [21] [HARP: Handoff-Aware Recovery Planning for Active Target Acquisition in Multi-UAV Handoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33640)
*Heegyeong Kim,Alice James,Avishkar Seth,Endrowednes Kuantama,Richard Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HARP，一种面向多无人机连续空中跟踪交接场景的"交接感知恢复规划器"，在接收机缺少目标先验观测时主动获取可用视角，同时不破坏发送机的持续覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续空中跟踪需要多架无人机长时间接力维持目标覆盖。交接时，接收无人机往往没有目标的历史观测，必须先主动获取可用视角；但若动作不当，会遮挡或干扰发送机正在进行的覆盖。因此需要一种既能完成视角恢复、又不打断现有跟踪的规划方法，并需区分"视野不足"与"物理遮挡"这两类成因，以便生成有针对性的恢复动作。

Method: HARP将发送机的有界预测登记到接收机局部地图中；区分视场失效与物理遮挡两类失败模式，并针对性地生成偏航、平移与视差视角候选动作。对每个候选运动进行多重可行性校验：接收机安全间距、与发送机基准的可观测性、受保护的"发送机—目标"视线可见性、时间有效性以及截止时间约束。当完整轨迹无法被接受时，可执行采样得到的可行非零前缀，并基于更新后的证据重新规划。

Result: 仿真中，HARP在全部49次标准试验中成功获取目标，在16次压力试验中成功15次；实地试验在视场受限与环境遮挡条件下验证了其恢复几何的有效性。

Conclusion: 以失败模式分类驱动、以多重安全与可观测性约束过滤候选动作、并支持前缀执行与在线重规划的恢复策略，能够在交接阶段可靠地恢复目标视角而不干扰发送机覆盖，具备仿真与实地双重验证的可行性。

Abstract: Continuous aerial tracking often requires multiple drones to maintain target coverage over long periods. During handoff, the receiving UAV may lack prior target observations and must actively acquire a usable view without disrupting the sender's ongoing coverage. This work introduces HARP, a handoff-aware recovery planner that registers bounded sender predictions in the receiver's local map, distinguishes field-of-view failure from physical occlusion, and generates failure-specific yaw, translation, and parallax viewpoints. Candidate motions are checked for receiver clearance, sender-fiducial observability, protected sender--target visibility, temporal validity, and deadline compliance. When a complete trajectory cannot be admitted, HARP may execute a sampled admissible nonzero prefix and replan from updated evidence. In simulation, HARP acquired the target in all 49 standard trials and in 15 of 16 stress trials. Field trials validated HARP's recovery geometry under field-of-view limitation and environmental occlusions.

</details>


### [22] [Energy-Based Fault Detection and Isolation Framework for Flat Port-Hamiltonian Mechanical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33679)
*Johnny Garcia,Nicolas Salguero,Naveed Iqbal,Brahim Brahmi,Ramy Rashad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于能量的SHAPE故障诊断框架，用机械端口功率残差（无需专用动态观测器）结合CWT与共享编码器CNN区分标称、执行器失效、异常摩擦与振动，在六自由度机械臂MPC仿真中平均准确率84.69%，低于广义动量观测器的97.17%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受调节运动下反馈会衰减跟踪偏差，导致故障信号不明显，需要一种无需专用动态观测器、也无需在线状态动力学计算或递归更新的高信息量诊断信号构造方式。

Method: 将端口哈密顿变量与微分平坦性结合，针对全驱动、共置驱动机械系统由规划运动生成标称功率参考；用指令力乘实测流量减去标称参考得到代数端口功率残差；再用连续小波变换尺度图与共享编码器卷积神经网络对完整轨迹分类。

Result: 在模型预测控制、存在固定对象-模型失配的六自由度Kinova机械臂仿真中（指定关节发生单故障、5个训练种子、3000次留出测试），SHAPE-CWT平均准确率84.69%（标准差0.70pp），力矩残差81.67%（0.70pp），广义动量观测器97.17%（0.37pp）。

Conclusion: 代数端口功率残差可实现有用的故障类型判别，但严重度-激励剖面与离线速度扰动的分析揭示其诊断能力存在边界，总体精度仍不及广义动量观测器。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis during regulated motion requires informative signals even when feedback attenuates tracking deviations. This paper develops an energy-based diagnostic framework that constructs mechanical port-power residuals without a dedicated dynamic observer. Spectral Hamiltonian Algebraic Power Evaluation (SHAPE) combines port-Hamiltonian variables and differential flatness to generate nominal power references from planned motion for fully actuated mechanical systems with collocated actuation. Subtracting these references from commanded effort multiplied by measured flow yields residuals whose final evaluation requires neither recursive observer updates nor measured-state dynamics calculations. Continuous wavelet transform (CWT) scalograms and a shared-encoder convolutional neural network are used to distinguish nominal operation, actuator loss of effectiveness, anomalous friction, and vibration from complete trajectories. Evaluation uses a simulated six degree of freedom Kinova manipulator under model predictive control and fixed plant-model mismatch, with single faults at a specified joint. Across five training seeds and 3000 held-out test runs, SHAPE-CWT achieves a mean accuracy of 84.69 percent, versus 81.67 percent for torque residuals and 97.17 percent for the generalized momentum observer under common CWT processing. The respective sample standard deviations are 0.70, 0.70, and 0.37 percentage points. These results demonstrate useful fault-type discrimination from algebraic power residuals, while severity-excitation profiles and offline velocity perturbations reveal limits to their diagnostic capability.

</details>


### [23] [EXE: Egocentric Cross-Embodiment Pretraining via Factorized Latent Actions for VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33685)
*Qingyuan Cai,Bo Zhang,Yongzhen Huang,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EXE，一种第一人称跨embodiment预训练框架，从人类操作视频中学习分解式潜在动作表示，分离共享操作先验与embodiment特有信号，实现向机器人策略的有效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型受限于机器人数据稀缺；第一人称人类操作视频丰富多样且蕴含操作意图，但人类与机器人存在 embodiment gap（具身差异），直接使用会引入人类特有运动学干扰。

Method: 从动作执行视角指出人类手与机器人共享到达、抓取、放置等操作基元但运动学不同，据此构建 EXE 框架：在人类视频上预训练，学习因子化（factorized）潜在动作表示，将可迁移的共享操作先验与 embodiment 相关的特有信号解耦，机器人策略仅利用共享动作空间进行学习。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0、RoboCasa GR1 以及真实世界操作任务上，EXE 相比无预训练基线取得一致性能提升；同一预训练可同时受益于平行夹爪与灵巧手等不同运动学结构。

Conclusion: 通过解耦共享操作语义与人类特有运动模式，第一人称人类视频可成为跨embodiment机器人策略学习的有效数据来源。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are bottlenecked by the scarcity of robot data. Egocentric human manipulation videos offer an abundant, diverse, and semantically-rich alternative that inherently captures manipulation intent. However, the embodiment gap makes it difficult to extract action-relevant representations from these videos. From the perspective of action execution, human hands and robots share analogous manipulation primitives---reaching, grasping, placing---yet exhibit distinct kinematics tied to their embodiments. This might motivate our key approach: egocentric video pretraining can yield shared action representations capturing transferable manipulation skills, while human-specific motion patterns should be disentangled to avoid interfering with robot policy learning. To this end, we propose EXE, an Egocentric Cross(X)-Embodiment pretraining framework that learns a factorized latent action representation from egocentric human videos, separating shared manipulation priors from embodiment-specific signals. By leveraging the shared action space, the robot policy acquires generalizable manipulation semantics from diverse human demonstrations without being biased by human-specific kinematics. This shared knowledge transfers across embodiments with different kinematic structures, enabling the same pretraining to benefit both parallel grippers and dexterous hands. EXE yields consistent gains over baseline without pretraining on RoboTwin 2.0, RoboCasa GR1, and real-world manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [24] [Open-World Law-to-Action: Vision-Language Priors and Touch for Parts with Hidden Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33705)
*Mohamed A. Mabrok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Open-World Law-to-Action (L2A)框架：VLM为未见场景中的可动部件提出运动模式与"运动定律"先验，机器人用带闭式证据的贝叶斯线性回归在线辨识部件物理规律，并在触摸不足时判断"再看一眼"是否值得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭机器人在从未见过的环境中需要开关门、抽屉、盖子与水龙头，而这些部件的物理特性（行程、阻力、锁定方式）无法仅从图像获得；"轻轻开一半"这类指令本质上依赖部件物理规律，因此机器人必须在接触中在线学习并保留可被触觉推翻的先验。

Method: VLM为每个可动部件提出候选运动、运动定律的项以及可能的锁定因素，形成带"地板"的先验以保证触觉总能将其推翻；机器人通过贝叶斯线性回归及闭式证据对单方向/双方向运动部件在线辨识其定律，并保留"存在未见的锁定部件或运动"这一假设；论文证明仅凭触觉无法排除该假设，推导了再次观察的收益条件，并指出单向运动时恒定负载与摩擦不可区分。全部实验在仿真中进行，在部分场景开发、仅在留出场景上运行一次。

Result: 新厨房中机器人基于VLM先验完成80%任务（部件全部已知时为98%）；提出物理项使需要新项物体的成功率从353提升到378–383/400；二次观察使混合场景成功率从284提升到329/366；不取用仿真器信息的移动机器人完成70/100个新场景请求，而由仿真器告知每个盒子对应部件时为62（两次留出测试间有一处改动）。

Conclusion: 将视觉语言先验与可被触觉推翻的在线贝叶斯物理辨识结合，能在开放世界家庭场景中显著提升操作成功率；但先验仍带来与完全已知情形间的差距，触觉辨识存在理论可识别性局限，需借助主动观察弥补。

Abstract: A robot in a home it has never seen must open doors, drawers, lids and taps whose physics a picture does not show. Instructions such as "open the drawer halfway, gently" depend on that physics: how far a part travels, how it resists, and what locks it. We present Open-World Law-to-Action (L2A). A vision-language model (VLM) proposes, for each movable part, its motions, the terms of its law of motion, and what may lock it. Each proposal becomes a prior with a floor, so that touch can always overturn it. The robot identifies each part's law online by Bayesian linear regression with closed-form evidence, for parts that move in one way or in two, and keeps a hypothesis that an unseen part or motion locks the target. We prove that touch alone cannot rule this hypothesis out, derive when another look is worth its cost, and show that a constant load and friction cannot be told apart while a part moves one way. All experiments are in simulation, developed on some scenes and run once on held-out ones. In new kitchens the robot completes 80% of tasks from the VLM's proposals, against 98% when every part is known. Proposed physics raises success on objects that need new terms from 353 to 378-383 of 400 episodes, and a second look raises success in mixed scenes from 284 to 329 of 366 tasks. A mobile robot that takes nothing from the simulator completes 70 of 100 requests in new scenes, against 62 when the simulator says which part each box is (after one change made between two held-out tests).

</details>


### [25] [Quantum Closed Loop Inverse Kinematics Through Hilbert-Space Encoding of Robot Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33715)
*Andrea Morghen,Pierluigi Arpenti,Roberto Schiattarella,Giovanni Acampora,Bruno Siciliano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种保结构（structure-preserving）的刚体变换与串联机械臂运动学量子表示，将经典运动学模型与参数化量子电路对应起来，并从量子观测量重建末端位姿与几何雅可比，进而提出混合量子闭环逆运动学方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算引入机器人学需要数学表示能够保持机器人运动的几何结构与位形依赖性；现有量子-机器人结合工作缺乏既能保持几何意义、又能兼容不同运动学约定与关节类型，并可支撑机器人层面算法的表示框架。

Method: 基于Bloch球表示，将空间方向编码为单量子比特态，用量子旋转复现参考系变换的有序复合；平移分量通过量子态聚合组合，其度量尺度以经典方式保留；从泡利期望值重建末端执行器位置与姿态，并保留原始关节变量以适配不同运动学约定与关节类型。进一步利用参数平移规则（parameter-shift rule）由量子可观测量导数重建机械臂几何雅可比，并在理想读出下证明其与经典雅可比解析等价；最后构建混合闭环方法——量子闭环逆运动学（Quantum Closed Loop Inverse Kinematics），利用量子导出的位姿与雅可比信息。

Result: 建立的表示可保持原关节变量与几何可解释性，兼容不同运动学约定与关节类型；可从泡利期望值重构末端位姿；几何雅可比可由量子可观测量导数重建，并在理想读出下与经典雅可比解析等价；提出的量子闭环逆运动学方法在混合闭环框架下可利用量子位姿与雅可比信息运行。

Conclusion: 该框架为机器人运动学的量子与混合计算方法提供了几何可解释的基础，可支持后续量子/混合算法在机器人运动学与运动规划中的开发与研究。

Abstract: The integration of quantum computing into robotics requires mathematical representations that preserve the geometric structure and configuration dependence of robot motion. This paper introduces a structure-preserving quantum representation of rigid-body transformations and serial-manipulator kinematics, establishing a correspondence between classical kinematic models and parameterized quantum circuits. Building on the Bloch-sphere representation, spatial directions are encoded into single-qubit states, while quantum rotations reproduce the ordered composition of reference-frame transformations. Translational contributions are combined through quantum-state aggregation, with their metric scales retained classically, enabling the reconstruction of end-effector position and orientation from Pauli expectation values. The formulation preserves the original joint variables and accommodates different kinematic conventions and joint types. Beyond forward kinematics, we establish that the manipulator geometric Jacobian can be reconstructed from quantum-observable derivatives using the parameter-shift rule, with analytical equivalence to its classical counterpart under ideal readout. To demonstrate the applicability of this representation to robot-level algorithms, we introduce Quantum Closed Loop Inverse Kinematics, a hybrid closed-loop method that exploits quantum-derived pose and Jacobian information. The proposed framework provides a geometrically interpretable foundation for developing and investigating quantum and hybrid computational methods for robotic kinematics and motion.

</details>


### [26] [RoboJev: An Open Vision-Language Judge for Robot Success Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33718)
*Josef Liyanjun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboJev-27B 是一个开源的机器人操作成功检测视觉语言模型，基于 JEV-27B-VL 用 rank-32 LoRA 训练，在 SO-101 同机器人测试集上达到 95.2% 平衡准确率与 0.993 AUROC，跨实验室/机器人 AUROC 为 0.890。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量机器人操作视频并未标注任务是否成功完成，缺乏自动、可复现的成功判定手段，限制了对操作数据的规模化评估与利用。

Method: 在 JEV-27B-VL 上仅对语言模型加 rank-32 LoRA 适配器（冻结视觉编码器），用 52,598 条 episode 记录训练；引入仅作用于研究数据来源的 source-specific KL 惩罚以锚定原模型预测；采用校准的双 token 读出方式，单次前向即输出成功/失败概率；输入为任务指令、10 帧操作画面及（若有）辅助相机末帧。

Result: 同机器人（SO-101）268 条留出 episode 上平衡准确率 95.2%、AUROC 0.993；未见实验室/机器人 459 条 episode 上 AUROC 0.890；社区标注数据 135 条上准确率 93.3%（基线模型在未见子集上 AUROC 为 0.900）。单张 H100 串行推理中位 0.26 秒/episode，约 $0.19/千次成功预测（不含媒体处理与服务开销）。同时发布推理代码、20,628 条预测（29 个模型/协议条目）用于可复现基准；并与强 API 前沿 VLM 对比，但后者仅用 4 帧主相机图像，非严格同条件对比。

Conclusion: RoboJev-27B 以较低成本实现了高精度的机器人操作成功检测，并具备一定的跨机器人/跨实验室泛化能力；其开源模型、推理代码与透明评测体系有助于推动成功检测的可复现基准研究，但跨域性能仍略低于同域水平，与前沿 API 模型的比较也需谨慎解读。

Abstract: Many robot manipulation videos do not indicate whether the task was successfully completed. RoboJev-27B is an open-weight vision-language model that takes a task instruction, 10 frames of a manipulation attempt, and (if available) final frames from auxiliary cameras and predicts whether the robot accomplished the task. RoboJev-27B is a variant of JEV-27B-VL, trained with a rank-32 LoRA adapter on the language model only (keeping the vision encoder frozen) using 52,598 episode records. It also uses a source-specific KL penalty that applies only to research data, anchoring predictions closer to the original model on those sources. It uses a calibrated two-token readout that outputs success/failure probabilities in a single forward pass. On 268 held-out episodes of the same robot model (SO-101), RoboJev-27B attains 95.2% balanced accuracy and 0.993 AUROC. It also generalises to episodes collected by unseen labs/robots (AUROC = 0.890 over 459 episodes) as well as to clearly labelled community-collected episodes (accuracy = 93.3% over 135 episodes), where the base model attains an AUROC = 0.900 on the unseen-lab/robot subset with the same inputs. It takes 0.26 seconds per episode (median, serial) to infer on a single H100, which is about \$0.19 per 1,000 success predictions at \$2.69 per GPU-hour (not including media processing and serving costs). RoboJev-27B is released together with its inference code, along with a transparent evaluation: 20,628 predictions organized into 29 model/protocol entries are made available to facilitate browsing and to enable reproducible benchmarking of success detection. We also compare against strong API-based frontier VLMs, which take in fewer input frames (4 images from the primary camera only); note that this comparison is not strictly like-for-like because RoboJev-27B is given more frames at inference.

</details>


### [27] [Floor Friction Assessment from Robot Tire-Surface Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33735)
*Kulbhushan Komarraju,Nathaniel Pearson,Yu Gu,Jason Gross*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究利用履带式/滑动转向机器人在地面行驶时"轮速里程计速度"与"惯性估计体速"之间的失配，从时域延迟与频域特征两方面刻画轮胎-地面相互作用，实现对五类地面的高精度识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮式移动机器人在GNSS拒止的室内环境（自动化制造、零售设施）中日益普及，地面牵引力直接影响打滑行为、航位推算精度与导航性能。作者希望挖掘机器人自身携带的测量信息，使其在完成定位的同时也能表征地面条件，从而改进定位并支持打滑感知的状态估计。

Method: 驱动一台滑动转向（skid-steer）机器人，在五种牵引特性不同的地面上，按能诱发打滑的预设速度剖面行驶（反复加速与方向反转）。计算基于惯性传感的体速与轮速里程计速度之差，提取三类指标：（1）积分相对速度误差；（2）由相位导出的"轮-体响应延迟"；（3）速度反转响应延迟。此外用Welch功率谱密度分析正、负加速度阶段的失配频率分布，提取带内积分谱功率、谱质心与谱衰减率，并用线性判别分析（LDA）与多分类进行区分。

Result: 响应延迟指标呈现一致的、依赖地面的变化趋势：肥皂地面上延迟最长，地毯上最短。谱特征在五类地面间实现清晰分离，在"按记录级交叉验证"下取得97.1%的平衡分类准确率。

Conclusion: 轮-体速度失配的幅值特性与频域特性都包含关于轮胎-地面相互作用的有用信息。这类信息可用于地面识别，并可为机器人状态估计框架中引入"打滑感知的轮速里程计加权"提供支撑。

Abstract: Floor traction influences wheel-slip behavior, dead-reckoning accuracy, and the navigation performance of wheeled mobile robots that rely on wheel odometry. As these robots become increasingly common in GNSS-denied indoor environments, including automated manufacturing and retail facilities, their onboard measurements could also be used to characterize floor conditions and improve localization. In this study, a skid-steer robot was driven over five floor surfaces with distinct traction characteristics using commanded velocity profiles designed to induce wheel slip through repeated acceleration and direction reversal. Differences between the body velocity estimated from inertial sensing and the velocity obtained from wheel odometry were evaluated using the integrated relative-velocity error, phase-derived wheel-to-body response delay, and velocity-reversal response delay. Welch power spectral density analysis was additionally applied to characterize the frequency distribution of the velocity mismatch during positive and negative acceleration. Band-integrated spectral power, spectral centroid, and spectral decay rate were extracted from each recording and evaluated using linear discriminant analysis and multiclass classification. The response-delay measurements produced consistent surface-dependent trends, with the longest delays observed on soap and the shortest delays observed on carpet. The spectral features also provided clear separation among the tested surfaces, achieving a balanced classification accuracy of 97.1% under recording-level cross-validation. These results demonstrate that both the magnitude and frequency domain characteristics of wheel-to-body velocity mismatch contain useful information about tire-surface interactions. Such information could support surface recognition and the development of slip-aware wheel-odometry weighting within robot state-estimation frameworks.

</details>


### [28] [POD-RoPE: Rotary Position Encoding over Physical Observation Distributions for Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33745)
*Xiaodi Huang,Zilin Wang,Jinpeng Liu,Pengchuan Xiao,Zhenlei Shao,Zhang Zhang,Shuxiang Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: POD-RoPE 把 token 的观测足迹表示为相机中心、视线方向与采样时间上的分布，用期望旋转编码分布间的相对关系，并在 Q/K 匹配与 V/O 聚合中引入观测几何。该方法在四类机器人策略架构、仿真与真机操作以及重建类任务上都取得了收益，最高提升真实机器人任务成功率 32.5%→52.5%，且计算开销可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身感知需要同时刻画物理几何与观测的时空范围，而传统的 pointwise RoPE 仅对单一位置编码，难以表达观测的空间和时间延展性。作者希望在不增加序列长度、不显式处理观测节点对的前提下，让注意力机制具备几何感知能力。

Method: 提出 POD-RoPE：将每个 token 的观测足迹建模为相机中心、视线方向与采样时间上的分布；在独立采样假设下，用期望旋转编码分布之间的相对关系，当分布退化为单点时退化为 pointwise RoPE。同一套旋转统计同时用于 Q/K 匹配和依赖相干性的 V/O 变换，实现"选择信息 + 聚合内容"两端几何引导。采用逐 token 预聚合，避免序列膨胀与成对计算。

Result: 在四个机器人策略架构、仿真与真实操作、以及重建/深度估计/新视角合成任务上评估。接入 Fast-WAM 后，RoboTwin 2.0 全部 50 个任务平均成功率由 91.83% 提升至 92.88%，Sharpa North 两项真机任务由 32.5% 提升至 52.5%。重建消融实验支持分布编码与 V/O 变换的贡献。H20 模型块基准中，前向+反向中位加速为 P-RoPE 的 1.91×、RayRoPE 的 5.34×，相对 Standard RoPE 中位延迟开销仅 17.4%。

Conclusion: 物理观测分布可以作为几何感知注意力的有效且计算高効的基础；POD-RoPE 在保持低延迟开销的同时，兼容现有 RoPE 形式，并在策略学习与几何重建任务上一致获益。

Abstract: Embodied perception requires positional representations that capture both physical geometry and the spatial and temporal extent of observations. We introduce POD-RoPE (Physical Observation Distribution RoPE), which represents token observation footprints as distributions over camera centers, viewing directions, and sampling times. Under independent sampling, expected rotations encode relative relationships between distributions; when each distribution collapses to a single address, the construction recovers pointwise RoPE. The same rotation statistics support Q/K matching and coherence-dependent V/O transformations, allowing observation geometry to guide both information selection and content aggregation. Per-token pre-aggregation avoids increasing sequence length or processing pairs of observation nodes. We evaluate POD-RoPE across four robot policy architectures, simulation and real-world manipulation, and complementary reconstruction, depth-estimation, and novel-view-synthesis tasks. Integrating POD-RoPE into Fast-WAM improves mean success from 91.83% to 92.88% across all 50 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks, and from 32.5% to 52.5% on two real-robot tasks on Sharpa North. Controlled reconstruction ablations support the contributions of distribution encoding and V/O transformations. In H20 model-block benchmarks, POD-RoPE achieves median forward-plus-backward speedups of $1.91\times$ over P-RoPE and $5.34\times$ over RayRoPE, with a median latency overhead of 17.4% over Standard RoPE. These results support physical observation distributions as an effective and computationally efficient basis for geometry-aware attention.

</details>


### [29] [RoboReason: Boosting Robotics-Relevant VLM Reasoning Through Scalable Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33778)
*Sagnik Anupam,Utkarsh Lal,Aneesh Edara,Botong Zhang,Yao Tang,Jiatao Gu,Dinesh Jayaraman,Osbert Bastani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出用真实机器人（立体相机+腕部相机）采集的图像上的空间/时序排序任务来评估VLM的视觉推理能力，发现当前SOTA VLM表现不佳，并提出可扩展的自主训练数据生成管线，通过监督微调与强化学习显著提升排序任务及下游机器人与视觉推理任务的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实机器人下游任务上评估VLM能力成本高、难以规模化；而排序类任务所考察的视觉推理能力与机器人操作直接相关，因此适合作为可规模化的评估代理。

Method: 设计三类排序评估任务：(1) 依据任务语言描述对打乱帧进行时序排序；(2) 按距相机远近对物体做深度排序；(3) 按像素坐标对物体做从左到右排序，并通过改变待排序集合大小调节难度。同时提出可扩展的自主训练数据生成管线，用监督微调和强化学习在该数据上训练模型。

Result: 多个先进的视觉语言模型在排序任务上表现吃力；在所生成数据上进行监督微调或强化学习后，模型在时空排序任务以及下游机器人和视觉推理任务上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 基于真实机器人相机图像的时空排序任务是评估VLM机器人相关视觉推理能力的有效且可规模化手段；自主生成数据配合SFT/RL训练可有效弥补VLM在此类能力上的不足。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated impressive performance when integrated into robot manipulation pipelines, but real-world evaluation of VLM capabilities on downstream robotics tasks is both difficult and expensive to scale. We propose that spatial and temporal ordering tasks using images from stereo and wrist cameras attached to real-world robots are particularly well-suited for evaluating visual reasoning capabilities, as they assess skills directly applicable to robots. We evaluate VLM reasoning abilities on three questions: ordering shuffled frames conditioned on a language description of the task, ordering objects depthwise based on distance from the camera, and ordering objects from left-to-right in pixel coordinates. The complexity of these tasks can also be modulated by changing the size of the set to sort. Our experiments show that several state-of-the-art vision-language models struggle on ordering tasks. To fix this, we propose a scalable autonomous training data generation pipeline, and show that both supervised finetuning and reinforcement-learning on the generated data can significantly improve VLM reasoning performance both on spatiotemporal ordering tasks as well as downstream robotics and visual reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [30] [One Scan is Enough: Demonstration-Free Adaptation for Language-Guided Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33794)
*Zhuoyuan Yu,Yuxing Long,Du Wang,Junyang Wang,Hongwei Fan,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Scan2Instruction 将单次 iPhone 扫描编译成完整训练环境，自动合成带指令的可执行导航片段；PRPT-KS 利用从重建场景直接解析的闭式 Pareto 效用进行无人类标注、无学习评论家的策略适配，实现免人类示范的语言引导导航场景适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开基准上的视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）在新建筑中会因布局、外观和语言漂移而逐场景失效，而在现场遥操作采集新轨迹来适配的成本无法规模化，因此亟需摆脱人类示范的场景适配方法。

Method: 方法包含两部分：1）Scan2Instruction，将一次简短的 iPhone 扫描编译为完整训练环境，并自动合成带落地指令的可执行导航片段；2）PRPT-KS，从重建场景直接读出闭式 Pareto 效用，对策略进行适配，不需要学习评论家（critic），也不需要人工标签。

Result: 在五个真实环境中，RealNav 达到 85% 的真实机器人成功率，而使用人类示范后训练的 RealNav 基线为 49%；在同一建筑的未扫描区域仍达到 82%，同一基线为 41%。

Conclusion: 该工作将语言引导导航的免示范场景适配从设想变为可落地的现实方案。

Abstract: Deploying a language-guided navigation policy in a new building usually means watching it fail. Public-benchmark VLAs degrade scene by scene as layout, appearance, and language drift, and the standard fix of teleoperating fresh trajectories on site does not scale. We replace human demonstration with a single brief iPhone scan. Scan2Instruction compiles that scan into a complete training environment, automatically synthesizing executable navigation episodes with grounded instructions. PRPT-KS then adapts the policy through a closed-form Pareto utility read directly off the reconstructed scene, with no learned critic and no human labels. Across five real environments, the two designs together drive our RealNav to an 85% real-robot success rate, compared with 49% for a RealNav baseline post-trained on human demonstrations, and still achieve 82% in unscanned regions of the same buildings, compared with 41% for the same baseline. Our work turns demonstration-free scene adaptation for language-guided navigation from an aspiration into a working reality.

</details>


### [31] [RoboWorM: Evaluating Movement Realism, Consistency and Acceleration Affects in Embodied Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33807)
*Doanh Le Thien,Viet-Thanh Nguyen,Pham Tri Quang,Nhi Pham,Christopher Wewer,Minh-Loi Nguyen,Hoang H. Tran,Tho Le,Tung M. Luu,Dung D. Le,Vu N. Duong,Jonas Fischer,Ngan Le,An Thai Le,Daniel Sonntag,Gim Hee Lee,James Zou,Mathias Niepert,Jan Peters,Duy M. H. Nguyen,Ngo Anh Vien*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboWorM——一个面向机器人操作场景、考虑部署需求的视频世界模型基准，利用机器人运动学树与固定相机等硬件先验，系统评估结构与时空调真度，并提出两个确定性、不依赖VLM的物理一致性指标 KIVA 与 RIGIS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型基准大多只把它们当作开放域视频生成器来评测，所用视觉语言模型缺乏严格的三维空间与运动学推理能力，无法反映机器人在真实部署中对物理一致性的需求。

Method: 构建包含1605个回合的数据集，覆盖单臂、双臂与类人形态，涵盖马尔可夫与长时程非马尔可夫任务及单/多视角设置；提出两个确定性、无需VLM的指标：KIVA用于视角内运动学稳定性评估，RIGIS用于多视角几何一致性评估；并据此对9种加速技术、3个主流世界模型（共超过29000条生成轨迹）进行大规模质量-效率权衡审计。

Result: 相对于最强的前沿VLM，KIVA将幻觉检测与人工标注的对齐度提升25.8%；RIGIS与人类偏好的Spearman相关性提高130%。质量-效率审计表明，加速方法可能保留表面视觉质量，却悄然破坏对部署至关重要的物理约束；使用该指标可实现最高2.3倍推理加速且质量损失极小。

Conclusion: RoboWorM 提供了部署导向的视频世界模型评测框架，通过硬件先验驱动的无VLM物理一致性指标，揭示了现有加速方案的质量假象，并为低延迟部署下的质量-效率权衡提供了可量化的指导。

Abstract: Video world models (VWMs) are rapidly emerging as scalable neural simulators for robotic manipulation. However, most benchmarks only evaluate them as open-world video generators, using Vision-Language Models (VLMs) that lack strict 3D spatial and kinematic reasoning. To bridge this gap, we introduce RoboWorM, a comprehensive, deployment-aware benchmark that leverages known hardware priors such as robot kinematic trees and fixed camera configurations, to systematically examine structural and spatiotemporal fidelity. RoboWorM curates 1,605 episodes across single-arm, bimanual, and humanoid morphologies, spanning both Markovian and long-horizon non-Markovian tasks under single- and multi-view setups. To explicitly measure physical consistency, we propose two deterministic, VLM-free metrics: KIVA for intra-view kinematic stability, and RIGIS for multi-view geometric consistency. By exploiting intrinsic robotic invariants, KIVA improves hallucination detection alignment with human annotations by 25.8% relative to the strongest frontier VLMs, while RIGIS achieves a 130% higher Spearman correlation with human preferences. Furthermore, since real-world deployment requires low inference latency, we utilize RoboWorM to conduct the first large-scale audit of the quality-efficiency trade-off. Evaluating 9 state-of-the-art acceleration techniques across 3 leading world models (over 29,000 generated rollouts), we demonstrate how these acceleration methods can retain superficial visual quality while silently compromising deployment-critical physical constraints. Using our metrics, we achieve 2.3$\times$ maximum inference speed-up with minimal quality loss.

</details>


### [32] [AdaDexTrack: Dynamic Modulation for Adaptive and Generalizable Dexterous Manipulation Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33852)
*Jianibieke Adalibieke,Qianwei Han,Xueyi Liu,Yuzhe Qin,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AdaDexTrack——一个"调制器在环"的语言条件灵巧操作跟踪框架,通过参考调制、物体潜变量调制和位置目标调制三项反馈修正,把开环固定参考跟踪改写为闭环调制跟踪,在未见轨迹/物体上超越现有SOTA并实现零样本 sim-to-real 迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用语言指令控制机器人是自然交互方式,但将单条指令转化为长时序、接触丰富的手-物交互仍很困难:合成参考含噪、人到机器人的重定向带来具身偏差,而固定参考跟踪会让小误差不断累积(雪球效应)。因此需要一种能在线纠偏的跟踪范式。

Method: 框架由两部分紧密耦合组成:(1) 作为"技能载体"的通用跟踪器,通过在语言条件手-物轨迹语料上做大规模"专家→通用"蒸馏学习得到;(2) 调制器在环执行三类反馈修正——参考调制(持续调整"跟踪什么")、物体潜变量调制(在线适配物体表示以调用合适技能)、位置目标调制(依赖状态的小幅执行精修)。调制器与跟踪器在同一任务目标下用强化学习训练,保证二者对齐。

Result: 大规模评测中,AdaDexTrack 在未见轨迹集与未见物体集上的平均跟踪误差与成功率均稳定优于此前SOTA,体现鲁棒性与泛化性;并在真实硬件上展示零样本 sim-to-real 迁移,加入调制器相比仅用跟踪器的变体有显著提升。

Conclusion: 论文将语言条件灵巧操作重构为"调制式跟踪",用闭环调制替代开环固定参考跟踪,同时调整参考、物体潜变量与位置目标,从而在含噪文本参考下实现抗漂移的执行。

Abstract: Language is a natural way to command robots, but converting a single instruction into a long-horizon, contact-rich hand-object interaction remains challenging: synthesized references are noisy, human-to-robot retargeting introduces embodiment bias, and fixed-reference tracking lets small errors snowball. We address this with AdaDexTrack, a modulator-in-the-loop framework for language-conditioned manipulation tracking. A distilled generalist tracker serves as the skill carrier, while a tightly aligned modulator performs three feedback corrections: reference modulation (continual adjustment of what to track), object-latent modulation (online adaptation of the object representation to recruit suitable skills), and positional-target modulation (small state-dependent refinements for execution). The tracker is learned via large-scale specialist to generalist distillation on a corpus of language-conditioned hand-object trajectories; the modulator is trained with RL under the same task objective, ensuring tight coupling. Across large-scale evaluations, AdaDexTrack consistently outperforms prior SOTA on unseen-trajectory and unseen-object sets in both average tracking error and success rate, demonstrating robustness and generalization. We further show zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on real hardware, where adding the modulator yields substantial gains over a tracker-only variant. AdaDexTrack reframes language-conditioned dexterous manipulation as modulated tracking, replacing the open-loop, fixed-reference tracking with in-loop modulation that adjusts the reference, object latent, and positional target, yielding drift-resistant execution from noisy text references.

</details>


### [33] [RoboLongR: Causally Consistent Rewards Teach Robots to Progressively Master Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33863)
*Zixuan Chen,Lin Cao,Fenggang Zhang,Haotong Zhang,Yangtao Chen,Jieqi Shi,Jing Huo,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出离线到在线的 RoboLongR 框架，学习依赖历史的奖励以引导策略适应长时序操作任务，核心目标是"因果一致性"；在 18 个未参与奖励预训练的长时序任务上平均成功率达 31.8%，超过最强基线 9.2 个百分点，真实机器人四任务上达 57.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序操作中动作的有效性依赖此前的交互历史，但仅凭视觉进展会掩盖缺失的任务前置条件，导致奖励与真实可执行进度不一致。

Method: （1）阶段打乱监督，使模型对执行顺序敏感；（2）循环记忆保留早期交互证据；（3）两轮训练借助预测进度细化轨迹片段；（4）由学到的奖励引导阶段条件策略从行为克隆逐步细化到在线强化学习；（5）利用稀疏终止反馈，在出现重复高分失败时触发纠正，把策略经验回灌到奖励学习。预训练使用 88 个 RLBench 任务示范与 Open-X 数据。

Result: 在 18 个未纳入奖励预训练的长时序任务上平均成功率 31.8%，较最强对比基线高 9.2 个百分点；4 个真实机器人任务成功率 57.5%，较对比方法高 32.5 个百分点。奖励层面的对照实验检验了对合成顺序扰动的敏感性，消融实验分析了各组件对策略提升的贡献。

Conclusion: 以因果一致性为目标的历史依赖奖励学习可有效缓解视觉进展掩盖前置条件缺失的问题，并显著提升长时序操作任务在仿真与真实场景中的成功率；顺序敏感监督、循环记忆与在线纠正机制均是性能提升的关键。

Abstract: Long-horizon manipulation requires actions whose validity depends on earlier interactions, yet visual progress can conceal missing task prerequisites. We propose RoboLongR, an offline-to-online framework that learns history-dependent rewards to guide policy adaptation to new tasks. Its central objective is causal consistency: aligning rewarded progress with prerequisites supported by execution history. Stage-shuffle supervision encourages execution-order sensitivity, recurrent memory preserves evidence of earlier interactions, and two-round training refines trajectory segments using predicted progress. The resulting rewards guide stage-conditioned policy refinement from behavior cloning to online reinforcement learning. Sparse terminal feedback connects policy experience to reward learning by triggering correction for repeated high-scoring failures. Pretrained on demonstrations from 88 RLBench tasks and Open-X data, RoboLongR achieves 31.8% average success on 18 long-horizon tasks excluded from reward pretraining, exceeding the strongest evaluated baseline by 9.2 percentage points.
