Across four real-robot tasks, it achieves 57.5% success, a 32.5-percentage-point improvement over the comparison method. Reward-level controls probe sensitivity to synthetic order perturbations, while ablations examine the contributions of individual components to policy improvement.

</details>


### [34] [Sybil-Resilient Multi-Drone Exploration of Indoor and Confined Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33906)
*Shyaam Mahesh,Aditya B Prakash,Rubal Sagwal,Avinash Gautam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究多无人机协同探索建筑时的Sybil攻击防御问题，提出五种无需安全基础设施、仅依赖无人机自身传感器与地图的防御手段，并在70张地图、2350次任务的仿真中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机团队协同探索时共享地图与前沿分配虽能避免重复劳动，但也意味着团队信任所有发消息的身份。一个被攻陷的无人机可伪造多个身份（Sybil攻击），抢占前沿并发送虚假扫描，导致真实无人机漏探索区域、地图中出现不存在的墙壁。现有方案往往依赖安全基础设施，作者希望仅靠无人机自身感知能力解决。

Method: 提出五种防御：(1) 将收到的地图数据与无人机自身观测进行审计比对；(2) 检查身份声称位置是否真实存在实体；(3) 仅信任已被目击过实体的身份；(4) 隔离无任何无人机观测到的身份；(5) 将每个被观测实体与唯一身份绑定。同时构造五类攻击者（含封堵楼层开口的攻击者），在7类共70张地图（办公室、隧道、三层建筑、洞穴等）上进行仿真测试。

Result: 无防御时，单个被攻陷无人机平均使最多25%地图未被探索（办公室达45%）；封楼层攻击者将三层建筑探索率降至82.3%，最差地图仅34.6%。隔离机制在所有场景中将探索率恢复到未受攻击水平，多数场景下虚假数据也恢复；例外为两种合谋攻击、假身份紧邻真实无人机、10个洞穴中的3个及16机团队，其中后三者可通过实体-身份绑定解决。2350次任务中，诚实无人机仅被队友撤销2次。

Conclusion: 仅依赖无人机自身传感器与地图的轻量级防御（尤以隔离机制为核心）可有效抵御Sybil攻击对协同探索的破坏，且误伤率极低；对合谋与近距离伪造等残余问题，实体-身份绑定提供了补充解决方案。

Abstract: When a team of drones explores a building together, each drone tells the others which frontier it is going to and shares the map it has built. This avoids duplicated effort, but it also means that the team trusts every identity from which it receives messages. In a Sybil attack, one compromised drone creates fake identities, and these fake teammates claim frontiers and send fabricated scans, so the real drones leave parts of the building unexplored and end up with walls in their maps that do not exist. We study five such attackers, including one that seals off the openings between floors, and five levels of defence that need no security infrastructure, only the drones' own sensors and maps: an audit of received map data against what a drone sees itself, a check that a body is present where an identity claims to be, trust only for identities whose body has been seen, a quarantine for identities that no drone has observed, and a rule that binds each observed body to one identity. Tests in simulation cover 70 maps of seven kinds, from offices and tunnels to three-storey buildings and caves. Without a defence, one compromised drone leaves on average up to 25% of a map unexplored (45% in offices), and the floor-sealing attacker cuts the explored part of a three-storey building to 82.3%, or 34.6% on the worst map. The quarantine brings exploration back to the level of an unattacked team in every setting, and in most settings it also brings the false data back to that level. The exceptions are two colluding attackers, fakes placed next to real drones, three of the ten caves and a team of 16 drones, and binding bodies to identities fixes the last three. Over 2,350 missions, an honest drone was revoked by a teammate only twice.

</details>


### [35] [Real-Time Human Trajectory Prediction from RGB-D for Mobile Robots Using Bi-Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33916)
*Basheer Al-Tawil,Magnus Jung,Ayoub Al-Hamadi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 TrajMamba，一种基于 RGB-D 相机的轻量级双向状态空间模型，用于短期人体轨迹预测，通过统一的训练—部署处理路径解决训练与部署输入分布不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在动态人群中导航需要预测行人短期轨迹，但实际部署存在"训练—部署流水线鸿沟"：训练数据使用理想化、平滑的真值，而推理时输入是姿态检测器和深度传感器带来的噪声估计，导致模型面对训练中从未见过的输入分布。此外，现有 RNN/Transformer 模型或难以捕捉短期时序依赖，或计算开销过大。

Method: 提出 TrajMamba：使用单一共享处理路径对齐人体姿态提取、深度反投影与平滑流程，使训练与部署一致；将短窗口的人体髋部中心速度序列编码为输入，采用堆叠的双向 Mamba 块进行编码，并用 GRU 头解码未来速度。

Result: 在 ETH/UCY 基准上平均 ADE 为 0.29 m、FDE 为 0.51 m；模型仅 262K 参数，可在普通硬件上实时运行，并部署于搭载 NVIDIA Jetson Orin 的 TIAGo 机器人。

Conclusion: TrajMamba 以极轻量的参数量实现了较强的短期轨迹预测精度与实时性，验证了统一训练—部署处理路径在真实机器人平台上的可行性。

Abstract: Predicting the short-term future trajectory of a person from RGB-D cameras is a key capability for mobile robots navigating in dynamic and human populated environment. The key challenge to deploying learned predictors on real robots is the train-deploy pipeline gap. Training data are often prepared with an idealized, smoothed ground truth, whereas at inference, the model is fed noisy estimates from pose detectors and depth sensors. The model is therefore evaluated on an input distribution it never encountered during training. Most existing models rely on recurrent neural networks (RNNs) or transformers, which either struggle to capture short-term temporal dependencies or suffer from computational cost. We present TrajMamba, a lightweight, bidirectional state space model for short-term human trajectory prediction using an RGB-D camera. The model uses a single shared processing path for both training and deployment, aligning human pose extraction, depth back projection, and smoothing processes. It encodes a short window of persons's hip center velocities with stacked bidirectional Mamba blocks and decodes future velocities with a GRU head. On the ETH/UCY benchmark, TrajMamba reaches an average ADE of 0.29 m FDE of 0.51 m. With 262K parameters, it runs in real time on modest hardware and is deployed on a TIAGo robot with an onboard NVIDIA Jetson Orin.

</details>


### [36] [Learning Functional Specialisation for Bimanual Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33947)
*Jingwen Li,Levin Kuhlmann,Jason Friedman,Gideon Kowadlo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文在含两个通信递归控制器的仿真肌肉骨骼系统中，研究"责任分配"应如何在模块化控制器与双臂之间组织，得出强专门化利于稳定角色下的性能与效率、分布式训练利于角色互换的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双手机器人控制需在运动噪声、能量代价与通信延迟下同时管理不同功能需求。作者关心学习责任（即控制分工）应如何在模块化控制器与两只手臂之间组织，以及控制目标与手—任务经验的交互如何塑造功能组织。

Method: 采用 2×2 设计，在仿真肌肉骨骼系统中比较：差分（differential）与共享（shared）控制器目标 × 固定（fixed）与平衡（balanced）手—任务分配，控制器为两个互相通信的递归控制器；以规范任务表现、控制效率、双手成功率及角色反转后的性能下降为指标。

Result: 任务对齐的功能专门化在"差分目标 + 与对侧控制通路一致的固定分配"下最强，该条件取得最高规范任务性能与控制效率，双手成功率达 81.33%。其相对共享目标的优势随运动噪声增大而扩大，但在更强能量惩罚下收窄。专门化增强降低了手部角色灵活性：反转角色后，Differential-Fixed 训练下双手成功率下降 80.46 个百分点，而 Shared-Balanced 仅下降 1.51 个百分点。

Conclusion: 控制器目标与手—任务经验共同决定功能组织。角色稳定时，更强的角色专属专门化有利于性能与效率；角色变化时，更分布式的训练有利于可互换性。

Abstract: Bimanual robotic control requires simultaneous management of distinct functional demands under motor noise, energetic costs, and communication delays. We investigate how learning responsibility should be organised across modular controllers and the two arms in a simulated musculoskeletal system with two communicating recurrent controllers. Using a 2 x 2 design, we compare differential versus shared controller objectives and fixed versus balanced hand-task assignments. Task-aligned functional specialisation was strongest when differential objectives were combined with a fixed assignment aligned with contralateral control routing. This condition achieved the highest canonical-task performance and control efficiency, reaching 81.33% bimanual success. Its advantage over shared objectives increased with motor noise but narrowed under stronger energy penalties. Stronger specialisation also reduced hand-role flexibility: reversing the hand roles decreased bimanual success by 80.46 percentage points under Differential-Fixed training, compared with 1.51 percentage points under Shared-Balanced training. These results show that controller objectives and hand-task experience jointly determine functional organisation. Stronger role-specific specialisation favours performance and efficiency when roles remain stable, whereas more distributed training favours interchangeability when roles change.

</details>


### [37] [I Won't Know Until I Try: Interactive Information Gathering for Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33959)
*Linfei Yuan,Rachel Holladay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个集成"任务导向交互式信息获取"与"规划-执行交替"的TAMP框架，在PDDLStream基础上乐观规划含交互动作的方案，执行中验证结果并局部重规划以继续任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作常依赖无法仅凭外观可靠推断的物体属性（如是否可移动、笔是否有墨水、抽屉能否打开、容器是否为空）。物理交互可揭示这些属性，但交互本身会改变世界状态，可能使后续动作失效，因此需要在规划与执行中权衡"感知信息"与"改变世界"的双重效应。

Method: 基于PDDLStream构建TAMP框架：1）将交互式信息获取动作显式建模进规划域，并以乐观方式进行规划（假定有利结果）；2）执行阶段验证交互动作的实际结果；3）根据验证结果自适应地保留仍然适用的动作，仅对继续执行所需的"连接部分"进行重规划（recovery replanning），实现规划与执行交替。

Result: 在仿真与真实机器人上针对四类任务进行评测：判断物体是否可移动、笔是否有墨水、抽屉能否打开、容器是否为空。实验表明，交互驱动的状态更新与恢复式重规划能够在结果不确定和外部扰动下维持任务执行的连续性。

Conclusion: 将交互式信息获取纳入TAMP的乐观规划+执行验证+局部重规划闭环，可有效应对不可从外观推断的物体属性及交互带来的状态变化，提升长时程操作在不确定性与扰动下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Long-horizon manipulation often depends on object properties that cannot be reliably inferred from appearance alone. Physical interaction can reveal these properties, but it can also change the world in ways that invalidate subsequent actions. We present a task and motion planning (TAMP) framework that integrates task-directed interactive information-gathering with interleaved planning and execution. Building on PDDLStream, our framework plans optimistically with these interactive actions, verifies their outcomes during execution, and adapts the remaining plan by retaining still-applicable actions and replanning only the connections needed to continue execution. We evaluate the framework in simulation and on a real robot across tasks that require determining whether an object is movable, a pen has ink, a drawer can be opened, or a container is empty. These experiments demonstrate how interaction-driven state updates and recovery replanning support continued execution under uncertain outcomes and external disturbances.

</details>


### [38] [ElevateHead: Decide Once, Then Commit: When the Action Head Matters for Multimodal Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34031)
*Tsung-Lung Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究策略的"动作头"（将状态映射为动作的模块）在多模态示范任务中的作用，发现在状态无提示的多方式任务中，采样式动作头比平均式动作头成功率高出0.72，但该趋势未能推广到其他任务；为此提出ElevateHead——在学习到的决策点处一次性确定方式并整回合承诺、且可被用户作为输入设定的动作头；经四轮冻结诊断迭代，第五版在留出Franka Kitchen任务上四个子任务全部完成率45.2%（BeT为29.3%，+0.160，95% CI [+0.129,+0.188]），强制模式时首个完成子任务与模式匹配率95%；同时定位了承诺失效场景（起始位置变化已修复，物体位置变化部分修复），块堆叠任务未超越BeT，真机测试为后续计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类示范学习的机器人常面临同一任务的多种合法执行方式（如绕障左/右、先开微波炉或先移水壶），而逐步重决策的策略会在方式之间摇摆。作者追问动作头在何时重要、以及应如何在这些方式间做选择。

Method: 在受控任务上对比采样单方式与平均多种方式的动作头；提出ElevateHead：在学习到的决策点处一次性决定、整回合承诺该选择，并把选择暴露为用户可设定的输入；采用"诊断式设计"——每版冻结后在未见任务上测试，下一版只修改被测量失败所指出的部分，且预测在每次运行前登记。共迭代五版，并进行强制模式实验、失效场景分析（起始位置、物体位置变化）及与BeT等强基线的对比。

Result: 受控任务上两方式时采样头成功率高出0.72，但该趋势在其他任务未复现；前四版至少在某一留出任务上低于BeT；第五版在留出Franka Kitchen上四子任务完成率45.2% vs BeT 29.3%（+0.160，95% CI [+0.129,+0.188]），强制模式时95%成功回合的首个子任务与模式一致；承诺失效：起始位置变化修复（承诺0.22→0.81，成功0.74→0.91），物体位置变化部分修复（探索性变体中承诺0.03→0.59，成功率无显著变化）；留出块堆叠任务第4版与BeT相当但未超越。

Conclusion: 多模态示范下的动作头设计值得重视，一次性决策+回合承诺+用户可指定的模式能显著提升长程多子任务表现；但收益依赖任务，物体位置泛化仍是未解问题，真机验证留待后续版本。

Abstract: Robots that learn from people often see several valid ways to do the same task: pass an obstacle on the left or on the right, open the microwave or move the kettle first. A policy that re-decides at every step can waver between these ways mid-task. We ask when the action head (the part of a policy that turns the state into actions) matters, and how it should make this choice. On a controlled task, heads that sample one way succeed far more often than a head that averages the ways when the demonstrations contain several ways and the state gives no hint which to take (+0.72 success with two ways); this trend was not reproduced on the other tasks we ran. We then present ElevateHead, a head that decides once at a learned decision point, commits to that choice for the episode, and exposes the choice as an input a user can set. We designed it by diagnosis: each version was frozen and tested on tasks it had not seen, and the next version changed only what a measured failure pointed to; predictions were registered before each run. The first four versions each fell below BeT on at least one held-out task. The fifth completed all four subtasks of the held-out Franka Kitchen task in 45.2% of episodes against 29.3% for BeT, the strongest baseline across our tasks (+0.160, 95% CI [+0.129, +0.188]); when a mode was forced, the first completed subtask matched it in 95% of successful episodes. Diagnosis also located where commitment breaks: varied start positions (repaired on the controlled task: commitment 0.22 to 0.81, success 0.74 to 0.91) and varied object placement (partly repaired: in an exploratory variant, commitment 0.03 to 0.59 on aligning with no detectable change in success). On held-out block stacking, version 4 was comparable to BeT and did not exceed it. A real-robot test with predictions fixed in advance is planned for a follow-up version of this paper.

</details>


### [39] [Enabling Motion-Induced WiFi-CSI Sensing in Multi-Robot Systems: A Simulation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34034)
*Brendan Dijkstra,Ninad Jadhav,Maurice Stoffijn,Stephanie Gil,Bahar Haghighat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个开源仿真框架，将 WSR WiFi-CSI 感知流程集成进 Gazebo 机器人仿真器，生成具有真实物理特性的合成 CSI 数据，支持仿真与真实数据对比处理，并用 TurtleBot3 实验验证了方位估计与机器人定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WiFi-CSI 可实现无线设备间空间关系的细粒度感知，WSR 工具箱已证明机器人运动中的 CSI 配合里程计可合成虚拟天线阵列实现运动诱导方位估计；但 WSR 与标准机器人工具集成有限、依赖已弃用的 WiFi-CSI 采集硬件，部署门槛高。

Method: 构建开源仿真框架，将 WSR 感知流水线嵌入 Gazebo，生成具备真实物理信号特性的合成 WiFi-CSI 测量，并结合仿真机器人运动（支撑运动诱导感知与下游定位）；框架同时支持模拟与真实 CSI 数据的处理以便直接对比验证。

Result: 基于 TurtleBot3 机器人开展仿真与真实世界对比实验，验证了框架有效性，并用于方位估计与机器人定位演示。

Conclusion: 该工作降低了多机器人系统中 WiFi-CSI 感知的入门门槛，使 WSR 式感知可作为模块化感知模态用于机器人研究，并为将 WiFi-CSI 与多模态感知结合用于定位与协同奠定基础；代码与实验细节已公开。

Abstract: WiFi Channel State Information (CSI) enables fine-grained sensing of spatial relationships between wireless devices. The WiFi Sensor for Robotics (WSR) toolbox demonstrated that WiFi-CSI measurements collected during robot motion, combined with odometry, can be used to synthesize virtual antenna arrays, enabling motion-induced bearing estimation in multi-robot experiments. However, deploying the WSR approach has remained challenging due to its limited integration with standard robotics tools and reliance on deprecated WiFi-CSI acquisition hardware. This work presents an open-source simulation framework that integrates the WSR sensing pipeline within the robotic simulator Gazebo. The framework generates synthetic WiFi-CSI measurements with realistic physical signal properties and leverages realistic robot motion required for motion-induced sensing and downstream localization. It further supports processing both simulated and real WiFi-CSI data for direct comparison and validation. Our aim is to make WSR-style sensing accessible as a modular sensing modality for robotic research. We validate our framework by comparing simulated and real-world experiments using TurtleBot3 robots, and demonstrate its use for bearing estimation and robot localization. This work lowers the barrier to entry for WiFi-CSI-based sensing in multi-robot systems and provides a foundation for integrating WiFi-CSI sensing with multi-modal perception for localization and coordination. All code and experimental details are publicly available at https://github.com/baharhaghighat/DARS_2026_Dijkstra_et_al

</details>


### [40] [Cut a Wall, See a Wall: Identity Substitution in Occluded Point-Cloud Perception for Construction Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34040)
*Tsung-Lung Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 点云分割网络在测试时遇到整区域遮挡（而非训练中的局部遮挡）时，不只是漏检被遮挡构件，而会把它们重标为单一"汇类"；加入14个手工几何特征（GPE）可将mIoU恢复到0.54–0.92，并几乎消除整物体丢失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 施工机器人只能看到建筑构件的部分区域，必须为这些元素打标签。训练时的遮挡是物体局部遮挡，而测试时往往整块场景区域被遮挡；作者要揭示这种分布偏移下分割网络的具体失效模式，并寻找有效的补救手段。

Method: 在两个数据集上做对照实验：固定与随机化的合成布局，训练3个随机种子，使用PointNet++和PTv3风格Transformer。对比三种设置：基线、每点附加14个手工计算几何特征（GPE）、以及遮挡数据增强；并在真实S3DIS扫描和基于预测的竣工柱检查任务上验证。通过特征置换实验分析各类别所依赖的线索（位置 vs 内在形状）。

Result: 基线失效严重：固定布局下柱子吸收高达70%的测试点；随机布局下汇类转移到楼梯，预测量为其真实占比的4.5–5.4倍；mIoU降至0.15–0.27（固定）和0.36–0.72（随机），单个测试集最多269个整物体消失。GPE恢复mIoU至0.54–0.92，减少64–100%的整物体丢失；在真实S3DIS上去除全部整墙丢失（10→0）；竣工柱检查中正确检查视角占比从17–58%提升到71–92%。特征置换显示：每个类别依赖其训练数据中最稳定的线索——层高带状板梁依赖位置，位置变化后墙和楼梯依赖内在形状。

Conclusion: 整区域遮挡会引发类别"汇类坍缩"，这是单独用mIoU难以暴露的失败模式；显式几何特征能稳定纠偏并显著减少整物体丢失。遮挡增强在mIoU上是强替代方案，但在所有设置下丢失的整物体都多于GPE（如25–35 vs. 0），因此在施工感知任务中GPE更具实用价值。

Abstract: Construction robots must label building elements they can only partly see. We show that when occlusion at test time removes whole regions of a scene, rather than parts of an object as in training, point-cloud segmentation networks do not simply miss the hidden elements: they relabel them, and the errors collapse onto a single sink class. With fixed synthetic layouts the column absorbs up to 70% of all test points; with randomized layouts the sink moves to the stair, predicted at 4.5-5.4x its true share across three training seeds. Baseline mIoU falls to 0.15-0.27 (fixed) and 0.36-0.72 (randomized), and up to 269 whole objects vanish in a single test set. Appending 14 hand-computed geometric features per point (GPE) restores mIoU to 0.54-0.92 and removes 64-100% of whole-object losses in every run, for PointNet++ and a PTv3-style transformer in both layout regimes; on real S3DIS scans it removes every whole-wall loss (10 to 0). In an as-built column check computed from the predictions, GPE raises the share of correctly checked views from 17-58% to 71-92%. Feature permutation on fixed, randomized and real scenes explains the pattern: each class relies on whichever cue is stable in its training data, position for height-banded slabs and beams, intrinsic shape for walls and stairs once their position varies. Occlusion augmentation is a strong alternative on mIoU, but in every setting it loses more whole objects than GPE (e.g., 25-35 vs. 0).

</details>


### [41] [T-ConSG: A LiDAR-Based Real-Time Temporal Construction Scene Graph for Dynamic and Evolving Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34041)
*Yilong Chen,Yong K. Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于LiDAR的实时时序施工场景图（T-ConSG），联合表示自由空间、静态物体、动态物体及环境事件，并用三个应用研究验证其统一时空表述能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 施工场景中移动智能体、临时障碍与长期结构变化并存，而动态/长期SLAM方法虽能检测运动与变化，但输出主要是度量表示，缺乏支撑高层时空推理的结构。

Method: 从LiDAR几何证据出发增量构建图结构，包含自由空间区域、静态物体、动态物体节点及空间关系边与事件参与边；持续维护自由空间与静态物体信息并跟踪动态物体，将累积空间证据的变化显式表示为安装/移除事件。

Result: 在三项互补应用中获得验证：砌砖试验台的动态感知导航、ConSLAM数据集上的施工进度监测、仿真施工环境中的在线变化检测。

Conclusion: T-ConSG为组织空间与时间信息提供了统一表示，可支撑机器人在同时存在短期动态与持久变化的环境中进行推理。

Abstract: Dynamic and continuously evolving environments present significant challenges for robotic mapping and scene understanding, particularly on construction sites where moving agents, temporary obstacles, and persistent structural changes coexist. While dynamic and long-term SLAM methods can detect motion and environmental changes, their outputs are predominantly metric representations that provide limited structure for higher-level spatial and temporal reasoning. This paper presents a LiDAR-based real-time temporal construction scene graph (T-ConSG) that jointly represents free-space regions, static objects, dynamic objects, and environmental events with spatial-relational and event-participation edges. The proposed framework incrementally constructs the graph from LiDAR-derived geometric evidence, maintaining persistent free-space and static-object information while tracking dynamic objects. Changes in accumulated spatial evidence are further represented as explicit installation and removal events, allowing the graph to capture both transient dynamics and persistent environmental evolution. We demonstrate the proposed representation through three complementary application studies: dynamics-aware navigation in a bricklaying testbed, construction progress monitoring on the ConSLAM dataset, and online change detection in a simulated construction environment. The results demonstrate that the proposed T-ConSG provides a unified representation for organizing spatial and temporal information and supports robotic reasoning across environments containing both short-term dynamics and persistent changes.

</details>


### [42] [Behavior Spatial Benchmark: Evaluating Spatial Understanding for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34051)
*Sirui Chen,Huang Huang,Qineng Wang,Wenlong Huang,Manling Li,Ruohan Zhang,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 BEHAVIOR-Spatial 基准，系统评测视觉语言模型（VLM）的空间理解能力及其与视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）机器人行为之间的关联，发现任务完成率不能反映空间指令遵循情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人任务需要把空间语言落地到物理场景中，但仅凭任务成功率无法判断机器人是否真正遵循了空间指令，现有评测缺乏对这一区别的刻画。

Method: 基于 BEHAVIOR-1K 构建基准，包含三个层级：49 个活动、3,256 道空间问答；14 个操作任务、1,240 个点定位查询；以及覆盖这 14 个操作任务和 10 个导航任务的动作评测。操作评测协议重点考查留出配置下的泛化，以及在固定训练布局中指令目标改变时的指令遵循能力；并对比 VLM 适配、机器人数据预训练与提供理想视觉目标提示等干预手段。

Result: 基线在留出配置上达到 41.6%，但在指令改变条件下仅 16.1%；VLM 适配与机器人数据预训练带来的提升有限，而理想视觉目标提示可将两项指标分别提升至 60.3% 和 38.0%。感知评测显示，VLM 问答与定位准确性和 VLA 操作表现之间的相关性依赖于具体策略。

Conclusion: 应在评估任务完成之外同时评估空间指令遵循能力，并进一步研究空间感知表现何时能够转化为实际的机器人行为。

Abstract: Robot tasks require grounding spatial language in physical scenes, but task success alone cannot establish whether spatial instructions are followed. We present BEHAVIOR-Spatial, a benchmark built on BEHAVIOR-1K that measures spatial question answering, point-based target grounding, and robot action to examine how VLM spatial understanding relates to VLA behavior. It comprises 3,256 spatial questions across 49 activities, 1,240 grounding queries across 14 manipulation tasks, and action evaluations covering those 14 tasks and 10 navigation tasks. Our manipulation protocol evaluates held-out configurations and instruction following when the requested target changes within a fixed training layout. The baseline achieves 41.6% on held-out configurations but only 16.1% under changed instructions. VLM adaptation and robot-data pretraining provide limited improvements, whereas oracle visual target cues raise these rates to 60.3% and 38.0%, respectively. Perception evaluations reveal policy-dependent correlations between VLM question-answering and grounding accuracy and VLA manipulation performance. Together, these findings motivate evaluating spatial instruction following alongside task completion and examining when spatial perception performance translates into robot behavior. Project website: https://arielchen07.github.io/behavior-spatial/.

</details>


### [43] [Extended LASMP: Language Aided Subset Sampling Based Motion Planner for Drone Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34054)
*Saswati Bhattacharjee,Anirban Sinha,Mukulika Ghosh,Chinwe Ekenna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"指令对齐采样分布"，将导航指令直接融入探索过程，以提升三维环境下四旋翼无人机基于采样的运动规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于采样的运动规划（如RRT）在复杂三维环境中采样效率低；同时现有语言辅助规划方法主要面向地面系统，尚未有效扩展到空中平台，且自然语言指令难以直接转化为可用的三维几何约束。

Method: 受 Language-Aided Subset Sampling Motion Planner 启发，将子集采样推广到"指令对齐区域"，实现结构化三维探索并保持概率完备性；利用基于提示词的大语言模型进行实体抽取与指令校验，将自然语言指令落地为偏航条件化、高度感知的几何约束，并据此构造采样分布。

Result: 在复杂环境中的大量仿真表明，该方法能够生成无碰撞且一致的轨迹，相比基于均匀采样的RRT类规划器显著降低了采样开销。

Conclusion: 指令对齐子集采样可有效扩展到空中机器人系统，在保证概率完备性的前提下提升四旋翼三维运动规划的采样效率，验证了语言指令与采样分布深度融合的可行性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a command-aligned sampling distribution that incorporates navigation commands directly into the exploration process to improve the efficiency of sampling-based motion planning for quadcopters in three-dimensional environments. Inspired by the Language-Aided Subset Sampling Motion Planner, this paper extends command-aware subset sampling to aerial systems. Subset sampling is generalized to command-aligned regions, enabling structured 3D exploration while preserving probabilistic completeness. Natural language instructions are grounded into yaw-conditioned and altitude-aware geometric constraints using a prompt-based large language model for entity extraction and instruction validation. Extensive simulations in complex environments demonstrate that the proposed method generates collision-free, consistent trajectories while significantly reducing sampling effort compared to uniform RRT-based planners.

</details>


### [44] [Lightweight Change Detection for Adaptive Collective Perception in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34059)
*Thiemen Siemensma,Tiago Travassos,Jiacheng Zhang,Bahar Haghighat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究动态环境下的群体机器人集体感知问题：在瓷砖比例和空间相关性随时间变化时，将轻量级变化检测机制嵌入贝叶斯集体决策框架，检测到分布漂移时重置机器人信念，并提出在线自适应采样间隔以缓解空间相关采样带来的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集体感知研究大多针对静态环境，而现实中瓷砖比例与地表图案的空间相关性会随时间变化，导致机器人在动态环境下决策失准；同时，已有较高竞争力的变化检测方法需要存储完整样本流，难以部署在内存受限的机器人上。

Method: 将轻量级变化检测器集成进贝叶斯集体决策框架，在检测到分布偏移时重置机器人信念；该检测器具有常数内存和常数单样本计算量，无需存储完整样本流。此外提出一种方法用于检测空间相关采样，并在线调整采样间隔以减轻相关性影响。在 Webots 仿真中验证。

Result: 仿真结果表明：变化检测延迟具有竞争力；在不同通信设置下精度更一致；在空间相关性变化时决策准确率有所提升。

Conclusion: 轻量级变化检测与在线自适应采样策略可在常数内存约束下有效支撑动态环境中的群体集体感知，兼顾检测响应、通信鲁棒性与决策准确性。

Abstract: Swarm robotics enables scalable inspection through collective perception, where robots infer environmental properties from distributed observations. A common benchmark considers robots exploring black-white tiled environments and determining the majority tile type. While most work studies static environments, dynamic environments remain underexplored. We study collective perception in dynamic environments where the tile proportion and the spatial correlation of surface patterns may change over time. To enable implementation on resource-constrained robots, we integrate a lightweight change detection mechanism into a Bayesian collective decision-making framework that resets robot beliefs when distribution shifts are detected. The proposed detector requires constant memory and constant per-sample computation, unlike two recent competitive approaches that store the full sample stream, making it suitable for memory-constrained robots. We further introduce a method that detects spatially correlated sampling and adapts sampling intervals online to mitigate correlation effects. Our simulation results in Webots show competitive change detection delays, more consistent precision across communication settings, and improved decision accuracy under changing spatial correlations.

</details>


### [45] [Learning Interaction-Aware Path Corrections from Unlabeled Trajectories: A LiDAR-Based Sim-to-Real GNN Framework for Construction Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34060)
*Yilong Chen,Yong K. Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种仅依赖LiDAR的实时交互感知导航框架，从无标注示范中学习路径修正，在砌砖试验与Dynablox序列上提升了路径平滑度与最小TTC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 施工机器人在工人附近作业，需要能预测人体运动、同时对动态非结构化场地保持鲁棒的导航。现有社会导航方法依赖手工交互规则、带标签示范或机器人专用控制策略，在施工环境中可扩展性与可迁移性受限。

Method: 构建实时LiDAR-only框架：用恒速外推生成残差学习目标，以GNN建模机器人、行人与目标上下文之间的关系；部署时用扩展卡尔曼滤波跟踪LiDAR检测构造动态图，并通过与规划器无关的接口用GNN对规划器生成的路径点进行偏移修正。

Result: 砌砖试验中路径不规则度降低39.2%且最小TTC提升；Dynablox序列上最小TTC提升38.3%，路径较基线更短更平滑。消融实验支持1.0秒预测区间，并验证了目标上下文、镜像增强与特征归一化的作用。

Conclusion: 该框架无需标注示范与机器人专属策略，即可实现可迁移的交互感知路径修正，适用于施工场景下的动态避让导航。

Abstract: Construction robots operating near workers require navigation that can anticipate human motion while remaining robust to dynamic and unstructured site conditions. However, existing social navigation methods depend on hand-crafted interaction rules, labeled demonstrations, or robot-specific control policies, limiting their scalability and transferability in construction environments. We propose a real-time, LiDAR-only framework that learns interaction-aware path corrections from unlabeled demonstrations. Constant-velocity extrapolation generates residual learning targets, while a GNN models relationships among the robot, pedestrians, and goal context. During deployment, an extended Kalman filter tracks LiDAR detections to construct dynamic graphs, and the GNN offsets planner-generated waypoints through a planner-agnostic interface. Within the bricklaying trial, the GNN reduced path irregularity by 39.2\% and increased minimum TTC. Across Dynablox sequences, it increased minimum TTC by 38.3\% while producing shorter, smoother paths than the baselines. Ablation studies supported a 1.0-s prediction interval and confirmed the benefits of target context, reflection augmentation, and feature normalization.

</details>


### [46] [DSGN: A Dual-Stream Gated Network for Scalable Simultaneous Surveillance and Containment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34084)
*Heng Zhang,Ziyang Zuo,Guoxiang Zhao,Xiaoqiang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双流门控网络DSGN，将"监视"与"围捕"解耦为专用表征流并用上下文感知门控协调，配以概率先验正则化，解决多机器人同时监视与围捕任务在规模扩大时的任务切换困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同时监视与围捕（SSC）任务中，监视要求空间分散以覆盖区域，围捕要求向目标聚拢以形成包围，两种目标相互冲突，导致启发式切换不稳定；且随着机器人网络规模增大，智能体-智能体、智能体-目标关系增多，单流端到端DRL策略把分散型与目标中心型线索混在同一特征空间，易相互纠缠、削弱任务转换能力。

Method: 提出双流门控网络（DSGN）：将监视与围捕分离到两个专门化的表征流中，通过上下文感知门控机制协调两流输出；并引入概率先验对门控过程进行正则化，以抑制模糊的中间决策。

Result: 在恒定密度跨尺度设置下，DSGN保持较强的任务有效性与可扩展性；消融实验与分阶段评估表明，动态门控机制与概率先验在纯监视场景和任务切换场景中均能实现有效的任务仲裁与可靠的模式切换。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 将冲突性任务解耦为独立表征流并以门控协调，是缓解大规模多机器人系统中任务转换难题的有效途径；概率先验正则化有助于获得更清晰的行为切换。

Abstract: The simultaneous surveillance and containment (SSC) problem arises in multi-robot applications such as perimeter security and environmental monitoring, where robots must survey a region of interest and coordinate to enclose targets once they are detected. Surveillance favors spatial dispersion for coverage, whereas containment requires target-centric cohesion for enclosure. This conflict makes heuristic switching unstable, and the instability becomes more pronounced as larger robotic networks introduce more inter-agent and agent-target relations. End-to-end DRL reduces reliance on hand-crafted switching, but conventional single-stream policies often mix surveillance and containment cues in one shared feature space that could entangle dispersion-oriented and target-centric signals and weaken task transitions. In this paper, we propose the Dual-Stream Gated Network (DSGN), an architecture designed to address scale-amplified task-transition difficulties by separating surveillance and containment into specialized representation streams and coordinating them through a context-aware gating mechanism. A probabilistic prior further regularizes the gating process, reducing ambiguous intermediate decisions and supporting clearer behavioral transitions under large-scale interaction complexity. Evaluations show that DSGN maintains strong task efficacy and scalability under constant-density cross-scale settings. Additional ablation and phase-specific evaluations show that the proposed dynamic gating mechanism and probabilistic prior support effective task arbitration and reliable mode switching in both pure surveillance and task-switching scenarios. The source code is available at https://github.com/zuozysama/DSGN.

</details>


### [47] [Doppler-aided Position-Velocity Tracking of Hidden Objects using mmWave Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34085)
*Yerin Cheon,Kush Garg,Akshat Dave*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出首个室内非视距（NLoS）毫米波雷达数据集，并将NLoS跟踪建模为位置-速度序列估计问题，实现8.95cm位置误差与1.75cm/s速度误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内机器人需要跟踪不可直接观测（被遮挡）的目标。光学NLoS方法依赖环境光照与场景几何，或多跳弱回波需长采集时间；毫米波雷达利用墙体反射且FMCW可快速测距、测角、测速，但雷达分辨率低导致观测空间模糊，而现有方法在学���前将多普勒维度压缩或丢弃，未充分利用对运动敏感的多普勒信息。

Method: 构建首个室内NLoS雷达数据集：连续RAD测量与同步位置、速度真值配对，覆盖576条序列、720,856帧、12条轨迹及不同速度、起始位置与材质配置。将NLoS跟踪表述为序列位置-速度估计：共享3-D编码器处理连续帧完整RAD表示，独立循环分支分别估计位置与速度，推理后精修融合为运动一致轨迹。

Result: 在留出轨迹上位置误差8.95cm、速度误差1.75cm/s，并可泛化到涉及运动人体目标的未见场景。

Conclusion: 数据集与跟踪器共同为低成本雷达跟踪隐藏运动目标建立了基准。

Abstract: Safe indoor operation requires autonomous tracking of objects that are not directly visible to the robot. Optical non-line-of-sight (NLoS) methods depend on ambient lighting and scene geometry or on weak multi-bounce returns that need long acquisitions. Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar offers an attractive alternative: walls act as reflectors for mmWave signals, and frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radars enable fast, practical measurement of range, angle, and velocity. However, the low resolution of these radars leads to spatially ambiguous observations. The Doppler information, although particularly sensitive to target motion, remains underexplored in indoor NLoS localization: existing methods emphasize spatial representations and either collapse or discard the Doppler dimension before learning. To leverage the complete Range-Azimuth-Doppler (RAD) information, we acquire the first indoor NLoS radar dataset that pairs sequential RAD measurements with synchronized position and velocity ground truth under controlled variations in motion: 576 sequences totaling 720,856 frames across 12 trajectories of a mobile robot moving along varying speeds, starting positions, and material configurations. We then formulate NLoS tracking as sequential position-velocity estimation rather than frame-wise localization: a shared 3-D encoder processes the full RAD representation of consecutive frames, separate recurrent branches estimate position and velocity, and post-inference refinement combines the two into a motion-consistent trajectory. Our method achieves 8.95cm position and 1.75cm/s velocity errors on held-out trajectories and generalizes to unseen scenarios involving a moving human target. Together, our dataset and tracker establish a benchmark for tracking hidden moving targets with a low-cost radar.

</details>


### [48] [X-World: Transferring Video Priors to Variable-View Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34105)
*Chun Gu,Xiaofei Wei,Yiming Chen,Juntong Zhang,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: X-World 将视频扩散模型的先验通过固定长度 token 序列迁移到共享动作专家，实现多视角、异构动作接口下的机器人策略学习，在 LIBERO 达 98.5%、真实双臂任务达 81.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型蕴含可用于机器人控制的交互表征，但迁移面临三大障碍：相机输入视角数量可变、动作接口异质（不同数据集的状态/动作空间不同）、以及闭环执行需求。

Method: 1) 残差跨视角注意力分支将预训练视频主干扩展到 1/2/3 视角，同时保留原始自注意力路径；2) 层级对齐的 Q-Former 聚合主干特征；3) 数据集专用模块在原生状态/动作接口与共享动作专家之间做映射；4) 训练分三阶段：视频适配、冻结视频主干的纯动作预训练、目标域联合后训练；5) 推理时单次主干前向提取视频特征，并采用异步动作分块执行。

Result: LIBERO 平均成功率 98.5%；LIBERO 训练权重零样本迁移到 LIBERO-Plus 得 76.5%。七类偏移分析显示在机器人、语言、光照、布局扰动上优于 OpenVLA-OFT，但在相机、背景、噪声偏移下较弱。六个双臂真实任务（每任务 20 次试验）平均成功率 81.7%。消融中移除视频适配或跨视角融合造成的性能下降最大。

Conclusion: 固定长度 token 接口 + 共享动作专家是迁移视频先验的有效范式，多视角融合与视频适配是性能关键；方法在仿真与真实场景均有效，但对相机/背景/噪声等视觉偏移的鲁棒性仍需改进。

Abstract: Video diffusion models provide a source of interaction representations for robot control, but transferring these representations requires reconciling variable camera inputs with heterogeneous action interfaces and closed-loop execution. We present X-World, a framework that transfers a video prior through a fixed-length token sequence to a shared action expert. A residual cross-view attention branch extends a pretrained video backbone to one, two, or three views while retaining its original self-attention path. A layer-aligned Q-Former aggregates backbone features, and dataset-specific modules map between native state and action interfaces and the shared action expert. Training separates video adaptation from action-only pretraining by freezing the video backbone in the latter stage, followed by joint target-domain post-training. The policy extracts video features with a single backbone pass and uses asynchronous action chunking for execution. On LIBERO, X-World reaches 98.5\% mean success; its LIBERO-trained checkpoint obtains 76.5\% on LIBERO-Plus without further adaptation. The seven-shift breakdown reveals gains in robot, language, lighting, and layout perturbations, alongside weaknesses under camera, background, and noise shifts relative to OpenVLA-OFT. On six dual-arm real-world tasks, it achieves 81.7\% mean success over 20 trials per task. Removing video adaptation or cross-view fusion produces the largest observed success drops among the tested removals.

</details>


### [49] [RoboDuo: Attempt, Check and Atomic Recovery through Code for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34108)
*Jie Wang,Bo Zhang,Renzhi Lu,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboDuo 闭环双系统框架，用代码作为编码智能体与两个 VLA 之间的接口：全任务 VLA 先尝试完成任务并检查是否完成，失败则调用原子技能 VLA 补完剩余步骤，无需更新原 VLA 权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型直接将指令与观测映射为动作，不检查指令是否已完成；长时程任务中单步失败会被忽略并导致整个任务失败，而失败片段往往已部分完成，剩余步骤是可复用的原子技能，因此失败尝试可通过原子技能组合恢复。

Method: RoboDuo 由编码智能体和两个 VLA 构成闭环：智能体编写程序，先让 full-task VLA 尝试整个任务并判定是否完成，失败时由 atomic VLA 完成剩余步骤；智能体不输出动作，只决定调用哪个 VLA，从而以无权重更新的方式包装已有 full-task VLA。为训练 atomic VLA 与具备完成度判断能力的编码智能体，基于同一套 LIBERO 演示构建 LIBERO-Atomic、LIBERO-Code 与 LIBERO-Judge 数据集。

Result: 在 LIBERO 与 LIBERO-PRO 上，RoboDuo 稳定提升 X-VLA 与 π_{0.5} 的表现，长时程任务增益最大；并在真实机器人上成功恢复冻结 full-task VLA 的失败 rollout。

Conclusion: 以代码为接口、双 VLA 分工（全任务尝试 + 原子技能补完）的闭环框架，可在不微调原模型的前提下有效缓解长时程 VLA 的失败累积问题，并具备真机可用性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models map a language instruction and the current observation directly to actions, without checking whether the instruction has been completed. For long-horizon tasks, a single failed step therefore goes unnoticed and leaves the whole task unfinished. Such failed episodes, however, often end with the task partially complete. Moreover, the unfinished steps are reusable atomic skills that recur across demonstrations. A failed attempt can thus be recovered by composing with atomic skills. We propose RoboDuo, a closed-loop dual-system framework that uses code as the interface between a coding agent and two VLAs. The agent writes a program that lets a full-task VLA attempt the whole task and checks whether it is complete; on failure, an atomic VLA finishes the remaining steps. Since the agent emits no actions and only decides which VLA for execution, RoboDuo wraps an existing full-task VLA without updating its weights. To train the atomic VLA and the coding agent with the completion-checking ability, we build LIBERO-Atomic, LIBERO-Code, and LIBERO-Judge from a single set of LIBERO demonstrations. RoboDuo consistently improves X-VLA and $π_{0.5}$ on LIBERO and LIBERO-PRO, with the largest gains on long-horizon tasks, and recovers failed rollouts of a frozen full-task VLA on a real robot.

</details>


### [50] [HumanoidNDM: Closing the Training-Deployment Gap of Motion Tracking via Humanoid Neural Dynamics Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34126)
*Chao Chen,Zhenrong Wang,Jilong Wang,Yusen Feng,Jiangran Lyu,Li Yi,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HumanoidNDM，一个由真实世界训练的神经动力学模型驱动的人形机器人运动跟踪在线微调框架。该框架包含三部分：低频参考扰动数据采集、基于本体感知与可微前向运动学的神经动力学模型训练、以及通过模型梯度反传直接微调策略。在Unitree G1上验证表明能快速适应多种扰动，是首个无需动捕的在线人形跟踪策略微调框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在运动跟踪上已取得显著进展，但真实部署中由于外部物理扰动或仿真建模不准确，会产生动力学差距，导致明显的训练-部署鸿沟。如何有效利用真实世界数据对策略进行微调，是实现鲁棒部署的关键"最后一公里"。

Method: 框架分三个阶段：(1) 数据采集阶段，提出带自适应强度机制的低频参考扰动方法，兼顾部署可行性与探索效率；(2) 模型训练阶段，在欧氏空间中训练基于历史信息、鲁棒的本体感知神经动力学模型，并引入可微前向运动学，从而摆脱对昂贵动捕系统的依赖；(3) 策略微调阶段，通过反向传播模型梯度直接微调策略，实现高数据效率。

Result: 在Unitree G1机器人上进行物理验证，HumanoidNDM能够针对多种扰动实现快速的真实世界自适应。据作者所述，这是首个在线、无需动捕（MoCap-free）的人形机器人跟踪策略微调框架。

Conclusion: 利用真实世界训练的神经动力学模型进行在线策略微调，可有效弥合仿真与现实的动力学差距，为人形机器人运动跟踪的鲁棒部署提供了可行且数据高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have recently achieved remarkable success in motion tracking. However, during real-world deployment, robots inevitably encounter dynamics gaps arising from either extra physical disturbances or inaccurate simulation modeling, resulting in a significant training-deployment gap. Therefore, effectively leveraging real-world data to fine-tune policies represents the crucial last mile for robust deployment. To bridge this gap, we propose HumanoidNDM, an online fine-tuning framework for humanoid motion tracking driven by a real-world-trained neural dynamics model. First, in the data collection stage, we introduce a low-frequency reference perturbation method with an adaptive intensity mechanism, balancing deployment feasibility and exploration efficiency. Second, for the model training stage, we train a robust history-based, proprioceptive neural dynamics model in Euclidean space by incorporating differentiable forward kinematics, eliminating the reliance on costly motion capture systems. Finally, in the policy fine-tuning stage, we directly fine-tune the policy via backpropagated model gradients, achieving high data efficiency. Through physical validation on the Unitree G1 robot, we demonstrate that HumanoidNDM enables rapid real-world adaptation to various perturbations, representing the first online, MoCap-free fine-tuning framework for humanoid tracking policies.

</details>


### [51] [Semantic-Coupled Perception Framework for Autonomous Laparoscope Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34128)
*Enduo Zhao,Fangzhou Li,Yujie Zhu,Sicheng Lu,Fang Chen,Longfei Ma,Hongen Liao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将手术阶段识别与相机运动补偿视觉感知相耦合的闭环腹腔镜自主控制框架，以提升遮挡、实体重叠与相机运动下的视野控制鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微创手术中维持合适视野对安全与效率至关重要，但现有自主腹腔镜控制方法将视觉跟踪与手术阶段识别彼此独立处理，在遮挡、实体重叠和相机运动条件下鲁棒性受限。

Method: 构建闭环自主腹腔镜控制框架：实时手术阶段识别与相机运动补偿的视觉感知相耦合，利用手术实体间的阶段依赖关系改进困难视觉条件下的目标状态估计，从而支持任务感知的自主视野控制。

Result: 在基准数据集上评估了手术阶段识别与目标跟踪（含遮挡和实体重叠场景）；体模实验验证了自主视野控制的可行性，相比固定视野控制提升了目标可见性与任务执行表现。

Conclusion: 将任务级语义引入视觉状态估计对自主腹腔镜引导具有潜力。

Abstract: In minimally invasive surgery (MIS), maintaining an appropriate field of view (FoV) is essential for surgical safety and efficiency. However, conventional autonomous laparoscope control methods often treat visual tracking and surgical phase recognition independently, limiting robustness under occlusion, entity overlap, and camera motion. This paper proposes a closed-loop autonomous laparoscope control framework that couples real-time surgical phase recognition with camera-motion-compensated visual perception. By exploiting phase-dependent relationships among surgical entities, the framework improves target state estimation under challenging visual conditions, supporting task-aware autonomous FoV control. Experiments on benchmark datasets evaluate the proposed framework in surgical phase recognition and target tracking, including scenarios involving occlusion and entity overlap. Phantom experiments further demonstrate the feasibility of autonomous FoV control, showing improved target visibility and task execution performance compared with fixed-FoV control. These results highlight the potential of incorporating task-level semantics into visual state estimation for autonomous laparoscope guidance.

</details>


### [52] [PS-DLO: Property- and Spatially Aware Deformable Linear Object Manipulation Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34147)
*Mengdi Li,Boxian Yao,Xiyu Zhu,Yifan Yang,Yunfan Lou,Xuan Han,Tao Liu,Zhibo Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 PS-DLO，一种融合物体物理属性先验与空间感知的可变形线状物体（DLO）操作策略，在九个真实任务上平均成功率 68.9%，超过最强基线 20.0 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLO 操作面临感知与控制耦合的难题：细长几何形状导致精确定位困难，而其形变又依赖物体自身的物理属性。现有方法缺乏对物体属性和任务相关区域未来构型的显式建模。

Method: 方法包含两部分：其一，通过对比式图文学习将初始观测映射为 DLO 属性的类别化描述，为动作生成提供显式的物理与结构先验；其二，用 SAM3 从未来观测中提取掩码引导特征，并将其蒸馏进策略的动作专家模块，以编码任务相关的 DLO 区域及其后续构型。

Result: 在单臂与双臂平台上的九个真实任务中评测，每种本体分别训练一个共享多任务策略。PS-DLO 平均成功率 68.9%，比最强基线高 20.0 个百分点；在蝴蝶结解结任务的七种视觉偏移条件中，有六种优于基线。

Conclusion: 将物体属性先验与未来空间监督相结合，可提升多任务 DLO 操作性能及对视觉变化的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deformable linear objects (DLO) pose a coupled perception and control challenge: their slender geometry makes precise localization difficult, while their deformations depend on object-specific physical properties. To address this challenge, we propose PS-DLO, a property- and spatially aware DLO manipulation policy. First, contrastive image-text learning maps the initial observation to categorical descriptions of DLO properties, providing explicit physical and structural priors for action generation. Second, SAM3 extracts mask-guided features from future observations, which are distilled into the policy's action expert to encode task-relevant DLO regions and their subsequent configurations. We evaluate PS-DLO on nine real-world tasks across single-arm and bimanual platforms, training a shared multi-task policy for each embodiment. PS-DLO achieves an average success rate of 68.9%, exceeding the strongest baseline by 20.0 percentage points. In generalization experiments, PS-DLO achieves higher success rates than the baseline in six of seven visual-shift conditions on butterfly-knot untying. These results demonstrate that combining object-property priors with future spatial supervision improves multi-task DLO manipulation and robustness to visual variation.

</details>


### [53] [HuGe: Scaling Humanoid Generalist Vision-Language-Action Models with Egocentric Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34186)
*Yang Liu,Jiacheng Liu,Hengtao Li,Yuheng Huang,Zijian Yang,Ji Dai,Maoxin Wang,Yi Gao,Qishen Wang,Baohua Yin,Peiyang Li,Shuanghao Bai,Mingyang Sun,Pengxiang Ding,Jianqin Yin,Lixin Yang,Donglin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HuGe 是一系列人形通用视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA），在超过 2000 小时的第一人称人类数据与机器人数据上预训练，通过预测潜空间运动 token 与手部控制信号驱动预训练全身控制器，并在 HumanoidArena 仿真基准上取得最优表现，在 Unitree G1 实机上显著提升抓取放置任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 VLA 扩展到人形机器人需要协调的全身控制与多样化示范，但真实场景采集成本高昂。第一人称人类示范是任务经验的补充来源，然而视觉具身差异（外观不同）与缺失机器人本体感知（proprioception）导致难以迁移到人形策略。

Method: 采用三种已有代表性 VLA 架构，输出供预训练全身控制器使用的潜空间运动 token 以及手部控制信号；设计三阶段训练流程：第一人称人类数据预训练、人形适配、下游微调；预训练阶段通过仿真回放构造伪本体感知状态，微调阶段通过重渲染修改第一人称观测中的手臂外观，以缓解跨具身差距。

Result: 在仿真基准 HumanoidArena 上达到 state-of-the-art；在 Unitree G1 实机实验中，将视觉适配后的第一人称人类示范与机器人示范结合，显著提升 pick-and-drop 任务成功率。

Conclusion: 第一人称人类数据可有效补充人形机器人示范数据，配合伪本体感知构造与视觉外观适配，可在仿真与真实人形平台上实现可用的通用全身 VLA 策略。

Abstract: Extending Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) to humanoid robots requires coordinated whole-body control and diverse demonstrations that are costly to collect in real-world scenarios. Although egocentric human demonstrations provide a complementary source of task experience, differences in visual embodiment and missing robot proprioception complicate transfer to humanoid policies. To address these challenges, we introduce HuGe, a family of Humanoid Generalist VLAs pre-trained on over 2,000 hours of egocentric human and robot data. Specifically, HuGe employs three existing representative VLA architectures to predict latent motion tokens for a pretrained whole-body controller, together with hand-control signals. Based on this, we introduce a three-stage training pipeline that combines egocentric pre-training, humanoid adaptation, and downstream fine-tuning. To facilitate transfer across embodiments, we construct pseudo-proprioceptive states through simulation playback during pre-training and modify arm appearance in egocentric observations through re-rendering during downstream fine-tuning. With this pipeline, HuGe achieves the state-of-the-art on simulation benchmark HumanoidArena. Furthermore, real-world experiments on the Unitree G1 indicate that augmenting robot demonstrations with visually adapted egocentric demonstrations significantly increases success on a pick-and-drop task.

</details>


### [54] [AdaPolicy: Action-Driven Anticipatory Robot Policies via Next-Chunk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34280)
*Fan Yang,Yuting Su,Chen Zhao,Fugui Fan,Peiguang Jing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AdaPolicy，一种由动作驱动的预期式策略框架，通过级联的下一动作块预测来塑造未来感知表征，无需未来观测监督，且无推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有赋予机器人策略预期能力的方法主要依赖未来观测（像素或隐空间）的显式监督，带来额外的架构与计算复杂度。作者希望探索能否直接从动作信号中诱导出这种前瞻能力。

Method: 采用级联的 next-chunk 预测：Online Action Head 同时输出当前动作块和演化后的隐状态；基于该状态，级联的 Unrolled Action Head 预测下一动作块，其损失经演化状态回传以向在线策略注入隐式前瞻。该展开仅在训练时使用，无需编码未来观测。

Result: 在 MetaWorld、RoboTwin 2.0、LIBERO 及真实世界部署上均取得一致性提升，在耳机装配、穿鞋带等毫米级精度任务上提升最多达 14 个百分点。

Conclusion: AdaPolicy 证明了预期能力可直接从动作信号中诱导，无需未来观测监督，保持轻量训练且推理零额外开销，并在多场景下有效。

Abstract: Endowing robot policies with anticipatory capabilities has proven effective in improving control performance. However, existing methods predominantly rely on explicit supervision derived from future observations, in either pixel or latent space, at the cost of additional architectural and computational complexity. Departing from explicit future supervision, we ask whether such foresight can be elicited directly from the action signal. To this end, we propose \textbf{AdaPolicy}, an \textbf{A}ction-\textbf{d}riven \textbf{a}nticipatory policy framework that shapes future-aware representations via cascaded next-chunk prediction. Specifically, an Online Action Head jointly produces the current action chunk and an evolved latent state. Building on this state, a cascaded Unrolled Action Head forecasts the next chunk, and the resulting loss flows back through the evolved state to instill implicit foresight into the online policy. Importantly, this training-only unroll requires no future-observation encoding, keeping AdaPolicy lightweight to train with zero inference-time overhead. Experiments on MetaWorld, RoboTwin 2.0, LIBERO, and real-world deployments demonstrate consistent gains across diverse settings, including up to $14$ percentage points on millimeter-precision tasks such as earphone assembly and shoelace threading. More details and visualizations are available at \href{https://teee111.github.io/AdaPolicy/}{https://teee111.github.io/AdaPolicy/}.

</details>


### [55] [ScheNav: Schematic-Guided Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34286)
*Guichen Huang,Chenchen Jing,Che Sun,Yuwei Wu,Yunde Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出由数据生成流水线与闭环导航框架 ScheNav 组成的示意地图引导具身导航方案，将粗略草图/方向标注转化为探索与停止决策，并在未见环境中提升导航成功率与路径效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物体目标导航要求智能体在复杂环境中定位并抵达目标物体，而人类常借助低成本但信息量大的示意地图（如粗略草图、方向标注）完成此类任务。现有方法缺乏对这类粗略示意引导的系统化利用，也缺少配套的训练数据与可迁移的闭环框架。

Method: 工作流包含两部分：(1) 轻量级数据生成流水线，将 2D 平面图转化为导航训练环境，构建 Furnished-RPLAN 数据集——超过 33k 套带家具布局，配有配对的导航回合与噪声可控的示意地图；(2) ScheNav 闭环导航框架，借助可学习空间模块 SchemaField 将粗略示意引导与累积观测融合，预测空间价值场，并随探索过程持续更新这些场；训练采用基于轨迹的方式，并引入跨帧一致性目标以保证变化视角下的引导连贯性。

Result: 实验表明，在示意地图、目标椭圆和指向方向等多种引导形式下，导航成功率与路径效率均有提升，且对所测试的示意扰动与缺失具有一定容忍性；策略完全在模拟平面图中学习，可迁移到未见的具身环境中。

Conclusion: 粗略示意引导是提升具身导航性能的实用途径，配合可扩展的仿真数据生成与闭环空间价值场框架，可在真实未知场景中有效发挥作用。

Abstract: Object goal navigation requires an embodied agent to locate and reach target objects in complex environments. Humans often rely on low-cost yet informative schematic maps, such as rough sketches or directional annotations. We develop a workflow for schematic-guided embodied navigation with two components: a training-data generation pipeline and ScheNav, a closed-loop navigation framework. Our lightweight data-generation pipeline turns 2D floorplans into navigation training environments, yielding Furnished-RPLAN, a dataset of over 33k furnished layouts with paired navigation episodes and noise-controllable schematic maps. ScheNav turns rough schematic guidance into exploration and stopping decisions by using SchemaField, a learned spatial module that fuses the guidance with accumulated observations and predicts spatial value fields. The framework updates these fields as exploration proceeds, with trajectory-based training and a cross-frame consistency objective supporting coherent guidance across changing viewpoints. ScheNav learns to use schematic guidance entirely from simulated floorplans and transfers this ability to unseen embodied environments. Experiments show improved navigation success and path efficiency across schematic maps, goal ellipses, and pointed directions, together with tolerance to the tested schematic perturbations and omissions. Together, these results demonstrate that rough schematic guidance offers a practical way to improve embodied navigation.

</details>


### [56] [RynnVLA-Latent: Scaling Robotic Manipulation Foundation Models with 3D-Grounded Latent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34356)
*Haoyu Zhao,Xingyue Zhao,Zhian Su,Bohan Hou,Yinzhou Tang,Fengyi Wang,Kehan Li,Yuming Jiang,Siteng Huang,Xin Li,Zhenyu Zeng,Mingxiu Chen,Deli Zhao,Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RynnVLA-Latent 通过从异构交互视频中学习连续、3D 接地的潜在动作（RynnLAM），再用流匹配训练可迁移到具体机器人本体的 VLA 策略，实现超越原生动作标注数据的机器人策略规模化预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型规模化受限于两点：带动作标注的示教数据稀缺，以及不同机器人本体控制空间互不兼容，难以直接复用跨本体的交互视频数据。

Method: 提出 RynnLAM：无原生机器人控制信号，仅从视觉转换中学习连续、3D 接地的潜在动作，结合残差未来特征预测、相机补偿 3D 光流监督与相机运动解耦，使表征聚焦可控运动而非外观变化；在 30.1K 小时人类/机器人/仿真视频上生成 745.52M 潜在动作描述子；RynnVLA-Latent 用流匹配从视觉观测与语言预测潜在动作块，再将预训练骨干与动作专家适配到本体特定动作。

Result: 4B 策略在 LIBERO 达 98.8% 成功率，LIBERO-Plus 无基准特定适配下达 84.7%，VLABench 五赛道平均 51.9%，RoboTwin 2.0 双臂基准平均 88.6%；在灵巧手与移动夹爪平台的六项真实操作任务中，所比较方法里成功率最高。表征消融与数据规模实验进一步验证有效性。

Conclusion: 潜在动作监督可将机器人策略学习扩展到原生动作标注数据之外，是缓解标注稀缺与跨本体控制空间不兼容的可行路径。

Abstract: Scaling vision-language-action (VLA) models is constrained by scarce action-labeled demonstrations and incompatible control spaces across robot embodiments. We present RynnVLA-Latent, a framework that turns heterogeneous interaction videos into scalable supervision for robot policy pretraining. At its core, RynnLAM learns continuous, 3D-grounded latent actions from visual transitions without requiring native robot controls. By combining residual future-feature prediction, camera-compensated 3D-flow supervision, and camera-motion decoupling, RynnLAM encourages representations that capture control-relevant motion rather than appearance changes alone. We apply RynnLAM to 30.1K hours of human, robot, and simulation videos, producing 745.52M latent-action descriptors. RynnVLA-Latent then learns to predict latent-action chunks from visual observations and language through flow matching, before adapting its pretrained backbone and action expert to embodiment-specific robot actions. The resulting 4B policy achieves 98.8% success on LIBERO, 84.7% on LIBERO-Plus without benchmark-specific adaptation, 51.9% average success across five VLABench tracks, and 88.6% average success on the RoboTwin 2.0 bimanual benchmark. It also achieves the highest observed success rates among the compared methods on six real-world manipulation tasks across dexterous-hand and mobile-gripper platforms. Together with representation ablations and data-scaling results, these findings demonstrate the potential of latent-action supervision for scaling robot policy learning beyond native action-labeled data.

</details>


### [57] [ThinkLocoManip: A Whole-Body Control Agentic Harness for Legged Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34370)
*Nuo Chen,Shizeng Zhang,Yujie Yang,Jinghong Wang,Zhenyang Qu,Xiao-Ming Wu,Yunfei Guo,Zhongwen Lu,Haojie Liu,Chunpeng Lu,Qiao Sun,Xueyi Zhang,Jiangwei Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ThinkLocoManip，一个面向腿式移动操作（loco-manipulation）的智能体化框架，采用"推理-行动交错"范式并编排感知、导航、操作、运动等专用工具，配合关键帧思考机制，在真实世界长时程任务中成功率达 77%，远超最佳 plan-then-act 基线（37.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模环境中的长时程腿式移动操作任务对泛化性与错误恢复能力要求很高。现有方法要么直接生成动作，要么采用"先规划后执行"（plan-then-act）范式，二者在长期执行中都难以兼顾泛化与从失败中恢复的能力；同时已有机器人智能体框架普遍未把"运动（locomotion）"作为独立的工具域纳入推理与执行。

Method: 1）采用推理与行动交错（interleaved reasoning-and-acting）范式，使智能体持续感知当前状态、执行动作、观察结果并调整策略；2）构建智能体化 harness，编排感知、导航、操作、运动四类专用工具，实现异构能力的灵活组合，并将 locomotion 显式作为工具域；3）设计一组技能，同时考虑单一功能域内的工具使用与跨域协调；4）提出关键帧思考机制（keyframe thinking），保留关键执行上下文以支持高效错误恢复。

Result: 在真实世界的多项挑战性长时程腿式移动操作任务上，ThinkLocoManip 显著优于直接动作生成与 plan-then-act 两类代表性方法；成功率达 77%，是最强 plan-then-act 基线（37.1%）的两倍以上。消融实验进一步验证了交错推理与行动、工具编排以及跨域协调技能执行各自的有效性。

Conclusion: 将交错推理-行动范式、多工具编排（含运动工具域）与关键帧记忆机制结合，可有效提升腿式移动操作智能体在长时程任务中的泛化性与错误恢复能力，为机器人智能体框架设计提供了可验证的新思路。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose ThinkLocoManip, an agentic harness for legged loco-manipulation designed to tackle complex, long-horizon tasks in large-scale environments. Existing approaches typically follow either direct action generation or a plan-then-act paradigm, both of which can struggle with the generalization and error-recovery capabilities required for extended task execution. In contrast, ThinkLocoManip adopts an interleaved reasoning-and-acting paradigm, allowing the agent to continuously reason about its current state, execute actions, observe their outcomes, and adapt its strategy accordingly. Building on this paradigm, our agentic harness orchestrates a suite of specialized tools for perception, navigation, manipulation, and locomotion, enabling flexible composition of heterogeneous capabilities. Unlike existing robotic agentic harnesses, ThinkLocoManip explicitly incorporates locomotion as a tool domain for agent reasoning and execution. We further design a set of skills that explicitly account for both tool usage within each functional domain and coordination across domains, supporting effective execution of complex behaviors over long horizons. To tackle task failure during long-horizon interactions, we develop keyframe thinking mechanism to preserve critical execution context and support efficient error recovery. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate that ThinkLocoManip substantially outperforms representative methods based on both direct action generation and plan-then-act paradigms across challenging long-horizon legged loco-manipulation tasks. Notably, ThinkLocoManip achieves a success rate of 77%, more than twice that of the strongest plan-then-act baseline (37.1%). Our experiments further validate the effectiveness of interleaved reasoning and acting, tool orchestration, and coordinated skill execution.

</details>


### [58] [On Distributed Sensing and Adversarial Localization of Narrowband Acoustic Sources for Offshore Data Center Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34393)
*Adnan Abdullah,David Blow,Harsha Gundala Sureshbabu,Dongyoon Joung,Sara Rampazzi,Md Jahidul Islam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向海上数据中心防护的被动声学监视框架，利用移动接收机与固定接收机临时构成"锚点对"以约束窄带信号的TDOA模糊解空间，并通过"锚点约束初定位+粒子滤波联合跟踪（位置与速度）"的两阶段流程实现稀疏传感器条件下窄带对抗声源的定位与跟踪，经仿真、ROS-Gazebo演示与开放水域实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上大型数据中心面临窄带对抗性声源的潜在威胁，需要被动声学监视能力。但常规声源定位（SSL）方法依赖宽带信号与紧凑阵列，在窄带发射、传感器稀疏的场景下，TDOA测量出现混叠，解空间高度模糊，传统方法失效，因此需要新的定位跟踪方案。

Method: 引入移动接收机，与选定的固定接收机临时配对形成锚点对，以约束可行解空间；两阶段流程：第一阶段利用锚点约束估计声源初始位置，第二阶段以此初始化粒子滤波器，结合一个实时锚点测量与质量自适应的固定对测量，联合跟踪声源的位置与速度。

Result: 通过蒙特卡洛声学仿真、ROS-Gazebo机器人演示以及开放水域现场实验进行评估：仿真中定位成功率达91%；在接收机位置不确定与传感噪声条件下，10次现场试验中有9次跟踪误差低于2m。

Conclusion: 结果验证了在稀疏传感器与窄带信号约束下，所提锚点辅助与粒子滤波相结合的方法可有效克服TDOA混叠模糊，表明被动声学监视用于保护大型基础设施免受声学威胁具有可行性。

Abstract: This report investigates an acoustic surveillance framework for localizing and tracking narrowband adversarial acoustic sources that threaten offshore data centers. Unlike conventional sound source localization (SSL) methods that rely on broadband signals and compact receiver arrays, the considered scenario involves narrowband emissions and sparse sensors. In such scenarios, the observed time-difference-of-arrival (TDOA) measurements are aliased and produce an ambiguous solution space, rendering conventional localization methods ineffective. To address this challenge, we integrate a mobile receiver that temporarily forms anchor pairs with selected fixed receivers, constraining the feasible solution space. The proposed two-stage pipeline first estimates the initial source position using anchor constraints, then initializes a particle filter that jointly tracks source position and velocity using one live anchor measurement and quality-adaptive fixed-pair measurements. We evaluate the proposed method through Monte Carlo acoustic simulations, ROS-Gazebo robotic demonstrations, and open-water field experiments. Our framework achieves a localization success rate of 91% in simulation, while 9 of 10 field trials show tracking error below 2m under receiver-position uncertainty and noisy sensing conditions. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of passive acoustic surveillance for protecting large-scale infrastructure against acoustic threats.

</details>


### [59] [AuRA: From Atoms to Bits through Autonomous Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34452)
*Xinchen Yu,Yoonkyo Jung,Haoran Chen,Zikui Cai,Aayush Talreja,Seungjae Lee,Hyunjin Kim,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AuRA 是一个自动化机器人交互与数据准备系统，用于可变形物体的数字孪生重建，显著减少人工劳动与采集时间，并保持可比的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真支撑大规模机器人学习实验，但获取物理一致的数字孪生通常依赖人工操作机器人、手工标注交互记录。本文希望减少这种对人类劳动的依赖，实现数字孪生重建中机器人交互与数据准备的自动化。

Method: AuRA 结合三项设计：执行感知的抓取选择、由机器人状态推导的夹爪运动测量，以及配准后的交互前几何与外观先验。这些设计使采集能自主完成，避免依赖视觉夹爪跟踪，并恢复被机器人遮挡的物体信息。

Result: 在13个可变形物体上，每个成功重建资产所需的人工劳动减少88.5%，采集实际耗时减少55.4%。相对人工采集的 PhysTwin 基线，在已知夹爪运动下的未来预测中，9项指标中有7项提升，其中单向 L1 Chamfer 距离降低28.1%；在评估的未见交互下，平均轮廓 IoU 与 Chamfer 距离与之相当。

Conclusion: 自主机器人交互有望支撑更高效的数字孪生采集，且重建质量与人工采集相当，表明自动化方案在效率与质量之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Simulation enables robot-learning experiments at scale, but acquiring physics-informed digital twins often requires human manipulation and manual annotation of recorded interactions. To reduce this reliance on human effort, we present AuRA, a system that automates robot interaction and data preparation for digital-twin reconstruction. AuRA combines execution-aware grasp selection, gripper-motion measurements derived from robot states, and registered pre-interaction geometry and appearance priors. These choices enable autonomous collection, avoid reliance on visual gripper tracking, and recover object information obscured by the robot. Across 13 deformable objects, AuRA reduces active human labor and wall-clock collection time per successfully reconstructed asset by 88.5% and 55.4%, respectively. Relative to the human-collected PhysTwin baseline, AuRA improves seven of nine metrics for future prediction under known gripper motion, including a 28.1% reduction in one-way $L_1$ Chamfer distance. It also achieves similar mean silhouette IoU and Chamfer distance under the evaluated unseen interactions. These results highlight the potential of autonomous robot interaction to support more efficient digital-twin acquisition with comparable reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [60] [Scaling Dexterous Manipulation via Action Representation Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34453)
*Yanjinghao Xu,Jiexin Zhang,Huan Zhao,Jianjia Mo,Zhenghao Yin,Dingkang Liang,Xiang Bai,Han Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ARPG，将动作表示学习与动作生成解耦：先在大规模人类操作数据上预训练动作表示编码器，再冻结该编码器、在表示空间中训练小型生成主干（130M）学习任务条件动作分布，在 DexJoCo 上取得 SOTA，双臂灵巧操作平均成功率从 π0.5 的 10.1% 提升到 44.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视觉、语言与视频预训练已显著提升机器人操作模型的感知与泛化能力，但复杂灵巧操作的动作生成仍受限于机器人演示数据稀缺。有限的机器人演示需同时用于学习动作表示和任务条件动作分布，导致动作表示难以充分学习，进而限制下游生成模型产生复杂灵巧动作的能力。

Method: 显式将动作表示学习与动作生成分离：第一步在大规模人类操作数据上预训练动作表示编码器；第二步冻结该编码器，训练一个紧凑的生成主干（130M 参数），在编码器表示空间中学习任务条件动作分布。

Result: 在 DexJoCo 基准上达到 SOTA；双臂灵巧操作平均成功率由 π0.5 的 10.1% 提升至 44.7%；缩放实验表明，人类操作行为覆盖范围更广可有效提升下游性能。

Conclusion: 动作表示预训练为提升复杂灵巧操作能力提供了一个独立于数据规模与模型规模的扩展维度。

Abstract: Large-scale vision, language, and video pretraining has substantially improved the perception and generalization capabilities of robotic manipulation models, but action generation for complex dexterous manipulation remains constrained by the scarcity of robot demonstrations. Limited robot demonstrations must be used to learn both action representations and task-conditioned action distributions, making it difficult to adequately learn action representations and, in turn, limiting the ability of downstream generative models to generate complex dexterous actions. To address this limitation, we propose ARPG (Action Representation Pretraining and Generation), which explicitly separates action representation learning from action generation: we first pretrain an action representation encoder on large-scale human manipulation data. We then freeze the encoder and train a compact generative backbone (130M parameters) to learn task-conditioned action distributions in the encoder's representation space. Experimental results show that ARPG achieves state-of-the-art performance on the DexJoCo benchmark and improves the average success rate on bimanual dexterous manipulation from 10.1% for $π_{0.5}$ to 44.7%. Scaling experiments further show that broader coverage of human manipulation behaviors effectively improves downstream performance. These results indicate that action representation pretraining provides an independent scaling dimension for improving complex dexterous manipulation capabilities.

</details>


### [61] [IntNet: A Communication-Driven Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Framework for Cooperative Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34466)
*Leandro Parada,Kevin Yu,Panagiotis Angeloudis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出IntNet——一个将车辆意图传输与自适应通信调度统一到端到端学习中的多智能体强化学习框架，通过观测预测模块预测后续策略输出并在车联网中共享，结合图注意力网络与动态通信调度器，在复杂城市环境中实现更好协调、更低碰撞率，通信开销最多降低60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶安全面临非平稳性、部分可观测性以及智能体间有效协调的挑战。已有合作式MARL方法依赖共享编码后的状态观测来改善协调，但在安全关键场景中这些策略并不充分，需要更直接的意图信息交换，同时避免通信带宽的浪费。

Method: 提出IntNet框架，将车辆意图传输与自适应通信调度统一到端到端学习范式中，对所有组件联合优化。核心是观测预测模块，使智能体预测自身后续的策略输出并通过V2V网络共享；采用无模型架构，使用图注意力网络处理接收到的消息，并引入调度器组件动态优化通信图以提升带宽效率。

Result: 在与现有先进通信式MARL方法对比的复杂城市环境（含自动驾驶与人工驾驶车辆）实验中，IntNet实现更优协调、更低碰撞率，并将通信开销最多降低60%；进一步评估了学习效率、可扩展性以及信息共享与带宽使用之间的平衡对无碰撞轨迹的影响。

Conclusion: 共享预测的意图信息而非仅共享编码状态观测，是提升安全关键多智能体协调的有效途径；将意图预测、图注意力消息处理与自适应通信调度联合端到端优化，可在保证安全协调的同时显著节省通信带宽。

Abstract: Achieving safety in autonomous driving through Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) is a critical yet challenging task due to non-stationarity, partial observability, and the need for effective coordination among agents. Although earlier cooperative MARL methods have aimed to improve coordination by sharing encoded state observations, we find these strategies are insufficient in safety-critical scenarios. To address this gap, we present IntNet, a novel MARL framework that enhances safety by incorporating the transmission of vehicle intent and adaptive communication scheduling into a unified end-to-end learning paradigm, jointly optimising all components for safe coordination. Central to our approach is an observation prediction module that enables agents to forecast subsequent policy outputs, which they can then share across a vehicle-to-vehicle network. Our model-free architecture employs a Graph Attention Network to process incoming messages and a scheduler component to dynamically optimise the communication graph for bandwidth efficiency. We compare IntNet against state-of-the-art communicative MARL methods in complex urban environments with both autonomous and human-operated vehicles, achieving improved coordination, lower collision rates, and reducing communication efforts by up to 60\%. Through extensive experiments, we assess the framework's learning efficiency, scalability, and the balance between information sharing and bandwidth usage for collision-free trajectories.

</details>


### [62] [TRIAGE: Efficient Vision-Language-Action Inference Needs Both Reuse and Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34519)
*Sooyoung Kwon,Carlee Joe-Wong,Kyunghan Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TRIAGE：基于 token 漂移阈值判定 KV 缓存有效性，在固定重算预算下对视觉 token 分别采取重算、复用、剪枝三种策略，在保持成功率的同时显著降低 VLA 模型端侧 prefill 延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型需部署在移动机械臂、人形机器人等无法连接工作站的嵌入式设备上，而每步控制都要处理数百个视觉 token，语言骨干的 prefill 延迟随 token 数量增长，难以满足实时性。现有免训练加速方法只做"剪枝"或"复用 KV"其中一种，二者节省的计算量相近，但缺少按 token 特性区分选择的依据，导致精度损失。

Method: 通过 token 级诊断发现：选择复用还是剪枝取决于该 token 的"漂移"（drift，即其浅层特征值相对缓存计算时的变化量）。漂移低于阈值时复用不劣于剪枝，超过阈值后复用带来的动作误差可达剪枝的 4.4 倍，且该阈值可按骨干网络校准。据此设计 TRIAGE：缓存漂移低于阈值且不过期则视为有效；在固定重算预算下，优先重算缓存失效的重要 token，对缓存有效的其余 token 复用 KV，剩余 token 剪枝。剪枝法与复用法成为该划分的两个极端。固定预算同时直接决定 prefill 延迟。

Result: 在 LIBERO 与 SimplerEnv 上，平均成功率与完全重算相差不超过 3 个百分点；在 Jetson AGX Orin 上 prefill 延迟最多降低 48%；仅重算 7% 视觉 token 时，在 LIBERO-Object 上比最强基线高 24.4 个百分点。

Conclusion: 按 token 漂移自适应地在复用与剪枝之间分配，并以固定重算预算锁定延迟，是一种兼顾精度与实时性的免训练加速方案，优于单一策略的极端做法。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are increasingly used for robot manipulation because they map camera images and a language instruction directly to actions. Robots that cannot be tethered to a workstation, such as mobile manipulators and humanoids, must run them on onboard embedded boards, where the prefill latency of the language backbone grows with the hundreds of visual tokens processed at every control step. To meet real-time limits, training-free accelerators recompute only a subset of the visual tokens and skip the rest, either by pruning them or by reusing their cached key-value (KV) states. The two options save nearly the same computation, yet existing methods apply only one of them to every skipped token. Through a token-level diagnostic, we show that which option keeps the action closer to full recomputation depends on the token's drift, the change in its shallow-layer value since its cache was computed. On both backbones tested, reusing tokens below a drift threshold was no worse than pruning them, whereas beyond it, reuse yields up to 4.4x the action error of pruning; the same diagnostic calibrates this threshold per backbone. Building on this finding, we propose TRIAGE, which deems a cache valid when its drift is below this threshold and it is not too old. TRIAGE recomputes a fixed budget of tokens, prioritizing important tokens with invalid caches, reuses every other token with a valid cache, and prunes the rest; prune-only and reuse-only methods are the two extremes of this partition. Because the budget fixes the number of recomputed tokens, it directly sets the prefill latency. On LIBERO and SimplerEnv, TRIAGE stays within 3 pp of full recomputation in average success rate while cutting prefill latency on a Jetson AGX Orin by up to 48%, and with only 7% of visual tokens recomputed, it beats the strongest baseline by 24.4 pp on LIBERO-Object.

</details>


### [63] [How High Can GPT-6 Astra Climb in Contact-Rich Manipulation? PhysLadder: A Hierarchical, Matched Sim-Real Benchmark for Physical Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34527)
*Haibao Yu,Jiaqi Meng,Zijian Zhang,Bowen Jing,Lei Yang,Yue Hu,Chenchen Ge,Yiming Hou,Chenbo Xin,Dawen Ye,Haifang Qin,Jin Li,Weitao Zhou,Jianing Qiu,Jiankai Sun,Dandan Zhang,Zaiqing Nie,Ping Luo,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PhysLadder——面向接触丰富操作的分层Sim-Real基准，含16项家务任务、三级累积难度；评测GPT-6 Astra零样本表现，真实环境成功率由L1的80.0%降至L2的47.6%、L3的0.0%，仿真分别为66.7%、47.6%、0.0%，多数任务两环境结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测难以刻画机器人在接触丰富操作中随交互需求递增的能力边界，且缺乏物理与视觉上严格匹配的仿真—真实对照基准，难以判断仿真能否作为可重复、可扩展的测试平台。

Method: 构建分层Sim-Real基准PhysLadder，16项家务任务分为三个累积层级：L1接触选择与几何对齐、L2持续接触下的力调节、L3接触重构与力调节的协同；每个任务在物理与视觉匹配的仿真和真实环境中实例化，共享任务定义与成功判据。对GPT-6 Astra进行零样本评测，每任务每环境3次试验（共48次/环境），并分析失败模式。

Result: 真实环境成功率从L1的80.0%降至L2的47.6%、L3的0.0%；仿真对应为66.7%、47.6%、0.0%。16项任务中10项在两环境成功次数完全相同，其余6项仅相差1次（三次试验中）。跨环境反复出现空间推理、接触选择、力调节与协同方面的困难，导致局部物理进展难以累积为任务完成。

Conclusion: 研究刻画了Astra随交互需求上升而暴露的能力边界；对该模型而言，匹配的仿真可作为可重复、可扩展的测试平台，用于研究限制可靠操作的持续协同物理交互。样本量较小（每任务3次试验），结论具有一定探索性。

Abstract: We introduce PhysLadder, a hierarchical Sim-Real benchmark for evaluating physical interaction capabilities in contact-rich manipulation. Its 16 household tasks span three cumulative levels: contact selection and geometric alignment (L1), force regulation within sustained contact (L2), and coordinated contact reconfiguration and force regulation (L3). Each task is instantiated in physically and visually matched simulated and real-world environments, with shared task definitions and success criteria. We evaluate GPT-6 Astra zero-shot with three trials per task in each environment. Real-world success rates decline from 80.0% at L1 to 47.6% at L2 and 0.0% at L3, with corresponding simulation rates of 66.7%, 47.6%, and 0.0%. Ten of the 16 tasks have identical success counts across environments; the remaining six differ by one success out of three trials. Across both environments, recurring difficulties in spatial reasoning, contact selection, force regulation, and coordination often prevent local physical progress from accumulating into task completion. These findings characterize Astra's observed capability boundary across increasing interaction demands and, for this model, support matched simulation as a repeatable, scalable testbed for studying the sustained, coordinated physical interactions that limit reliable manipulation.

</details>


### [64] [Multimodal Socially Aware Framework for Human-Robot Interaction in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34534)
*Richard Xu,Dakarai Crowder,Alexis E. Block,Wenzhen Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个融合语音、视觉与触觉的多模态框架，用于生成情境适配的机器人语音与动作，并在VR中用18名被试、5个社交场景与人遥操作基线对比，自主系统得分高于中点但显著低于遥操作基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人需在理解人类多模态输入的同时协调言语与动作，而自主系统在自然度与社会适切性上仍存差距，作者希望量化并定位这一差距。

Method: 构建整合语音、视觉、触觉的多模态框架生成机器人言语与动作；在虚拟现实中开展18人、5个社交互动场景的被试内评测，与人类遥操作基线对比，结合行为编码与定性反馈分析。

Result: 自主系统在自然度与社会适切性上均高于中性中点，但显著低于遥操作基线；行为编码与反馈显示差距主要源于会话时机、对模糊请求的澄清以及空间协调。

Conclusion: 自主多模态社交机器人虽已具备基本可接受性，但在会话时机、歧义澄清与空间协同方面仍面临关键挑战，是未来改进重点。

Abstract: Social robots must coordinate speech and movement while interpreting multimodal human input. We present a multimodal framework that integrates speech, vision, and touch to generate contextually appropriate robot speech and movement. We evaluated the framework in virtual reality with 18 participants across five social interaction scenarios, comparing its performance against a human-teleoperated baseline. Although participants rated the autonomous system above the neutral midpoint for both naturalness and social appropriateness, the teleoperated baseline received significantly higher ratings on both measures. Behavioral coding and qualitative feedback revealed that conversational timing, clarification of ambiguous requests, and spatial coordination were important factors contributing to this performance gap. These findings highlight key challenges in developing autonomous robots capable of natural, multimodal social interaction.

</details>


### [65] [Dual Gradient Consensus for Adaptive Learning to Transient Out-of-Distribution Disturbances in Quadrotor Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34538)
*Mingtian Tan,Vasileios Tzoumas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双重梯度共识（DGC）自适应学习方法，通过比较残差梯度与长/短期梯度记忆来分类自适应状态并用置信图抑制不可靠更新，在四组混合域内外扰动下取得最低平均位置RMSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼轨迹跟踪在未建模扰动下常依赖在线残差自适应，但恒定步长更新对所有残差梯度一视同仁，短暂的域外残差会污染已学到的扰动模型。

Method: 保留DAPL（域自适应残差策略）的随机傅里叶特征模型、离线BPTT策略与最小二乘自适应，仅修改在线更新步长：将每个残差梯度与长期和短期梯度记忆比较，判定当前自适应状态，并借助置信图抑制不可靠更新，同时在持续域偏移后恢复自适应。

Result: 在数值四旋翼轨迹跟踪中，用四种混合域内分量与短暂域外事件的残差进行评估，与L1增强MPC、基于RFF的MPC、恒定步长DAPL及冻结参数基线相比，DGC在全部四种情形下取得最低平均位置RMSE。

Conclusion: DGC通过梯度共识机制调节更新步长，可有效抵御短暂域外残差对扰动模型的污染，并在域偏移持续时恢复自适应能力，提升轨迹跟踪精度。

Abstract: Quadrotor trajectory tracking under unmodeled disturbances often relies on online residual adaptation, but short out-domain residuals can corrupt the learned disturbance model when constant-step updates treat every residual gradient uniformly. We propose Dual Gradient Consensus (DGC), an adaptive-learning method for mixed in-domain/out-domain disturbances that retains the random Fourier feature model, offline BPTT policy, and least-squares adaptation of a prior domain-adaptive residual policy (DAPL) while modifying the online update step size. DGC compares each residual gradient with long- and short-term gradient memories, classifies the current adaptation regime, and uses a confidence map to suppress unreliable updates while recovering adaptation after persistent domain shifts. We evaluate DGC in numerical quadrotor trajectory tracking under four residuals that mix an in-domain component with short out-domain events. Compared with $L_1$-augmented MPC, RFF-based MPC, constant-step DAPL, and a frozen-parameter baseline, DGC achieves the lowest mean position RMSE in all four cases.

</details>


### [66] [The German Tank Problem & Navigating by Scent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34583)
*Kordel Kade France*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文借鉴二战"德国坦克问题"的序列号估计方法，将机器人气味传感器读数当作观测样本，构造气味羽流峰值浓度的无偏估计量，并给出精确置信界，作为机器人/UAV判定"是否已到达气味源、应继续搜索还是降落"的终止准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气味源搜索中"我是否已到达"这一问题实际很难：在源附近浓度梯度趋于平坦，湍流使信号间歇性出现，小型机器人往往缺乏足够定位精度来构建羽流分布图。因此需要一个轻量、低算力、仅凭传感器读数即可判断搜索是否终止的机制。

Method: 类比德国坦克问题中由缴获坦克序列号估计敌方产量的思路，把机器人采样的化学传感器读数视为观测值，用同一套估计程序推断羽流峰值浓度（进而定位气源）。估计量只需维护一个运行最大值和一个样本计数器；再结合精确置信界，将这两个量转化为机器人或无人机可执行的原则性决策规则（继续搜索或降落）。论文给出估计量及其界的推导，并分析其样本效率与假设失效时的鲁棒性。

Result: 推导出了峰值浓度的无偏估计量与精确置信界；实验展示了该方法具有较高的样本效率（少量读数即可收敛），并且在模型假设不成立时仍保持一定鲁棒性；最后总结了该估计量作为嗅觉导航终止准则的作用。

Conclusion: 序列号式估计为气味源定位提供了一个极轻量的终止判据：仅用运行最大值与样本计数即可给出有统计保证的到达判断，可嵌入机器人/UAV的嗅觉导航流程中作为停止搜索或降落的决策依据。

Abstract: A robot searching for the source of an odor must eventually answer a deceptively hard question: have I arrived? Near the source, concentration gradients flatten, turbulence makes the signal intermittent, and a small robot often lacks the positional accuracy to map the plume. Here, we describe a lightweight remedy inspired by a World War II statistics conundrum: the German tank problem. To approximate enemy armament production, Allied analysts developed an estimator based on serial numbers from captured tanks. Analogously, we use the same procedure over sampled chemical sensor readings from a robot to create estimates for the peak concentration of an odor plume, and therefore the odor's source. Treating each sensor reading as an observation, our unbiased estimator needs only a running maximum and a sample counter. An exact confidence bound turns both numbers into a principled rule that the robot or UAV can use to determine whether to keep searching for the plume source or land. We derive the estimator and its bound, demonstrate its sample efficiency, and examine its robustness even when its assumptions fail. Finally, we summarize the estimator's role as the termination criterion for olfactory navigation.

</details>


### [67] [What Does World Modeling Actually Improve in Robot Action Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34593)
*Hanwen Cui,Yuanyuan Li,Qing Guo,Zhida Zhang,Chao Chen,Xi Chen,Jie Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文通过受控消融实验，考察未来视频预测（future-video prediction）作为辅助监督对机器人动作学习的影响：在已见任务上显著提升成功率，但在分布偏移下收益不一致；并在π0.5上用轻量Transformer预测器验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）将未来视频预测与机器人动作学习结合，但仅看任务成功率提升，无法说明预测性监督如何体现在执行行为中，也无法说明其收益在任务和环境变化下是否持续。因此需要受控实验来分离预测监督的真实作用。

Method: 在FastWAM和OpenWAM内部进行受控的未来视频预测损失消融，固定同一模型族内的架构、初始化、机器人训练数据和训练步数；用轨迹聚类分析执行行为变化；并考察分布偏移（未见任务、环境随机化）下的表现；此外将未来视频预测作为辅助训练目标引入π0.5，仅训练时使用轻量Transformer预测器。

Result: 在已见任务的干净条件下，预测性监督分别使成功率提升24.38和72.91个百分点；轨迹聚类显示多个粗粒度轨迹簇内成功率更高，包括簇频率变化很小的情况，说明收益并非仅来自轨迹模式占比变化。但在分布偏移下收益不稳定：未见任务上的提升因模型与任务而异，两个模型在环境随机化下成功率均低，预测监督无一致优势。π0.5上辅助目标使成功率由82.94%升至89.19%。

Conclusion: 未来视频预测是动作学习的有效辅助监督，但需区分"熟悉任务上的执行改进"与"可靠的迁移能力"；在所评估设定下，此类收益并不依赖大型预训练视频扩散Transformer（DiT）。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) combine future-video prediction with robot action learning, yet improvements in task success alone do not reveal how predictive supervision manifests in execution behavior or whether its benefits persist under task and environmental shifts. We conduct controlled future-video prediction loss ablations within FastWAM and OpenWAM, keeping model architecture, initialization, robot training data, and training steps fixed within each model family. Under clean conditions on seen tasks, predictive supervision increases success rates by 24.38 and 72.91 percentage points, respectively. Trajectory clustering further reveals higher success rates within several coarse trajectory clusters, including cases where cluster frequencies change little, indicating that the gains are not solely reflected in changes in the prevalence of trajectory patterns. However, these benefits do not consistently persist under distribution shifts: improvements on unseen tasks vary across models and tasks, while both models exhibit low success rates under the evaluated environmental randomizations, without a consistent advantage from predictive supervision. We further introduce future-video prediction as an auxiliary training objective for $π_{0.5}$, where a lightweight Transformer predictor used only during training increases success from 82.94% to 89.19%. These findings support future-video prediction as effective auxiliary supervision for action learning, while distinguishing improved execution on familiar tasks from reliable transfer and showing that such gains need not rely on a large pretrained video diffusion transformer (DiT) in the evaluated setting.

</details>


### [68] [RobotAgentBench: Towards Open-world Robot Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34649)
*Lingjun Mao,Jinglin Cao,Lukun He,Wenpeng Xu,Yuchen Yan,Hengyu Jin,Zijun Zhang,Jinzhou Tang,Songyao Jin,Ziche Liu,Yifei Shao,Junbo Huang,Fang Nan,Ruobing Han,Ziqiao Xi,Ziming Xu,Shuang Liang,Sibo Zhu,Wenyi Wu,Xinyue Wang,Yiming Huang,Xinkai Zou,Gao Zhu,Kun Zhou,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.RO
