TL;DR: 提出 RobotAgentBench (RAB)，一个面向开放世界家庭环境的机器人智能体综合基准，构建50个真实到仿真的家庭场景、多层级任务（90原子/18短程/8长程/4开放场景），从任务级五维度与场景级需求完成两方面评价智能体自主性，并统一规划-执行-验证框架比较VLA/WAM模型与多种智能体架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA与WAM模型虽提升了操作能力，但开放世界部署需要超越"执行预设任务"的自主性——即任务发现、情境推理、物理执行与自适应恢复的闭环。现有基准大多只评测孤立能力，无法刻画开放世界机器人自主性的复杂度，因此需要一个多层级、贴近真实家庭环境的综合评测平台。

Method: 采用智能体辅助、人在回路的real-to-sim流水线构建基准：50种家庭布局，含可交互物体与物理可落地任务；任务体系为90个原子任务、18个短程任务、8个长程任务、4个开放场景。评测分为任务级（任务发现、情境推理、操作、失败恢复、鲁棒性五维）与场景级（能力整合以满足高层用户需求）；并在统一的规划-执行-验证框架下对比独立VLA/WAM模型与多种智能体架构。

Result: 大量实验显示：操作能力与自主性之间存在显著差距；智能体式架构与递归自我改进具有明显优势；同时揭示了迈向开放世界机器人自主性仍存在若干未解挑战。

Conclusion: RAB将评测从孤立能力扩展到端到端自主性，为VLA/WAM与智能体架构提供了统一、可比的评测基准，表明"会操作"不等于"能自主"，未来需在长程规划、失败恢复与开放场景泛化等方面继续突破。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action models (WAMs) have significantly improved robotic manipulation, yet deploying robots in the open world demands a higher level of autonomy beyond executing predefined tasks. Robot agents offer a promising paradigm for this transition, integrating task discovery, contextual reasoning, physical execution, and adaptive recovery into a closed interaction loop. However, existing benchmarks largely evaluate isolated capabilities, failing to capture the complexity of open-world robot autonomy. We introduce RobotAgentBench (RAB), a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating robot agents in realistic, open-world household environments. Through an agent-assisted, human-in-the-loop real-to-sim pipeline, RAB constructs 50 diverse household layouts with interactive objects and physically grounded tasks, encompassing 90 atomic tasks, 18 short-horizon tasks, 8 long-horizon tasks, and 4 open-ended scenarios. RAB provides a multi-level evaluation of robot agent capabilities: (1) task-level assessments systematically examine five complementary dimensions: task discovery, context reasoning, manipulation, failure recovery, and robustness; (2) scenario-level evaluation assesses how agents integrate these capabilities to autonomously fulfill high-level user needs. Furthermore, RAB enables systematic comparisons of standalone VLA/WAM models and diverse robot agent architectures under a unified planning-execution-verification framework. Extensive experiments reveal substantial gaps between manipulation and autonomy, demonstrate the advantages of agentic architectures and recursive self-improvement, and highlight the remaining challenges toward open-world robot autonomy.

</details>


### [69] [Dream to Assist: Intent-Conditioned World Models for Shared Vehicle Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34650)
*Jonathan DeCastro,Thomas Balch,Emily Sumner,Deepak Gopinath,Laporsha Dees,Patricio Reyes Gomez,Elliot Weiss,Megan Applegate-Kenton,Todd Rowell,John Leonard,Avinash Balachandran,Guy Rosman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Dream2Assist：基于世界模型的强化学习框架，将意图推断融入循环状态空间世界模型，通过虚拟协同博弈训练辅助策略，并给出资源高效的部署流程，在两个领域的真人实验与实车初步部署中验证了意图条件化能改善人机对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享控制下辅助智能体面临双重挑战：既要主动贴合人的意图，又要保持足够可读性，使人能理解并实时流畅地配合；同时从仿真策略到真实人类用户的迁移存在sim-to-real-human gap。

Method: Dream2Assist在循环状态空间世界模型上扩展意图推断，用虚拟协同博弈（fictitious co-play）训练辅助策略，在不剥夺人类自主权的前提下预判并支持离散的人类目标；同时描述将世界模型策略转化为安全、以人为中心的系统的部署流水线，通过策略筛选与奖励重构、迭代式人机接口设计来弥合仿真到真人的差距。

Result: 通过三项人类被试研究和两个性质不同领域的初步实车部署验证：高速竞速（N=30）与意图结构更丰富的城市驾驶（N=10）；结果显示意图条件化在力矩对齐与碰撞率上带来可测改进。

Conclusion: 总结了在安全攸关、实时人机协作场景中部署基于学习的辅助智能体的关键设计洞见与实践经验，强调意图条件化与迭代式人机接口设计的价值。

Abstract: Assistive agents in shared-control settings face a dual challenge: acting proactively to align with the human's intentions while remaining legible enough for the human to comprehend and fluently respond in real time. We present Dream2Assist, a model-based reinforcement learning framework for tightly-coupled shared decision making that addresses both sides of this challenge. Our approach extends a recurrent state-space world model with intent inference, enabling an assistive policy trained via fictitious co-play to anticipate and support discrete human objectives without overriding human agency. Beyond the computational framework, we describe the full deployment pipeline needed to translate a world-model-based policy into a safe, human-centered system in a resource-efficient way. Crucially, we address the sim-to-real-human gap via policy down-selection and reward restructuring within an iterative human-machine interface design. We validate the framework through three human-subjects studies and a preliminary on-vehicle deployment spanning two qualitatively distinct domains: high-speed racing (N=30) and urban driving with richer intent structure (N=10). Our results demonstrate that intent conditioning yields measurable improvements in torque alignment and collision rate. We surface key design insights and practices for deploying learning-based assistive agents in safety-critical, real-time human-robot teaming.

</details>


### [70] [Benchmarking Obstacle-Conditioned Safety in Household Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34674)
*Hyunwoong Bae,Jisu Han,Jiyeon Choi,Dohoon Kim,Taesup Moon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Introduces OSMA-Bench, a spatially matched scenario benchmark differing only in obstacle type, plus the Semantic Sensitivity Index (SSI) to measure whether clearance and distance-dependent speed/smoothness follow the caution ordering of obstacles; finds VLM- and VLA-based policies recognize obstacle safety but fail to ground it into motion, and that an explicit parameter-inference step improves VLM-based policies.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Safe household navigation should adjust clearance and speed based on what an obstacle is and how close it is, without over-conservatism. Existing evaluations do not test whether semantic safety distinctions are reflected in continuous motion, leaving a blind spot in policy assessment.

Method: Builds OSMA-Bench (Obstacle-Conditioned Safe Motion Adaptation Benchmark) on spatially matched scenario sets that differ only in obstacle type, and proposes the Semantic Sensitivity Index (SSI), which measures whether clearance and distance-dependent speed and smoothness follow the caution ordering of obstacles. VLM-based and VLA policies are evaluated, including with explicit safety prompts, plus an ablation adding an explicit parameter-inference step between obstacle recognition and navigation program generation.

Result: Neither VLM-based nor VLA policies consistently adapt motion to obstacle safety, even with explicit safety prompts. Analysis shows policies correctly distinguish obstacles by safety relevance but fail to reflect this in the motion parameters or actions they produce. For VLM-based policies, inserting an explicit parameter-inference step between recognition and program generation improves adaptation.

Conclusion: Exposes a gap in semantic-to-motion grounding: semantic caution recognition alone is insufficient, and there is a need to translate semantic caution into appropriately safe motion.

Abstract: Safe household navigation requires robots to regulate clearance and speed according to what an obstacle is and how close it is, without unnecessary conservatism. Yet existing evaluations do not test whether semantic safety distinctions are reflected in continuous motion. To that end, we introduce OSMA-Bench, an Obstacle-Conditioned Safe Motion Adaptation Benchmark built on spatially matched scenario sets that differ only in obstacle type. We also propose the \emph{Semantic Sensitivity Index} (SSI), which measures whether clearance and distance-dependent speed and smoothness follow the caution ordering of obstacles. Evaluating VLM-based and vision-language-action (VLA) policies on the benchmark, we find that neither consistently adapts its motion to obstacle safety, even with explicit safety prompts. Our analysis shows that the policies can correctly distinguish obstacles by safety relevance but fail to reflect this distinction in the motion parameters or actions they produce. For VLM-based policies, which recognize the obstacle and then generate a navigation program, adding an explicit parameter-inference step between these two stages improves adaptation. These findings expose a gap in \emph{semantic-to-motion grounding}, highlighting the need to translate semantic caution into appropriately safe motion.

</details>


### [71] [Resource-Performance Trade-offs in Intra-Aisle Robotic Picking: A Discrete-Event Simulation Comparison of Shuttle Configurations With and Without an Onboard Tote Lift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34708)
*Mohsen Reda Mahmoud Moursi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过离散事件仿真比较两种已实现的两层巷道内机器人拣选配置（无车载周转箱升降 vs. 有车载周转箱升降），并给出周期时间差异的统计结论，同时明确结果的适用范围与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比两种已实施的两层巷道拣选配置的性能差异，验证"用一套车载升降机构替代第二台专用穿梭车"这一拓扑方案在周期时间上的代价，为配置选型提供可量化的仿真依据。

Method: 采用离散事件仿真：无车载升降方案由两台层级受限穿梭车分别服务两层；有车载升降方案仅一台穿梭车停留在本层，通过车载机构把订单周转箱送至相邻上层完成拣选。共设置30组"配置×任务规模"实验（n=1–15个库位，各10次重复），得到300个重复级周期时间观测；对重复均值做双侧Welch检验，并对15次比较做Holm校正。

Result: 车载升降配置在所有测试的任务规模下平均周期时间均更长。n=15时两者均值分别为299.477与333.231 s/周期，增加33.753 s（+11.27%）；由周期时间折算的物品速率为180.314与162.050 件/小时（−10.13%）。作者强调该速率是3600n除以场景平均周期时间的折算值，并非两台层级受限穿梭车并发能力的测量。

Conclusion: 研究定量刻画了在同一两层模块内由两台专用穿梭车缩减为一台所带来的周期性能差异；但两种配置的任务库位取自不同的合格层级集合，因此结论针对"配置+任务生成"整体方案，而非相同拣选位置下5秒周转箱升降操作的孤立效应；亦未量化系统级最大吞吐、投资成本或可靠性。

Abstract: This study compares two implemented intra-aisle robotic-picking configurations through discrete-event simulation. Without an onboard tote lift, two level-captive shuttles serve the two tested levels; with the lift, one shuttle remains on its horizontal level and its onboard mechanism moves the order tote to allow picks from the adjacent upper level. Thirty configuration-task-size experiments (n = 1-15 locations; ten replications each) yield 300 replication-level cycle-time observations. Two-sided Welch tests are applied to the replication means, with Holm adjustment across the 15 comparisons. The onboard-tote-lift configuration has longer mean cycle time at all tested task sizes. At n = 15, the means are 299.477 and 333.231 s/cycle, respectively: a 33.753 s increase (11.27%). The corresponding cycle-time-derived item rates are 180.314 and 162.050 items/h (-10.13%). These rates are calculated as 3600n divided by the scenario-mean cycle time; they are not measurements of the combined concurrent capacity of two level-captive shuttles. Importantly, task locations are sampled from different eligible-level sets in the two configurations, so the result concerns the implemented configuration-and-task-generation packages, not the isolated effect of a five-second tote-lift operation under identical picked locations. The study quantifies a cycle-performance difference alongside a reduction from two dedicated shuttles to one within the tested two-level block; it does not quantify system-level maximum throughput, investment cost, or reliability.

</details>


### [72] [VLA-HWBench: Where the Time Goes When VLA Models Run on Edge Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34717)
*Borui Xu,Hongshi Tan,Wenqi Jiang,Bingsheng He,Ming Wu,Jason Zeng,Michael Heinrich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文对18个开源VLA模型在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor与AMD Ryzen AI Max+ PRO 395上进行七阶段推理剖析与基准测试，提出三点洞见：动作生成策略比模型规模更关键、带4-bit权重的算子融合收益最大但通用性差、优化后瓶颈转移为"少token阶段受限于内存带宽、其余阶段受限于软件"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型正迁移到机器人边缘设备，推理时延直接约束闭环控制；但如何协同设计模型、推理软件与边缘硬件以实现实时推理尚不明确。现有工作只报告任务成功率，动作生成策略覆盖有限、推理阶段划分至多三段，且缺乏同一模型下多种推理优化的横向比较。

Method: 在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor与AMD Ryzen AI Max+ PRO 395两个平台上，对18个开源VLA模型在官方发布后端与替代后端上进行基准测试，并将推理过程拆分为七个阶段进行细粒度时延剖析；同时在相同模型上对比不同推理优化（如torch.compile、算子融合、4-bit低比特权重）的效果，并分析时延与参数量、动作生成策略之间的关系。

Result: 第一，动作生成策略比模型规模更重要：在19个Jetson配置中有14个占主导，时延在Jetson上相差82倍、在AMD上相差157倍，参数量最多只能解释15%的时延方差；少数流匹配步生成长动作块的模型可跟上控制频率，逐token解码则不能。第二，带4-bit权重的算子融合收益最大，使π0.5与GR00T N1.7比torch.compile版PyTorch快2.6–2.9倍，但仅覆盖这两个模型且仅在Jetson上可用；优化还会使时间向缩短最少的阶段转移，如GR00T N1.7的CPU预处理占比从12%升至39%。第三，优化后Jetson上仅处理少量token的阶段（如去噪步）受内存带宽限制，其余阶段（未融合算子、主机CPU串行预处理等）均受软件限制。

Conclusion: 低时延边缘VLA应采用少步数的动作生成策略；推理软件需将算子融合与低比特权重扩展到更多模型与平台；边缘硬件需同时具备高内存带宽与快速主机CPU。

Abstract: Vision--language--action (VLA) models increasingly run on edge devices aboard robots, where inference latency constrains closed-loop robot control. However, it remains unclear how to design VLA models, their inference software and edge hardware so that robots can run VLA inference in real time. Most VLA models report only task success, while prior studies cover limited action-generation strategies, divide inference into at most three stages, and lack comparisons of inference optimizations on the same models.benchmarking 18 open VLA models across released and alternative backends on NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor and AMD Ryzen AI Max+ PRO 395, with a seven-stage inference breakdown. Our study reveals three insights. First, action-generation strategy matters more than model size. It dominates 14 of 19 Jetson configurations, latency varies by $82\times$ on Jetson and $157\times$ on AMD, and parameter count explains at most 15% of latency variance. Models that generate long action chunks in a few flow-matching steps keep up with control, whereas token-by-token decoding does not. Second, kernel fusion with 4-bit weights helps most, running $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.7 2.6--2.9$\times$ faster than PyTorch with \texttt{torch.compile}, but it exists for only these two models and only on the Jetson. Optimization also moves the time to the stages it shortens least, as CPU preprocessing grows from 12\% to 39\% of the GR00T N1.7 call. Third, after optimization, memory bandwidth limits only the stages that process few tokens on the Jetson, such as the denoising steps, whereas software limits all other stages, from unfused kernels to serial preprocessing on the host CPU. Overall, low-latency edge VLAs should use few action-generation iterations, inference software should extend kernel fusion and low-bit weights to more models and platforms, and edge hardware needs high memory bandwidth and a fast host CPU.

</details>


### [73] [Networked Humanoid Intelligence: Communication, Coordination, Collaboration, and Social Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34740)
*Loc X. Nguyen,Huy Q. Le,Ji Su Yoon,Nguyen H. Tran,Eui-Nam Huh,Choong Seon Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于"网络化人形智能"的综述，从通信、协调、协作和与人类交互的社会智能四个信息交换功能出发，系统梳理人形机器人从单体系统走向联网机群的文献，并指出未来开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人正从实验室平台走向共享工作空间、基础设施、任务与知识的联网机群，其运行与改进依赖网络中的信息交换；但已有研究多把人形机器人当作独立系统研究，缺乏对网络化人形智能的系统性梳理。

Method: 综述按四个信息交换功能（通信、协调、协作、社会智能）组织；先建立网络化人形系统的基础，界定各功能所回答的问题，并用统一的工厂场景将四者串联；沿六个维度（交互架构、交换信息、物理耦合、运行时间尺度、系统规模、具身形态）回顾文献；将真实与仿真人形机器人的结果同其他机器人平台或无身体智能体的结果区分开；并考察硬件、数据集与基准，区分本地风险与联网引入的风险（如信息过期、不安全的知识传播）。

Result: 给出网络化人形智能的四功能框架与六维分析视角；通过分离人形专属结果与非人形平台结果，揭示后者无法充分体现平衡、全身运动、接触与物理交互等人形特有约束；并梳理硬件、数据集、基准及本地与网络化风险的差异。

Conclusion: 提出未来开放问题：控制感知的网络、紧凑的以身体为中心的消息、物理落地的协调、网络与具身感知的知识共享、机群级社会一致性，以及可问责的人类监督。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are moving from laboratory platforms toward connected fleets that share workspaces, infrastructure, tasks, and knowledge with other robots and humans. As deployments grow, the operation and improvement of each humanoid depend on information exchanged across the network. However, existing research has largely studied humanoids as individual systems. This survey examines networked humanoid intelligence through four functions of information exchange: communication, coordination, collaboration, and social intelligence with human interaction. We first establish the foundations of networked humanoid systems, define the question addressed by each function, and connect them through a common factory scenario. We review the literature along six dimensions: interaction architecture, exchanged information, physical coupling, operating timescale, system scale, and embodiment. We separate results obtained with physical and simulated humanoids from those based on other robotic platforms or disembodied agents, because the latter do not fully capture humanoid-specific constraints such as balance, whole-body motion, contact, and physical interaction. We examine hardware, datasets, and benchmarks, and distinguish risks handled locally from those networking introduces, such as stale information or unsafe knowledge propagation. Finally, we identify open problems in control-aware networking, compact body-centric messages, physically grounded coordination, network- and embodiment-aware knowledge sharing, fleet-level social consistency, and accountable human oversight.

</details>


### [74] [SceneAgent: An Agentic Pipeline from 3D and 4D Captures to Simulation Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34745)
*Luke Hollis,Tianxing Fan,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SceneAgent 是一个智能体流水线，通过基于模拟器的视觉检查与修复，把视觉采集转换为可交互的仿真环境，并在重建保真度与动态场景分割上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉采集能提供场景外观细节，但转为可交互仿真环境需经历物体重建、物理建模及中间误差修正等多步流程，现有方法缺乏对这些步骤的协调与自动纠错能力，因此作者提出以智能体方式统一调度。

Method: SceneAgent 以模拟器内的视觉检查与修复为核心循环协调各步骤，输入支持 3D Gaussian Splatting（3DGS）、摄影测量、LiDAR、AI 生成的 3DGS 场景及动态场景 4D 视频；结合物体分割、物理参数估计、部件分解、关节建模与可变形仿真；并生成多样化物体变体（"digital sisters"）与脚本化演示以支撑策略学习。

Result: 在 GraspClutter6D 的杂乱箱体场景上，SceneAgent 使每个可评分编码智能体的 1 cm F1 均提升（有/无技能皆然），且重建优于 SimFoundry：所有智能体配置的 F1 均超过 SimFoundry，最佳为 60.75% vs 6.9%；在 4DCodeBench 的三个动态场景上，将 Gemini 4 Argon 的平均动态 IoU 从 0.86% 提升至 99.09%；并展示了多样化的仿真交互与初步的真机策略迁移。

Conclusion: 基于模拟器视觉反馈的智能体式检查—修复流水线可有效弥合视觉采集与可交互仿真之间的差距，在重建质量、动态场景建模及策略学习支持方面均具优势，并具备向真实机器人迁移的潜力。

Abstract: Visual captures provide detailed scene appearance, but converting them into interactive simulation environments requires object reconstruction, physical modeling, and correction of intermediate errors. We present SceneAgent, an agentic pipeline that coordinates these steps through simulator-based visual inspection and repair. The pipeline processes 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), photogrammetry, LiDAR captures, AI-generated 3DGS scenes, and 4D videos of dynamic scenes, and combines object segmentation, physical parameter estimation, part decomposition, articulation, and deformable simulation. To support policy learning, SceneAgent generates diverse object variants ("digital sisters") and scripted demonstrations. On a cluttered bin scene from GraspClutter6D, SceneAgent raises F1 at 1 cm for every coding agent with scorable outputs both with and without its skills, and its reconstructions are better than SimFoundry: all agent configurations exceed SimFoundry's F1 score, with a best of 60.75% versus 6.9%. On three dynamic scenes from 4DCodeBench, SceneAgent raises the mean dynamic IoU of Gemini 4 Argon from 0.86% to 99.09%. We further demonstrate diverse simulated interactions and preliminary policy transfer to a real robot.

</details>


### [75] [ISB-NMPC: Interception-Oriented Spherical-Bearing Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34789)
*Li Hong,Linkai Liu,Xiangyu Ren,Shuli Lv,Chen Min,Chunjiang Wu,Yujun Zhang,Dingchen Zeng,Quan Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向拦截的球面视线角度非线性模型预测控制（ISB-NMPC），将球面视线预测与四旋翼非线性动力学耦合，联合优化接近运动与目标可见性，实现低成本捷联相机四旋翼拦截方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搭载捷联相机的四旋翼拦截器是低成本对抗非法无人机的方案，但机体机动与相机指向天然耦合，导致"快速接近"与"保持目标可见"之间存在冲突。

Method: 在S^2球面上构建姿态补偿扩展卡尔曼滤波估计目标视线（bearing）运动、补偿感知延迟并预测目标视线以引导尺度自适应检测；将球面视线预测与非线性四旋翼动力学结合，构建NMPC联合优化接近方向与可见性。

Result: 30组配对仿真试验中拦截成功率达86.7%（对比控制器中最高），平均每试验目标可见时间占比99.11%；户外飞行在约150 m处捕获30 cm目标，远距离飞行速度达17 m/s，追踪四旋翼拖曳机动目标时约14 m/s。

Conclusion: ISB-NMPC有效缓解了机动与可见性的耦合冲突，在高保持可见性的同时取得最高拦截成功率，并在户外高速、远距离场景中得到验证；软硬件均已开源。

Abstract: Quadrotor interceptors equipped with strapdown cameras offer a low-cost solution for physically countering unauthorized UAVs. However, this configuration inherently couples vehicle maneuvering and camera pointing, creating a conflict between rapid closing and maintaining target visibility. We present interception-oriented spherical-bearing nonlinear model predictive control (ISB-NMPC), which couples spherical-bearing prediction with nonlinear quadrotor dynamics to jointly optimize target-directed closing motion and visibility. An attitude-compensated extended Kalman filter on S^2 estimates target bearing motion, compensates for perception delay, and predicts target bearings to guide scale-adaptive detection. Across 30 paired simulation trials, ISB-NMPC achieves an interception success rate of 86.7%, the highest among the evaluated controllers, with a mean per-trial target-visible time of 99.11%. Outdoor flights demonstrate acquisition of a 30 cm target at approximately 150 m, with interceptor speed reaching 17 m/s in the long-range flight and approximately 14 m/s while pursuing a quadrotor-towed maneuvering target. The software and hardware are open source at https://github.com/HOLYNOTBAD/ISB-NMPC and https://github.com/HOLYNOTBAD/holydrone, respectively.

</details>


### [76] [Behavioural Quality Is Not Learning Utility: Objective Mismatch in Human Behaviour Models for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34839)
*Haoyang Jiang,Dana Kulic,Michael Burke,Pamela Carreno-Medrano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文检验用于机器人强化学习训练的人类行为模型：常用的生成模型/行为评估指标（跨高速驾驶与物体交接两个HRI任务，并考虑多种轨迹表示）并不能稳定预测下游策略性能，且评估结果对轨迹表示敏感；即便已知真实测试行为分布，用其他分布训练也可能取得更好的下游表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要用"生成行为与真实人类数据的相似度"来评价人类行为模型，很少评估其作为训练伙伴（training partner）的实际有用性。这造成潜在的目标错配：行为指标好的模型未必能为下游机器人策略学习提供有用的训练经验。

Method: 在高速驾驶和物体交接两个HRI场景中，对多种类型的人类行为模型使用常见生成模型评估指标进行评价；采用多种人类轨迹表示；将模型评估结果与用对应模型训练的RL策略的下游性能进行对比分析。

Result: 现有行为评估指标无法跨域、跨轨迹表示一致地预测下游策略性能；且这些指标本身对轨迹表示的选择很敏感。另外，即使真实测试行为分布已知，针对另一分布训练也可能比直接对真实分布训练获得更好的下游性能。

Conclusion: 仅以行为相似度评估人类模型不足以为RL机器人训练选择训练伙伴；应以下游策略学习效果为导向建立评估与选择标准，且训练分布不必等同于测试分布。

Abstract: Human behaviour models are increasingly used to provide simulated human interactions for training robots via reinforcement learning (RL). Existing approaches primarily evaluate these models according to how closely their generated behaviours resemble real human data, while their usefulness as training partners is less commonly assessed. This creates a potential objective mismatch: a model that performs well according to behavioural evaluation metrics may not necessarily provide useful training experiences for downstream robot policy learning. Across two human-robot interaction (HRI) domains, highway driving and object handover, we evaluate different types of human behaviour models using common generative-model evaluation metrics. We consider multiple human trajectory representations and compare the resulting evaluations with the downstream performance of RL policies trained using the corresponding models. We find that existing behavioural evaluation metrics do not consistently predict downstream policy performance across domains and trajectory representations, while their evaluations are sensitive to the choice of trajectory representation. We also show that even when the true testing behaviour distribution is known, training against a different distribution may yield better downstream performance than training directly against the true distribution.

</details>


### [77] [Physics-Informed and Data-Driven Composite Disturbance Separation and Predictive Control for Airborne Electro-Optical Gimbals with Multiple Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34849)
*Yaokun Lu,Yifan Li,Zhixuan Zhang,Enmei Wang,Hao Teng,Jianzhong Qiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究机载光电稳瞄平台在周期与周期外复合扰动下的约束指向控制问题，提出一种扰动分离式模型预测控制（MPC）方案：用自适应周期观测器预测多谐波振动，用线性观测器估计非周期扰动并借助有界LSTM预测其在输入时延内的变化，同时通过输入集收紧保证执行器约束可行，并给出区域实用跟踪界。室内悬停下无人机两轴稳瞄平台实验表明，偏航与俯仰相对角RMSE分别为0.770°与0.350°，较线性自抗扰控制分别降低25.1%与45.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机载光电稳瞄平台同时受到载体振动引起的周期扰动、非周期扰动、输入时延与执行器饱和约束，这些异质扰动若统一处理易导致估计误差被优化器放大，难以同时保证视轴稳定与精确跟踪，因此需要按扰动物理特性分离补偿。

Method: 提出扰动分离的MPC框架：自适应周期观测器基于线性回归频率估计，在MPC预测时域内预测多谐波振动并嵌入预测模型；线性观测器估计非周期扰动，并由有界LSTM网络预测其在输入时延内的变化，在优化器之外进行补偿；采用输入集收紧在给定余量包络内生成可行指令，并在显式输入序列与预测状态裕度条件下推导区域实用跟踪界。

Result: 搭建无人机搭载的两轴稳瞄平台进行室内悬停对比实验，并辅以频率变化与参数失配仿真。所提方法偏航、俯仰相对角RMSE分别为0.770°与0.350°，相较线性自抗扰控制跟踪误差分别降低25.1%和45.9%；周期预测嵌入MPC、非周期补偿置于优化器外可抑制非周期估计误差放大，提升频率变化与参数失配下的指向精度。

Conclusion: 按扰动特性分离的MPC方案能在执行器约束与时延条件下有效抑制复合扰动、保证指令可行性并给出跟踪性能界，实验验证了其相对线性自抗扰控制在指向精度上的明显优势。

Abstract: This paper studies the constrained pointing control problem of airborne electro-optical gimbals subjected to composite periodic and aperiodic disturbances. Under carrier vibration, input delay, and actuator constraints, achieving line-of-sight stabilization and tracking is challenging. A disturbance-separated model predictive control (MPC) scheme is proposed to compensate these heterogeneous disturbances according to their physical characteristics. An adaptive periodic observer predicts multi-harmonic vibrations over the MPC horizon using linear-regression frequency estimation. A linear observer estimates the aperiodic disturbance, and a bounded long short-term memory (LSTM) network predicts its change over the input delay. Embedding periodic predictions into the MPC model while applying aperiodic compensation outside the optimizer limits amplification of aperiodic estimation errors and improves pointing accuracy under frequency changes and parameter mismatch. On the stated headroom envelope, input-set tightening provides feasible commands within the prescribed limits, and a regional practical tracking bound is established under explicit input-sequence and predicted-state margin conditions. A UAV-mounted two-axis gimbal platform is assembled for comparative indoor hovering experiments, complemented by simulations under frequency changes and parameter mismatch. In the reported trials, the proposed method achieves yaw and pitch relative-angle root-mean-square errors (RMSEs) of 0.770 degrees and 0.350 degrees, reducing tracking errors by 25.1% and 45.9% compared with linear active disturbance rejection control.

</details>


### [78] [Localization-Uncertainty-Aware UAV Swarm Search for GNSS-Denied Maritime Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34873)
*Siyuan Yang,Junning Lyu,Xiaoyu He,Lakmal Seneviratne,Shaoming He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出不确定性感知的CBF框架，用Fisher信息矩阵近似级联定位协方差，实现GNSS拒止海上无人机集群的连通性保持、避碰与高效搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNSS拒止海上环境中，无人机集群依赖机间测距实现协同定位，不确定性沿多跳参考链级联传播，进而影响连通性、避碰与搜索效率，现有方法缺乏对这种级联不确定性的显式建模与控制融合。

Method: (1) 基于Fisher信息矩阵构建级联协方差模型，刻画测距噪声与邻居不确定性沿定位链的累积；(2) 将协方差模型及其变化率融入CBF裕度，形成不确定性收紧的连通性保持与避碰约束，并给出条件不变性与性能优雅退化界；(3) 采用分层Voronoi-目标策略并结合编队保持，实现前瞻式覆盖搜索；分布式控制器以标准无偏EKF在环验证。

Result: 在多种多边形形状及6~30架小型无人机规模下验证；估计器在环单次搜索302.5 s完成；20次蒙特卡洛实验均重复完成，所有局部QP可行，UAV坐标系包含约束满足率不低于98.97%；六次MBZIRC实飞提供估计器在环证据；源码公开。

Conclusion: 所提框架能在级联定位不确定性下兼顾安全约束与搜索效率，具备理论保证与可重复的仿真/实飞验证，适用于GNSS拒止海上集群搜索任务。

Abstract: This paper studies UAV swarm search in GNSS-denied maritime environments, where inter-UAV ranging drives cooperative localization and uncertainty propagates along the multi-hop reference chain, affecting connectivity, collision avoidance, and search effectiveness. We propose an uncertainty-aware control barrier function (CBF) framework approximating cascading localization covariance through Fisher information matrices. First, the cascading covariance model captures how ranging noise and neighbor uncertainty accumulate along the localization chain. Second, uncertainty-tightened CBFs incorporate the covariance model and its rate into margins for connectivity maintenance and collision avoidance, with conditional invariance and graceful-degradation bounds. Third, a hierarchical Voronoi-target strategy with formation keeping supports efficient forward-looking coverage. The distributed controller is validated with a standard unbiased extended Kalman filter in the loop. The framework is demonstrated on several polygon shapes and swarm sizes from 6 to 30 sUAVs. An estimator-in-the-loop run completes the search in 302.5 s, 20 Monte Carlo deployments confirm repeatable completion with all local QPs feasible and containment in at least 98.97% of UAV-frames, and six MBZIRC runs provide estimator-in-the-loop evidence. The source code is publicly available.

</details>


### [79] [MMGNet: Multi-Modal 6-DoF Grasp Synthesis for Cluttered Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34893)
*Rui Cao,Jia-Hui Pan,Yichuan Li,Yunfan Yu,Chi-Wing Fu,Jiangliu Wang,Yun-Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出MMGNet，一种多模态场景级6自由度抓取合成网络，通过分层图像到3D特征注入与旋转抓取性建模，缓解点云质量不足与图像信息利用不充分两个瓶颈，在GraspNet-1Billion上取得60.34的AP，并在真实对抗物体实验中较AnyGrasp提升8.46个百分点成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景级6-DoF抓取合成依赖点云获取度量几何，但真实点云常含噪声、不完整且过度平滑；RGB图像可提供物体区域、边界与外观线索，但在场景表征与局部抓取推理中存在两个瓶颈，导致图像信息难以有效利用。论文旨在打通这两个瓶颈，充分融合图像与点云信息以提升抓取质量。

Method: 提出MMGNet多模态抓取合成网络，包含两个核心模块：一是分层图像到3D特征注入，在3D表征形成过程中逐层整合多尺度图像特征；二是旋转抓取性建模，先联合预测抓取接近方向与平面内旋转，再通过抓取阴影分组（grasp-shaded grouping）组织融合图像的局部特征。

Result: 消融实验表明两个模块带来互补增益；在GraspNet-1Billion（RealSense）上取得60.34的平均AP，超过所有对比方法；在真实对抗性抓取物体实验中，相比AnyGrasp成功率提升8.46个百分点。

Conclusion: 分层图像特征注入与旋转抓取性建模能够有效结合图像的语义/边界线索与点云的几何信息，显著提升场景级6-DoF抓取合成的精度与真实场景鲁棒性，验证了多模态融合在机器人抓取中的价值。

Abstract: Grasping is a foundational capability in robotic manipulation. Scene-level 6-DoF grasp synthesis relies on point clouds for metric geometry, yet real-world point observations are often noisy, incomplete, and over-smoothed. RGB images provide object-region, boundary, and appearance cues, yet two bottlenecks limit their effective use in scene representation and local grasp reasoning. We present MMGNet, a Multi-Modal Grasp Synthesis Network that addresses these bottlenecks through hierarchical image-to-3D feature injection and rotational graspness modeling. Hierarchical injection integrates multi-scale image features throughout 3D representation formation, while the rotational graspness jointly predicts the grasp approach direction and in-plane rotation before organizing local image-informed features through grasp-shaded grouping. Ablation studies show that these two modules provide complementary gains. MMGNet achieves a mean AP of 60.34 on GraspNet-1Billion with RealSense, surpassing all compared methods. In real-world experiments with adversarial grasp objects, MMGNet improves the grasp success rate over AnyGrasp by 8.46 percentage points. \emph{Code, models, and data will be released upon publication.}

</details>


### [80] [Quantile-Based Command-Reserve MPC for Current-Command Gimbal Pointing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34913)
*Yaokun Lu,Yifan Li,Enmei Wang,Hao Teng,Pengwei Hu,Jianzhong Qiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对受非匹配机械扰动影响的两轴电流指令万向节，提出分位数扰动预测器与受 tube 启发的模型预测控制相结合的方法，在嵌入式平台上实现高精度指向控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩擦、转矩脉动、电流响应滞后、模型失配与驱动指令受限使万向节精确指向困难，且扰动与指令通道不匹配，常规控制难以同时保证精度与指令可行性。

Method: 利用状态、指令与参考历史学习加速度残差的中位数与外侧分位数，构建分位数扰动预测器补偿可预测残差并刻画剩余不确定性；据此设计受 tube 启发的 MPC，用校准半径与分级输入裕度预留指令空间，且不增加优化变量；对给定线性误差模型与固定辅助策略给出有限时域 tube 包含性结论，并通过原生指令界与末端限幅器保证指令在范围内。

Result: 在两轴嵌入式平台上，四种控制器配置各重复七次试验；所提配置取得二维指向均方根误差 3.94 ± 0.10 mrad，评估窗口内无指令限幅样本，并单独报告了 100 Hz 标称实现的求解时间与循环超时记录。

Conclusion: 在测试工况下，残差预测与自适应指令裕度的结合可有效提升指向精度并保持指令可行，验证了该方案在嵌入式实时环境中的实用性。

Abstract: This paper studies the constrained line-of-sight pointing control problem of a two-axis current-command gimbal subjected to mismatched mechanical disturbances. Friction, torque ripple, current-response lag, model mismatch, and limited drive commands make accurate pointing more challenging yet interesting. A quantile disturbance predictor is proposed to compensate predictable acceleration residuals and describe their remaining uncertainty, with the aid of learning median and outer quantiles from state, command, and reference histories. A tube-inspired model predictive controller is proposed to reserve command headroom according to prediction uncertainty, with the aid of calibrated radii and stage-dependent input margins. The median improves the nominal prediction, while the radius adapts the command reserve without adding optimization variables. A finite-horizon tube inclusion result is established for a specified linear error model and fixed ancillary policy; its conditions are distinguished from the approximations used in hardware. Native command bounds and a final command limiter keep the sent commands within their prescribed range. On a two-axis embedded platform, four controller configurations are compared in seven repeated trials each. The proposed configuration achieves a two-dimensional pointing root-mean-square error of $3.94 \pm 0.10$ mrad with no command-limit samples in the evaluation windows. Its performance supports the combined use of residual prediction and adaptive command margins in the tested operating condition. Solver times and logged loop overruns are reported separately for the nominal 100 Hz implementation.

</details>


### [81] [Learning Direct Control from Optimization for Dynamic Target Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34929)
*Bing Sun,Yue Lin,Canhui Wen,Xin Chen,Yang Liu,Dong Wang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LECO将在线轨迹优化转化为离线学习直接控制策略：网络预测控制序列，经可微运动学模型映射为轨迹，使轨迹级目标直接指导控制学习；配合衰减专家引导课程与部署期预测性安全锁，实现平滑、鲁棒的动态目标跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态目标跟踪要求机器人在避障同时保持目标可见，并输出平滑可行的控制。在线轨迹优化需反复数值求解，计算负担重；基于路点的神经策略又把几何预测与控制执行割裂。作者希望兼得优化式目标的显式性与神经策略的实时性。

Method: 提出LECO框架：(1) 策略直接预测控制序列，并通过可微运动学模型映射为轨迹，使跟踪、平滑等轨迹级目标反向传播指导控制学习；(2) 采用衰减式专家引导课程，先初始化有效跟踪行为，再逐步将学习重心转向任务目标；(3) 部署时设置预测性安全锁，检查短时域障碍间距，当预测运动不安全或目标预测无效时，调用经典规划与控制作为回退。

Result: 在三种仿真障碍密度下，LECO相较该指标最优对比方法将线加速度均方根抖动降低14.1%–31.2%，并取得最高跟踪成功率，单周期计算耗时4.25–5.63 ms；在两个机器人平台上的实验进一步验证了室内狭窄通道与室外森林环境中的跟踪能力。

Conclusion: 实验表明，从优化中学习直接控制可在保证实时性的同时获得平滑、鲁棒的动态目标跟踪表现，为移动机器人目标跟踪提供了一种兼顾优化目标与神经策略效率的可行范式。

Abstract: Dynamic target tracking requires a mobile robot to maintain target visibility while navigating obstacles with smooth, feasible controls. Online trajectory optimization explicitly encodes these objectives but requires repeated numerical solving, whereas waypoint-based neural policies separate geometric prediction from control execution. We introduce LECO, a framework that transfers tracking optimization into offline learning of a direct-control policy. LECO predicts control sequences and maps them to trajectories through a differentiable kinematic model, allowing trajectory-level objectives to directly guide control learning. A decaying expert-guidance curriculum initializes effective tracking behavior and progressively shifts learning toward these task objectives. At deployment, a predictive safety lock checks short-horizon obstacle clearance and invokes a classical planning and control fallback when the predicted motion is unsafe or the target prediction is invalid. Across three simulated obstacle densities, LECO reduces root-mean-square linear jerk by 14.1--31.2\% relative to the best competing method for this metric and achieves the highest tracking success rate, with per-cycle computation times of 4.25--5.63\,ms. Experiments on two robot platforms further demonstrate tracking through indoor narrow passages and outdoor forest environments. These results demonstrate that learning direct control from optimization enables smooth and robust dynamic target tracking.

</details>


### [82] [JevVLA: Jev-Inspired Energy-Based Adaptation for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35010)
*Wei Wang,Wenqiao Zhang,Zheqi Lv,Zheng Dong,Yingjie Niu,Jiaqi Zhu,Jun Xiao,Chao Li,Yueting Zhuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 JevVLA：一个轻量、基于能量的适应框架，用单一可学习的动作偏好（能量）函数同时支持离散候选选择与连续动作修正/生成，在冻结基座策略的前提下提升 VLA 策略表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略存在两类决策形式（离散候选选择用于抓放、连续动作块生成用于运动控制），如何在保持预训练策略能力不变的情况下，用统一机制同时改进两者是一个重要的适配问题。

Method: 受结构化概率决策接口启发，冻结基座策略，仅学习一个以观测、任务上下文和动作为条件的可微标量能量函数，训练信号来自执行反馈、专家纠正或示教偏好；该能量函数被两用：用能量值比较离散候选，用能量对动作的梯度修正连续动作或引导其生成。各基准独立训练权重、固定输入输出编码，共享同一核心网络，约 8 万可训练参数。

Result: 在留出测试集上，相对各自匹配的基座策略对照组，任务成功率分别在 MetaWorld MT50、VIMA、LanguageTable、LIBERO Plus、CLIPort 上提升 1.28、2.31、1.60、1.52、1.28 个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果支持该轻量、Jev 启发式能量模型可同时适配离散动作选择与连续动作引导的可行性，且实现将开源。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies can select among discrete candidates for pick-and-place manipulation or generate continuous action chunks for motion control. Improving both forms of decision making through a common mechanism while retaining the capabilities of a pretrained policy is an important adaptation problem. Inspired by Jev's structured, probabilistic decision interface, we propose JevVLA, a lightweight energy-based adaptation framework for VLA policies. JevVLA instantiates this inspiration by modeling the relative quality of action proposals: with the base policy frozen, it learns a differentiable scalar energy conditioned on observations, task context, and actions, using execution feedback, expert corrections, or demonstration-derived preferences. JevVLA uses this single learned action preference function in two ways: its values compare discrete candidates, while its action gradients correct continuous actions or guide their generation. Candidate selection and continuous control thus share one trainable decision core. Each benchmark uses independently trained weights and fixed input/output encodings, while retaining the same core network with approximately 80,000 trainable parameters. On the held-out test sets, JevVLA improves task success over the respective matched base-policy controls by 1.28, 2.31, 1.60, 1.52, and 1.28 percentage points on MetaWorld MT50, VIMA, LanguageTable, LIBERO Plus, and CLIPort, respectively. These results support the feasibility of a lightweight, Jev-inspired energy model for adapting both discrete action selection and continuous action guidance. The JevVLA implementation will be open-sourced at https://github.com/ZJU4EmbodiedAI/JevVLA.

</details>


### [83] [Context-dependent Runtime Construction of Safe Regions of Attraction from Design-Time Identification for Multi-Candidate Simplex Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35040)
*Georg Jäger,Nils-Jonathan Friedrich,Norman Seyffer,José Cecílio,António Casimiro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出两阶段区域吸引域(RoA)认证流程，结合收缩稳定性与泰勒模型可达性分析，将高保障控制器下的闭环系统视为切换系统，从而在运行时构造上下文相关的安全RoA并安全切换HA候选控制器，在保持Simplex安全性的同时扩大高性能控制器的可用工作范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键信息物理系统需要兼顾高性能与严格安全保证。经典控制保守，优化与学习型控制器缺乏形式化验证。现有运行时保障架构中，安全滤波器存在抖振问题，Simplex的允许性受限于其高保障控制器认证RoA的紧致程度，因而过于保守。

Method: 提出两阶段RoA认证流程：先用基于收缩的稳定性分析确定目标集，再以泰勒模型可达性分析验证。将HA控制下的闭环系统建模为切换系统，据此(a)在运行时构造依赖上下文的安全RoA，(b)在多个HA候选控制器间安全切换，以优化恢复过程并最大化未认证HP控制器的工作包络。

Result: 在机器人路径跟踪任务上开展三阶段评估：ODE仿真理论验证、含未建模动态的高保真Gazebo仿真、以及实物硬件实验。结果表明Simplex架构在故障注入下保持安全，并成功屏蔽了行为克隆训练的HP控制器；决策模块开销可忽略（平均0.028 ms，最坏4.13 ms），占控制周期不足10%。

Conclusion: 通过收缩稳定性与可达性分析相结合的两阶段RoA认证及切换系统视角，可在不牺牲安全性的前提下显著降低Simplex架构的保守性，扩大高性能控制器的可用范围，且运行时开销极小，具备在真实机器人系统上部署的可行性。

Abstract: Deploying safety-critical cyber-physical systems requires control strategies that reconcile high performance and adaptability with rigorous safety guarantees. Classical control strategies offer stability proofs but are conservative, while advanced optimization- and learning-based controllers achieve high performance but lack formal verification. Runtime Assurance (RTA) architectures bridge this gap by introducing their own (dis-)advantages. Safety filters use online optimization and introduce chattering, while Simplex permissiveness is bounded by the tightness of the certified Region of Attraction (RoA) of its High-Assurance (HA) controller. Targeting this conservatism, we present a two-stage RoA certification procedure leveraging contraction-based stability to establish a target set for Taylor-model-based reachability analysis. We then treat the closed-loop system under HA control as a switched system, enabling: (a) runtime construction of context-dependent safe RoA and (b) safe switching between HA candidates to optimize recovery and maximize the operating envelope of the uncertified High-Performance (HP) controller. We validate this framework on a robotic path-tracking task using a three-stage evaluation: Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) simulation for theoretical verification, high-fidelity Gazebo simulation under unmodeled dynamics, and experiments on physical hardware. Results show that the Simplex architecture maintains safety under fault injection and successfully shields a behavioral-cloning-trained HP controller. Crucially, the decision module introduces negligible overhead (0.028 ms average, 4.13 ms worst-case), consuming under 10 % of the control period.

</details>


### [84] [VISTA: Active Exploration for Self-Improvement in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35051)
*Zhang Zhang,Shengjun Zhang,Simin Huang,Min Chen,Chong Xia,Xin Fei,Yueqi Duan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 VISTA 元智能体框架，将事后轨迹反思转变为主动自探索，通过维护 harness 图来迭代改进具身智能体的 harness，在 LIBERO-Pro、RoboCasa、RoboTwin 上以更少 rollout 取得更高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能不仅依赖预训练基础模型，还依赖 harness。已有方法把 rollout 当作静态日志做事后反思，产生的 harness 更新缺乏自主测试与后续具身交互中的精炼，因而改进有限。

Method: VISTA 维护一个 harness 图，记录所提 harness 的谱系，使元智能体可检索先前假设、复用有效机制并避免重复探索失败方向。每轮迭代从保留下来的 harness 版本及其执行证据出发，不直接依据历史轨迹提交更新，而是在环境中主动探索：自主生成新 rollout、探查执行失败与恢复行为，依据新证据迭代修改 harness；精炼后的 harness 重新评估并加入 harness 图。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Pro、RoboCasa 和 RoboTwin 三个基准上，VISTA 逐步提升任务成功率；与静态反思相比 rollout 效率更高，用更少的仿真 rollout 达到更高成功率。

Conclusion: 将事后反思重构为主动自探索，并借助 harness 图组织假设谱系，是提升具身 harness 改进效果与 rollout 效率的有效途径。

Abstract: Agentic embodied intelligence plays a foundational role in open-ended, dynamic physical environments. Rather than treating policy learning as an end-to-end mapping from instructions to actions, recent studies reveal that embodied systems depend not only on pre-trained foundation models, but also on harness. Prior approaches largely treat rollouts as static logs for post-hoc reflection, producing harness updates without autonomously testing and refining them through subsequent embodied interaction. To address this challenge, we propose VISTA, a meta-agent framework that transforms post-hoc rollout reflection into active self-exploration for embodied harness improvement. Specifically, VISTA maintains a harness graph that records the lineage of proposed harnesses, which enables the meta-agent to retrieve prior hypotheses, build on effective mechanisms, and avoid repeatedly exploring failed directions. Each iteration begins from a retained harness version and its execution evidence. Rather than directly committing an update from these historical trajectories, the meta-agent actively explores in the environment. It autonomously generates new rollouts, probes execution failures and recovery behaviors, and iteratively modifies the harness based on the resulting evidence. The refined harness is then evaluated afresh and added to the harness graph. We evaluate VISTA on the LIBERO-Pro, RoboCasa, and RoboTwin benchmarks. Experimental results demonstrate that VISTA progressively improves task success and is more rollout-efficient than static reflection, achieving higher success rates with fewer simulator rollouts.

</details>


### [85] [3D-Force Estimation for Dual-arm Manipulation of Deformable Linear Objects via Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35058)
*Annika Delucchi,Vincenzo Di Paola,Bruno Marco Quadrelli,Matteo Zoppi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出基于因子图的概率方法，仅用单一传感信息同时估计作用在可变形线性物体上的三维外力（大小、方向、位置）并重建其形状，在加噪的真实测量条件下验证了估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形线性物体欠驱动、通常细长（如线缆、电线），应变片与执行器读数等常规手段不实用；而可靠的接触力估计对精确控制与安全操作至关重要。

Method: 采用概率建模，将物体状态与传感器数据通过条件分布关联，用因子图联合求解三维接触力的幅值、方向、位置与物体形状重建；仅依赖单一类型传感信息，以保持方法通用性。

Result: 在测量中引入噪声、物体沿给定路径运动并受外力作用的逼真场景下，外力估计仍保持准确。

Conclusion: 概率化的因子图框架能以单一传感信息实现外力与形状的联合估计，适用于更广泛的可变形线性物体操作任务。

Abstract: Estimating forces acting on Deformable Linear Objects (DLOs) is challenging due to their underactuated nature. However, reliable contact estimation is crucial to precisely control them and enable safe manipulation tasks. Differently from the various approaches presented in the literature, this work adopts a probabilistic formulation where the DLO state and sensor data are related through conditional distributions. In particular, factor graphs are used to determine the magnitude, direction, and location of a 3D force acting on a DLO, while simultaneously reconstructing its shape. Since DLOs are not actuated and are typically thin, like cables and wires, strain gauges and actuation readings are usually impractical. Therefore, to overcome this issue, this work is tested using one type of sensing information only, thus developing a generic technique suited for a wider variety of tasks. Realistic tests are performed by introducing noise into the measurements while the DLO follows a prescribed path in the presence of external forces that are accurately estimated.

</details>


### [86] [PushAround: Collaborative Path Clearing via Physics-Informed Hybrid Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35062)
*Zili Tang,Zongyuan Li,Zishao Qiao,Meng Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PushAround 是一种“物理感知”的混合搜索方法，将几何连通性推理（W-clearance 连通图 WCCG）与短时域物理仿真结合，为大车在杂乱环境中生成可执行的可移动障碍物清理（拓宽走廊）方案，并扩展到多机器人团队。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂乱环境中的大型车辆常被可移动障碍物阻挡，需要移动机器人团队清理出可通行走廊。现有 NAMO（可移动障碍物下的导航）方法通常对障碍物位移做抽象近似，忽略机器人尺寸、障碍物质量、接触几何与接触力，从而产生物理上不可执行的规划结果。

Method: 提出 PushAround：一种物理信息驱动的混合搜索。1）重复查询 W-clearance 连通图（WCCG），找出需要拓宽的前沿缺口（frontier gaps），并按估计的下游代价排序；2）在搜索中生成直接推动或递归推动候选，选择障碍物、推动方向、接触点/接触面与推力；3）用短时域仿真对候选进行物理评估，仅接纳通过物理检验的候选。

Result: 数值实验将 WCCG 与标准连通性检查进行对比验证；方法可扩展到更密集的杂乱场景与最多四个机器人的团队；并对仿真执行端的物理参数失配具有一定鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过把几何连通性推理与物理仿真相结合，并显式考虑机器人尺寸、障碍物质量与接触力，PushAround 能得到物理可行的清理规划，并适用于多机器人协同与存在参数误差的现实场景。

Abstract: Large vehicles in cluttered environments are often blocked by movable obstacles, motivating mobile robot teams to clear traversable corridors. Navigation Among Movable Obstacles (NAMO) approaches often abstract obstacle displacements while neglecting robot dimensions, obstacle mass, contact geometry and forces, yielding physically infeasible plans. PushAround is a physics-informed hybrid search that couples geometric connectivity reasoning with short-horizon simulation to construct executable clearing plans. Repeated W-clearance connectivity graph (WCCG) queries expose frontier gaps requiring widening and rank them by estimated downstream costs. The search generates direct or recursive pushing candidates, selecting obstacles, directions, contacts and forces, and admits physics-evaluated candidates. Numerical experiments validate WCCG against standard connectivity checks and demonstrate scaling to denser clutter and teams of up to four robots, with robustness to simulated execution-side physical-parameter mismatch.

</details>


### [87] [Zero-HLM: Generalizable Humanoid Loco-Manipulation without Real Robot Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35075)
*Dianyi Yang,Mengya Liu,Zhumei Wang,Yu Liu,Xiaochen Zhang,Yixuan Li,Le Ma,Yuhang Yang,Weiqi Huang,Wei Liang,Baoxiong Jia,Siyuan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Zero-HLM 提出 real-to-sim-to-real 框架，用智能手机拍摄与仿真源演示生成可扩展训练数据，为四足/人形机器人全身移动操作训练提供无需大量真实示教的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人全身移动操作（loco-manipulation）的泛化能力受限于真实示教成本高昂；仿真虽可扩展，但需要视觉逼真、物理可用的场景以及可执行的全身动作，二者都难以获得。

Method: 框架包含两部分：(1) 高斯引导的智能体式 real-to-sim 重建，使视觉场景与物理场景对齐；(2) 运动-环境协同适配，利用 rollout 反馈修正全身参考轨迹与交互几何。最终生成 4,800 条物理验证过的演示数据。

Result: 在 Unitree G1 的三个任务上实现零样本迁移：仿真训练的 GR00T N1.7 平均全任务成功率为 66.7%，而真实数据训练为 46.7%；在泛化配置下，扩大仿真训练使汇总成功率从 23%（基于真实数据训练的基线策略）提升至 69%，且无需额外真实演示。

Conclusion: 高质量的仿真数据可替代大量真实示教，显著提升人形机器人移动操作策略的泛化能力，验证了 real-to-sim-to-real 流程的可行性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Learning generalizable humanoid loco-manipulation remains bottlenecked by costly real-world demonstrations. Simulation provides a scalable alternative, but requires visually faithful, physics-ready scenes and executable whole-body motions. We present Zero-HLM, a real-to-sim-to-real framework that transforms smartphone captures and simulated source demonstrations into scalable training data for humanoid loco-manipulation. Gaussian-guided agentic real-to-sim reconstruction aligns visual and physical scenes; motion-environment co-adaptation uses rollout feedback to correct whole-body references and interaction geometry. We generate 4,800 physically validated demonstrations for zero-shot transfer across three Unitree G1 tasks. Simulation-trained GR00T N1.7 achieves 66.7% mean full-task success, versus 46.7% with real-data training. On generalized configurations, expanded simulation training raises pooled success from 23% for base-trained real-data policies to 69%, without additional real demonstrations. Project page: https://young-bit.github.io/zero-hlm/

</details>


### [88] [The Language of Deformation: Semantic Failure Analysis as a Prerequisite for Dexterous Manipulation of Deformable Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35103)
*Ignacio Cuiral-Zueco,David Hardman,Michele Pierallini,Erfan Shahriari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"语义失效分析"应作为可变形物体操作走向成熟的关键一步，并用概念验证实验支撑这一主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚体灵巧操作已较成熟（抓取稳定性、控制、规划已在工业与物流落地），而线缆、塑料件、布料等可变形物体的操作仍困难重重，因为刚体方法无法刻画其额外复杂性。刚体操作的目标可用力平衡、滑移等明确准则定义，可变形操作则因材料、几何、行为差异产生更多种类的失效模式，且往往难以被检测，需采取不同的纠正策略，因此亟需对失效进行语义层面的分析。

Method: 论文将语义失效分析定位为研究方向：归纳可变形物体操作中的典型失效类型（屈曲/失稳、塑性变形、压力变化、撕裂等），界定语义失效分析的核心组成要素与挑战，并设计概念验证性实验加以验证。

Result: 概念验证实验的结果支持语义失效分析作为研究方向的潜力，表明其有助于识别与区分可变形物体操作中的多样化失效模式。

Conclusion: 可变形物体操作的成熟化需要超越刚体范式，将语义失效分析纳入其中；本文明确了其关键组成与挑战，并初步验证了其可行性，为后续研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation of rigid objects has reached a level of maturity where grasp stability, control, and planning strategies are successfully applied in industry and logistics. In contrast, manipulating deformable objects (cables, plastic pieces, cloth) remains far more challenging, as rigid-object methods do not account for their additional complexity. This paper identifies semantic failure analysis as a key step toward mature deformable-object manipulation and supports this claim with proof-of-concept experiments. Unlike rigid-object manipulation, where goals are well-defined in terms of criteria such as force balance and slippage, deformable-object manipulation involves a broader variety of failure modes arising from differences in materials, geometry, and behaviour. Typical failures include buckling, plastic deformation, pressure variations, and tearing. These diverse and often hard-to-detect events require distinct corrective strategies, making semantic failure analysis necessary. We define its key components and challenges and present experimental results supporting its potential as a research direction.

</details>


### [89] [How Tuning, Reference Preview, Actuator Modeling, and Fallback Policies Change Quadrotor Controller Comparisons: A Reproducible Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35165)
*Xuan-Quy Dao,Le-Minh Nguyen,Ngoc-Bich Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究在统一非线性四旋翼模型上系统比较六种控制器（级联PID、悬停LQR、BL-SMC、带LQR回退的LMPC、带预瞄的LMPC、几何SO(3)控制），量化调参协议、参考信息、回退策略与指令时序对排序的影响，并提出报告清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四旋翼控制器的对比研究通常只报告单一跟踪误差指标，但控制器排名实际上还受调参投入、参考信息（预瞄）、执行器预测、优化器回退机制和指令时序等因素影响，这种单一指标式的报告方式可能误导控制器选型结论。

Method: 在含旋翼陀螺耦合、电机滞后与饱和的共同非线性被控对象上，对六种控制器进行对比。主研究共556个episode（50 Hz），包括12候选匹配调参、400个配对评估episode和48格MPC因子实验；验证研究共1462个episode，在50组全新实现上以12/24/48候选调参预算重复评估。

Result: 采用12候选匹配调参后，平均RMSE排序相对固定基线设置发生改变，BL-SMC由末位升至首位，且该排序在全新实现上复现，48候选预算下保持不变；参考预瞄显著降低MPC跟踪误差，并在八字任务中消除回退行为；阶跃响应结果取决于回退策略、预测器与执行器权限的相互作用；指令时序方面，回放实测指令延迟使桌面机上4个标称MPC配置中3个超时终止、Raspberry Pi 5上全部4个超时，而将求解器限制在100次迭代可消除所测标称时序下的全部截止期违约。

Conclusion: 控制器排名不仅取决于跟踪精度，还取决于调参协议、参考信息、回退行为与实现时序。作者提出一套报告清单，涵盖跟踪精度、调参投入、完成率与失败率、回退活动、施加输入变化量以及指令时序，以提升比较研究的可复现性与公平性。

Abstract: Quadrotor controller comparisons commonly report a single tracking error, although the resulting ranking also depends on tuning effort, reference information, actuator prediction, optimizer fallback, and command timing. We quantify these effects for cascade PID, hover LQR, boundary-layer sliding-mode control (BL-SMC), linear MPC with LQR fallback (LMPC-F), a preview-enabled LMPC-F, and a geometric $SO(3)$ controller on a common nonlinear plant with rotor gyroscopic coupling, motor lag, and saturation. A primary study (556 episodes at 50~Hz) combines matched twelve-candidate tuning, 400 paired evaluation episodes, and a 48-cell MPC factorial; a confirmation study (1462 episodes) repeats the evaluation on 50 fresh realizations with tuning budgets of 12, 24, and 48 candidates. A matched twelve-candidate tuning protocol changed the mean-RMSE ordering obtained with the fixed baseline settings, moving BL-SMC from last to first; the new ordering recurred on the fresh realizations, and a 48-candidate budget left it unchanged. Reference preview substantially reduced MPC tracking error and, on the figure-eight task, removed fallback activity. On an abrupt step, the outcome depended on the interplay between fallback policy, predictor, and actuator authority. Command timing also changed the outcome: replaying measured command-call delays terminated three of four nominal MPC configurations on a desktop computer and all four on a Raspberry Pi~5, whereas capping the solver at 100 iterations removed every deadline miss in the tested nominal timing profiles. Controller rankings therefore depend not only on tracking accuracy but also on the tuning protocol, reference information, fallback behavior, and implementation timing. We propose a reporting checklist covering tracking accuracy, tuning effort, completion and failure rates, fallback activity, applied-input variation, and command timing.

</details>


### [90] [RetroNav: Scene-Grounded Retrospection and Retry for Self-Evolving Lifelong Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35172)
*Diantao Tu,Yangdong Liu,Niannian Dai,Mengqi Rong,Shuhan Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RetroNav 是一个无需训练、可自我演化的导航框架，将导航执行、记忆积累与失败反思整合为统一闭环，在五个导航基准上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人导航方法多针对孤立任务，忽视跨重复导航回合的知识与经验积累；已有的终身导航方法偏重场景知识，忽略历史导航经验及其与场景证据的关联，导致无法反思过往错误以改进后续决策。

Method: 提出统一记忆系统，将几何与语义场景知识与历史导航经验相连接，并区分客观观察与任务相关解释；每次导航尝试后由独立评估器判断是否需重试，若需要则触发 Retrospection Agent，借助累积的场景知识和导航记录回看历史决策，生成有证据支撑的重试简报以指导下一次尝试。整体为免训练框架。

Result: 在五个导航基准上达到当前最优（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: 通过把失败反思与跨回合记忆引入导航过程，可无需额外训练即实现持续自我提升，验证了融合场景证据与历史经验的价值；代码将开源于给定链接。

Abstract: Existing robot navigation methods primarily focus on isolated tasks, overlooking the need to accumulate knowledge and experience across repeated navigation episodes. Although recent lifelong navigation methods have explored cross-task knowledge accumulation, they mainly focus on scene knowledge while neglecting historical navigation experience and its connection to scene evidence, limiting the ability to reflect on past mistakes and improve future decisions. To address this limitation, this paper presents RetroNav, a training-free self-evolving navigation framework that integrates navigation, memory accumulation, and failure reflection. Specifically, we develop a unified memory system that connects geometric and semantic scene knowledge with historical navigation experience, distinguishing objective observations from task-dependent interpretations. After each navigation attempt, an independent evaluator determines whether a retry is necessary. If so, a Retrospection Agent revisits historical decisions using accumulated scene knowledge and navigation records, generating an evidence-grounded retry brief to guide the next attempt. RetroNav achieves state-of-the-art performance across five navigation benchmarks. Our method will be open-sourced at https://github.com/3dv-casia/RetroNav .

</details>


### [91] [Thermal-Headroom Routing for Efficient Robot Perception in Extreme Heat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35231)
*Sparsh Roy,Kevin Zhou,Arhaan Shah,Shreyan Garg,Vihaan Joshi-Apte,Maxim Kudryashov,Nanda Cheruvanthala*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Thermal-Headroom Routing (THR)，一种轻量级模型选择策略：用闭式 RC 热模型预测处理器温度，提前排除可能越过降频阈值的检测器配置，从而在高温环境下兼顾准确率、时延与能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应机器人感知系统需在检测精度、推理延迟与能耗之间权衡；当环境温度很高时处理器会热降频（thermal throttling），使原本的时延模型失效，导致截止期违约与能效下降，因此需要事前预测热状态而非事后被动响应。

Method: THR 使用闭式 RC 热模型预测处理器温度，在推理前剔除预计会跨越降频阈值的检测器配置（YOLO11 的四种配置）。实验覆盖 7 条真实感知轨迹、8 种环境温度、20 个随机种子，并与延迟感知路由、固定阈值温控器、占空比上限以及等算力预算对照组比较；另用按截止期节拍、非重叠帧执行的方式复验，并在 Apple M5 上验证单节点升温近似。

Result: 在 45–55°C 环境温度且截止期紧张时，THR 在仿真中消除降频，截止期违约率降低 3.9–7.5 个百分点，按时帧的单位能耗较延迟信念路由降低约 12–13%；按截止期节拍执行时违约率降低 6.2–9.5 个百分点。调优后的温控器或占空比上限可在单一固定工况下达到与 THR 相当的效果；M5 实测支持单节点升温近似，但未验证真实硬件降频。

Conclusion: 预测式热路由在环境条件或标定热参数偏离静态策略调优工况时优势最明显；静态策略在固定工况下可与之相当。研究界定了热预测能够提升资源受限机器人感知效率与时序可靠性的适用条件。

Abstract: Adaptive robot perception systems must balance detector accuracy, inference latency, and energy consumption, particularly when high ambient temperatures cause processors to thermally throttle. We propose Thermal-Headroom Routing (THR), a lightweight model-selection policy that forecasts processor temperature using a closed-form RC thermal model and excludes detector configurations predicted to cross a throttling threshold. We evaluate THR with four YOLO11 configurations across seven real-world perception traces, eight ambient temperatures, and 20 randomized seeds, comparing it with latency-aware routing, fixed-threshold thermostats, duty caps, and a matched-compute-budget control. At ambient temperatures of 45-55 degrees C and a tight inference deadline, THR eliminates throttling in simulation, reduces deadline misses by 3.9-7.5 percentage points, and reduces energy per on-time frame by approximately 12-13% relative to a latency-belief router. A separate experiment using deadline-paced, nonoverlapping frame execution yields miss-rate reductions of 6.2-9.5 percentage points. A tuned thermostat or duty cap can match THR at a fixed operating point; predictive routing is most useful when ambient conditions or calibrated thermal parameters differ from those used to tune static policies. Measurements on an Apple M5 support the single-node heating approximation but do not validate hardware throttling. The results characterize the conditions under which thermal forecasting improves the efficiency and timeliness of resource-constrained robot perception.

</details>


### [92] [Labference: Benchmarking the Reasoning-Action Connection in Robotic Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35252)
*Shitong Liu,Jingwei Zhang,Sheng Lian,Xinyu Zhuang,Kaiwen Guo,Quan Liu,Chunhai Zhao,Jiaxin He,Fan Yang,Manli Tao,Tingyu Yuan,Jinqiao Wang,Hongwei Shi,Chaomin Guo,Xiao-jing Liu,Xiangyu Zhu,Zhuang Xiong,Feichan Yang,Chaoyang Zhao,Zhiyong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出Labference基准，用于评估机器人在科学实验中如何根据观测到的物理执行结果调整后续操作选择，并独立衡量操作选择、物理执行与任务完成三个环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准只以任务完成率或推理/执行指标做聚合评价，无法厘清"结果依赖的决策选择"与"物理执行"之间的联系，难以诊断具身AI在自动化实验中的真实短板。

Method: 构建Labference基准：设计受控的证据变化来测试结果依赖的行为适应；将操作选择、物理执行与任务完成分开度量；并从共享检查点出发，用匹配的Oracle辅助续接来考察不同支持形式对后续决策与任务完成的影响。

Result: 初步称量评估显示：被测VLA策略虽能成功转移物体，但存在目标选择错误；GPT驱动的控制在目标选择上正确，却出现执行失败且计算开销显著。

Conclusion: 结果凸显需将任务适配的推理与可靠、及时的动作相连接；Labference使这一连接可测量、可诊断，从而为面向科学发现的自主实验提供有针对性的改进依据。

Abstract: Realizing Embodied AI for Science requires robots to translate experimental goals into reliable physical procedures, reducing hazardous and repetitive human work while generating evidence for scientific discovery. Although language-based planning and learned manipulation provide complementary capabilities, their integration requires robots to adapt subsequent operations to actual execution outcomes. For example, an unsuccessful placement changes what must be done next, even when the experimental goal remains unchanged. Existing benchmarks assess planning, manipulation, and laboratory workflows through task completion and reasoning or execution metrics; however, aggregate performance leaves the connection between outcome-dependent choices and physical execution unresolved. To address this evaluation gap, we introduce Labference, a benchmark for evaluating how observed physical outcomes guide subsequent choices and how those choices translate into robotic laboratory actions. Its evaluation protocol is designed to test outcome-dependent behavioral adaptation through controlled evidence variations, while independently measuring operation selection, physical execution, and task completion. Matched Oracle-assisted continuations from shared checkpoints then assess which forms of support improve subsequent choices and task completion. Initial weighing evaluations reveal target-selection errors in the tested VLA policies despite successful object transfers, whereas GPT-driven control achieves correct selection but encounters execution failures and substantial computation costs. Together, these findings highlight the need to connect task-appropriate reasoning with reliable, timely action. By making this connection measurable and diagnosable, Labference supports targeted improvements toward autonomous experimentation for Embodied AI for Science.

</details>


### [93] [Reward-Guided RRT* for Budgeted Task and Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35289)
*Vinícius Araújo de Oliveira,Gabriel Ferreira Mendes Lourenço,Paulo L. J. Drews-Jr,Armando Alves Neto,Douglas G. Macharet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Reward-Guided RRT*框架，在连续状态空间中通过一系列局部"任务选择—运动规划"联合阶段逐步构造预算可行的空间分布式任务序列，无需预计算任务转移图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人任务常涉及空间分布的多目标点，需要在有限行程预算下同时决定访问哪些有利位置并规划可行路径；传统方法往往依赖预先计算的任务转移图（如PRM类），计算开销大且难以直接融入运动学约束。

Method: 构建基于采样的增量式框架：使用奖励引导的RRT*搜索，在每个局部阶段联合完成任务选择与运动规划；引入返回可行性（return-feasibility）检查，确保每次选中的任务在预算内可完成并返回；整体直接在连续状态空间中评估，无需任务转移图；并扩展至Dubins转向模型以处理车辆运动学约束。

Result: 在障碍复杂度与任务数量递增的实验中，所得解与VNS-PRM*相当，但所需采样状态数显著更少；在CoppeliaSim中用缩比例车式平台验证了Dubins转向扩展，表明框架能纳入车辆运动学约束。

Conclusion: Reward-Guided RRT*为预算受限下的任务选择与运动规划联合问题提供了无需转移图、采样效率更高的连续空间求解方案，并具备向带运动学约束的实际平台推广的能力。

Abstract: Robotic missions involving spatially distributed tasks often require jointly selecting rewarding locations and planning feasible motions under limited travel budgets. This paper proposes Reward-Guided RRT*, a new sampling-based framework that constructs a budget-feasible mission through a sequence of locally joint task-selection-and-motion-planning stages, evaluated directly in the continuous state space, without requiring a precomputed task-transition graph. The method incrementally builds a mission using reward-guided RRT* searches, while a return-feasibility procedure ensures that each selected task can be visited without violating the mission budget. Experiments with increasing obstacle complexity and number of tasks show solutions comparable to VNS-PRM* while requiring substantially fewer sampled states. We also extend the framework to Dubins steering and evaluate it in CoppeliaSim with a scaled car-like robotic platform, demonstrating its ability to incorporate vehicle kinematic constraints.

</details>


### [94] [VisGeo-PipeTrack: A Visual Geometric Servo Control Framework with MPC-RCAPID Control for UAV-Based Dynamic Pipes Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35342)
*Zhenyang Gao,Jianlin Chen,Yang Yu,Honghao Shan,Jiale Gao,Ziyan Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VisGeo-PipeTrack伺服控制框架，利用几何约束实现无人机对动态（振动）管道的自主跟踪：用YOLOv5检测管道标签并结合视觉几何约束实现精确定位，并提出MPC-RCAPID控制器（RLS实时模型估计+滚动优化动态调参）进行跟踪控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机管道巡检研究大多假设管道静止，而真实工业环境中管道常存在振动等动态运动，对无人机的自适应控制与计算效率提出更高要求，因此需要针对动态管道的跟踪方案。

Method: 框架包含两部分：（1）定位——用YOLOv5检测管道上的标签，基于检测标签的视觉几何约束推算位置以实现精确定位；（2）控制——提出基于模型预测控制的实时校正自适应PID（MPC-RCAPID），采用递归最小二乘法进行实时模型估计，并融合滚动时域优化动态调整PID参数。

Result: 在AirSim仿真与Tello无人机平台上验证了框架有效性。仿真中与标准PID、分数阶PID、自适应PID、模糊PID对比，MPC-RCAPID获得最短调节时间（3.26 s）和最低平均跟踪误差（3.52%）；Tello实物实验中，相比标准PID，MPC-RCAPID在三个测试维度上平均跟踪误差分别降低55.52%、26.72%和26.52%。

Conclusion: VisGeo-PipeTrack能够支撑无人机在动态管道环境中的高效自主跟踪，几何约束定位与MPC-RCAPID控制相结合在仿真和实物平台上均表现优于多种PID基线方法。

Abstract: While unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are increasingly deployed for pipeline inspection, prevailing research predominantly assumes static pipes. In real-world industrial environments, pipes often exhibit dynamic movements, such as vibrations. This imposes more stringent requirements on UAVs' adaptive control and computational efficiency than in static scenarios. Therefore, this paper proposes a servo control framework named VisGeo-PipeTrack, which utilizes geometric constraints to enable UAV tracking of dynamic pipes. To achieve efficient UAV positioning, YOLOv5 is employed to detect labels on the pipe. The positions are derived from visual geometric constraints based on these detected labels to enable precise localization. To address tracking control challenges, a model predictive control based real-time corrected adaptive PID (MPC-RCAPID) controller is proposed. This controller employs the Recursive Least Squares method for real-time model estimation and integrates rolling horizon optimization to dynamically adjust PID parameters. Experiments conducted on both the AirSim simulator and the Tello UAV platform validate the effectiveness of the proposed framework. Simulation comparisons with standard PID, fractional-order PID, adaptive PID, and fuzzy PID demonstrate that MPC-RCAPID achieves the shortest settling time (3.26 s) and the lowest average tracking error (3.52%). In physical experiments on the Tello platform, compared with standard PID control, MPC-RCAPID reduces the average tracking error by 55.52%, 26.72%, and 26.52% across the three test dimensions. These results demonstrate that VisGeo-PipeTrack facilitates efficient autonomous tracking in dynamic pipe environments.

</details>


### [95] [Resilient Decentralized Radar-LiDAR-Inertial Odometry in Challenging Subterranean Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35363)
*Moumita Mukherjee,Avijit Banerjee,Anton Koval,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出去中心化激光雷达-雷达-惯性里程计（LRIO）框架，结合自适应残差阈值（ART）与鲁棒容错最优信息滤波（RETOIF），在无GPS、烟雾退化地下隧道中实现 resilient 位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无GPS且受烟雾、粉尘、雾等颗粒遮挡的视觉退化环境中，精确定位仍是自主系统的难题。LiDAR分辨率高但在颗粒遮挡下性能下降，FMCW雷达鲁棒但测量稀疏且含离群值，单一传感器均不可靠，因此需融合互补优势并应对传感器退化与间歇故障。

Method: 搭建去中心化LRIO融合框架；核心为自适应残差阈值（ART）机制，自主检测传感器时间退化并选择性调整融合过程以抑制不可靠测量；在此之上采用鲁棒容错最优信息滤波（RETOIF），以故障感知方式在去中心化架构中融合激光-惯性与多路雷达-惯性里程计估计。

Result: 在无GPS地下隧道真实数据上评估：无烟实验中RETOIF的RMSE为2.1733，优于传统集中式LRIO的2.9929，降低27.4%；烟雾退化条件下仍能维持位姿估计。

Conclusion: 该框架在挑战性地下环境中具备韧性自主导航与建图的潜力，可应对环境扰动与间歇性传感器失效。

Abstract: Accurate localization in GPS-denied and visually degraded environments affected by smoke, dust, or fog remains a critical challenge for autonomous systems. While LiDAR provides high-resolution spatial perception, its performance deteriorates under particulate occlusion. In contrast, frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radar offers robustness under such conditions but suffers from sparse measurements and outliers. This article presents a resilient decentralized LiDAR Radar Inertial Odometry (LRIO) framework that fuses the complementary strengths of LiDAR, radar, and inertial sensing to achieve robust pose estimation in challenging subterranean environments. At the core of the framework is an Adaptive Residual Thresholding (ART) mechanism that autonomously detects temporal sensor degradation and selectively adjusts the fusion process to minimize unreliable measurements. Building on this, a Robust Error-Tolerant Optimal Information Filter (RETOIF) is employed within a decentralized fusion architecture to integrate LiDAR-inertial and multiple radar-inertial odometry estimates in a fault-aware manner, enhancing resilience to environmental disturbances and intermittent sensor failures. The proposed framework is evaluated using real-world sensor data collected in GPS-denied subterranean tunnels under smoke-free and smoke-degraded conditions. In the smoke-free experiment, the proposed RETOIF framework achieves a root mean square localization error (RMSE) of 2.1733, compared with 2.9929 for the conventional centralized LRIO approach, corresponding to a 27.4\% reduction in RMSE. Experimental results further demonstrate the framework's ability to maintain pose estimation under smoke-induced sensor degradation, highlighting its potential for resilient autonomous navigation and mapping in challenging subterranean environments.

</details>


### [96] [EmbodiedLab: Benchmarking Embodied Agents That Act to Know](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35385)
*Hongcheng Gao,Zelin Zheng,Alex Zhou,Jay Zhu,Shijia Ge,Yazhe Wang,Xiping Li,Jianshu Zeng,Di Wu,Xuan Shangguan,Yexing Wan,Lingyu He,Yongli Wang,Xiaohan Yu,Zhiqi Jia,Yue Liu,Bryan Hooi,Yinpeng Dong,Wentao Zhang,Sihang Wu,Xiao He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 EmbodiedLab 基准，要求具身智能体"通过行动来认知"：智能体在物理仿真实验室中自主选择流程、操作仪器并用相机读取读数，最终被评估其获得的知识以及所需的物理状态是否达成。在 30 个实验、5 个学科、14 种装置平台上评测六个前沿模型，即使最强智能体在原生控制下成功率也不到一半，失败主要来自与装置的交互环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准存在割裂：操作类基准只到"为改变而行动"（目标状态已给出）；科学发现类基准评"认知"却用符号动作或数值查询替代真实物理实验。缺少一个智能体必须"为了知道而行动"的统一设定。

Method: 构建 EmbodiedLab 基准：采用定量科学实验，每个实例的体系参数重新采样，结果无法预先得知但可精确评分；覆盖 5 个学科共 30 个实验、14 种装置平台。智能体获得目标与装置，自行决定流程，在物理仿真实验室中操作并通过相机读取仪器。评估同时检查智能体获得的知识（基于回合中记录的读数）与任务要求的物理状态，且每个实验均经验证可解。评测六种前沿模型，分别在三种配置下运行：开发者编码代理的原生观测与控制、加入感知与运动规划、以及从最强智能体蒸馏的操作技能。

Result: 原生控制下，即使最强智能体成功率也不足 50%；对每个智能体而言，多数失败发生在与装置的交互环节；操作技能对较弱智能体的帮助大于感知与规划带来的提升。

Conclusion: "为了知道而行动"是一个当前前沿具身智能体尚未解决的开放问题，瓶颈主要在物理交互而非感知或规划；EmbodiedLab 提供可精确评分、可复现的评测平台，基准与评估代码已开源。

Abstract: Embodied agents are expected to accomplish real-world goals by deciding for themselves how and when to interact with their surroundings. For many such goals, changing the physical state of the world is the means rather than the end: the agent changes the state of a system through interaction, observes the feedback, and explores until it either achieves the goal itself (e.g., discovering physical laws) or learns what the goal requires; it acts to know. Manipulation benchmarks stop at acting to change, with the target state given; scientific discovery benchmarks evaluate knowing but replace physical experimentation with symbolic actions or numerical queries. We introduce EmbodiedLab, a benchmark in which a robot must act to know. Quantitative science experiments make this setting controllable: the parameters of the system are resampled for every instance, so the result cannot be known in advance, yet it can be scored exactly. Across 30 experiments in five disciplines on 14 apparatus platforms, the agent receives an objective and the apparatus, chooses its own procedure, and operates a physics-based simulated laboratory, reading the instruments through its cameras. Evaluation checks what the agent came to know, grounded in readings recorded during the episode, as well as the physical state the task requires; every experiment is verified to be solvable. We evaluate six frontier models in their developers' coding agents with native observation and control, with added perception and motion planning, and with manipulation skills distilled from the strongest agents. With native control, even the strongest agent succeeds less than half the time; for every agent, most failures arise in interacting with the apparatus, and manipulation skills help weaker agents more than perception and planning. The benchmark and its evaluation code are available at https://github.com/HexaFuture/EmbodiedLab.

</details>


### [97] [MATE-BT: A Behavior-Tree-Based Framework for Hierarchically Decentralized Mission Autonomy in Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35429)
*Sunbeen Lee,Hoseon Gi,Jik Hur,Inmo Jang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层去中心化框架MATE-BT，将任务执行、分配与行为执行分离，支持领导者接替与机器人故障下的任务连续性，并在30机器人/120目标场景下保持约130ms响应时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人团队执行预定义任务时需应对运行期变化（新目标出现）与机器人（含领导者）故障，现有方法往往需要全局重规划，响应慢且鲁棒性不足。

Method: MATE-BT采用分层去中心化架构：任务层由可接替的主动机载领导者管理任务推进与团队级任务分派；分配层以可互换的去中心化方法求解目标分派，实现插件式替换；执行层各机器人用行为树结合规划与执行自适应本地行为。仅更新受影响层，避免全计划重生成。

Result: Webots仿真与真机实验验证了新目标与机器人故障（含主动领导者故障）下的任务连续性；物理实验中全部计算均机载运行，硬件在环资源测试支持Jetson Orin Nano与Raspberry Pi 5的机载执行；30机器人120目标下响应时间约130ms；四种目标分配插件比较显示任务完成时间与总行驶距离存在场景依赖的权衡。

Conclusion: 分层解耦+行为树+可接替领导者的设计可在有限机载算力下实现故障鲁棒与低延迟的任务管理，分配策略需按场景选择以平衡时效与能耗。

Abstract: Multi-robot teams executing predefined missions must adapt to runtime changes and sustain mission management despite robot failures. This paper proposes MATE-BT (Mission, Allocation, and Task Execution with Behavior Trees), a hierarchically decentralized framework that separates mission execution, allocation, and task execution. An active onboard leader manages mission progression and team-level task assignment, with authority transferable to another leader-capable robot after failure. Interchangeable decentralized methods resolve target assignments, while each robot adapts its local behavior through planning and acting with behavior trees. Webots simulations and real-robot experiments demonstrate mission continuity despite new targets and robot failures, including active-leader failure. In the physical experiments, all MATE-BT computation runs onboard. Hardware-in-the-loop resource measurements further support onboard execution on Jetson Orin Nano and Raspberry Pi 5. By updating only the affected layers without full-plan regeneration, MATE-BT maintained a response time of approximately 130 ms with 30 robots and 120 targets. Comparisons of four target-assignment plugins reveal scenario-dependent trade-offs between mission completion time and total travel distance.

</details>


### [98] [Model-Aware Generation of Safety-Critical Scenes for Robust End-to-End Driving via Distilled Planner Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35448)
*Khaled M. Seyam,Julian Wiederer,Samar Fathalah,Markus Braun,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文通过对NeuroNCAP的失败模式分析发现端到端驾驶模型95.9%的碰撞源于规划而非感知，根源是安全关键交互数据稀缺导致的数据不平衡；作者提出模型感知框架，在BEV层面用可微代理攻击特定规划器并以质量-多样性搜索生成安全关键场景及安全逃逸轨迹，仅渲染50个场景微调即可将两个规划器的闭环碰撞率大致减半（SparseDrive 28.0%→12.2%，DiffusionDrive 37.5%→19.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端驾驶模型在标准基准上表现良好，但在安全关键场景中频繁碰撞。对NeuroNCAP的失败模式分析显示，DiffusionDrive的碰撞中有95.9%属于规划失败而非检测失败，说明瓶颈在规划器而非感知。作者将原因归结为根本性的数据不平衡：真实驾驶日志中安全关键交互极为罕见，规划器几乎从未在此类场景上训练过。因此需要一种能针对特定规划器生成安全关键训练场景的方法来弥补这一缺口。

Method: 提出一种模型感知（model-aware）的框架：对每个规划器，利用从其自身规划头蒸馏出的可微代理，在鸟瞰图（BEV）层面对其进行攻击，从而将昂贵的视频渲染器排除在攻击循环之外。每个规划器提取50个场景，每个场景将一个攻击与一条安全逃逸轨迹配对作为微调标签。采用全自动的质量-多样性（quality-diversity）搜索同时生成攻击与逃逸轨迹，其多样性无需人工指定。仅对这些场景进行渲染以微调规划器，显著降低计算成本。

Result: 在这些场景上微调后，两个规划器在NeuroNCAP上的闭环碰撞率大致减半：SparseDrive从28.0%降至12.2%，DiffusionDrive从37.5%降至19.2%，同时基本保持正常驾驶性能。跨规划器迁移实验表明，收益来自针对特定规划器的定向生成：每个规划器在针对自身生成的场景上提升最大，DiffusionDrive在用SparseDrive场景微调后仅保留了其18.3个百分点碰撞率降幅中的6.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 端到端驾驶的瓶颈在规划器，而非感知；安全关键数据的稀缺可通过模型感知的对抗场景生成高效弥补。仅需少量针对性渲染与微调即可大幅降低闭环碰撞率，且该方法具有规划器特异性——收益不能简单跨规划器迁移，凸显了针对目标规划器定制生成场景的重要性。

Abstract: End-to-end driving models perform well on standard benchmarks but collide frequently in safety-critical scenarios. We present a failure-mode analysis on NeuroNCAP which shows that 95.9% of DiffusionDrive's collisions are planning failures despite reliable detection, pinpointing the bottleneck in planning rather than perception. We attribute this to a fundamental data imbalance, as safety-critical interactions are rare in real driving logs, so planners are hardly ever trained on them. We close this gap with a novel model-aware framework that generates safety-critical training scenes against a specific planner. Each planner is attacked at the bird's-eye-view (BEV) level through a differentiable surrogate distilled from its own planning head. This keeps the expensive video renderer out of the attack loop. We extract 50 scenes per planner, each pairing an attack with a safe escape trajectory that serves as the fine-tuning label. A fully automated quality-diversity search generates both the attacks and the escapes, so that their variety is not specified by hand. Only these scenes are rendered to fine-tune the planner, which reduces the computational cost significantly. Fine-tuning on these scenes roughly halves the closed-loop collision rate of two planners on NeuroNCAP (SparseDrive 28.0% to 12.2%, DiffusionDrive 37.5% to 19.2%) while largely preserving normal driving. A cross-planner transfer experiment shows that the gain comes from targeting the specific planner. Each planner improves most on the scenes generated against itself, and DiffusionDrive fine-tuned on SparseDrive's scenes retains only 6.7 of its 18.3-point collision-rate reduction.

</details>


### [99] [RoKON: Efficient and Adaptive Data-Enabled Predictive Control for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35458)
*Akshun Sharma,Zhongqi Wei,Buqing Ou,Frederike Dümbgen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文扩展了数据驱动预测控制（DeePC）方法，采用基于高斯核的非线性预测器，提出阻尼高斯-牛顿优化方法以支持实时控制，并设计混合缓冲区管理策略融合离线与在线数据，在Franka Research 3机械臂推方块任务中验证了跟踪性能与计算可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动预测控制无需显式学习动力学模型即可构建透明的优化控制问题，但要在真实机器人实时任务中落地仍面临计算效率与在线适应动态变化的挑战，本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 1）沿用基于高斯核的非线性预测器来表示输入与输出数据间的关系；2）提出阻尼高斯-牛顿方法，充分利用控制问题的最小二乘结构实现高效求解；3）引入混合缓冲区管理策略，在保留相关离线数据的同时纳入信息量大的在线数据，使预测器能够适应动力学变化。

Result: 在MuJoCo仿真中使用Franka Research 3机械臂在表面上推动方块，在事先不知道摩擦系数变化的情况下，验证了所提出方法的跟踪性能和计算适用性。

Conclusion: 所提扩展使数据驱动预测控制在无需先验动力学知识的前提下具备实时性与对时变动力学的适应能力，为实时机器人控制任务提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Data-enabled predictive control has recently emerged as a promising control paradigm, enabling a transparent optimal-control formulation without the need to learn an explicit dynamics model. This paper presents a number of extensions paving the path towards its use in real-time robotics tasks. Following recent work, we utilize a nonlinear predictor based on Gaussian kernels to represent the relationship between input and output data. We propose a damped Gauss-Newton method that enables real-time control by efficiently exploiting the least-squares structure of the control problem. In addition, we introduce a hybrid buffer management strategy that incorporates informative online data while retaining relevant offline data, allowing the predictor to account for changes in the dynamics. We demonstrate the tracking performance and computational suitability of the proposed method in MuJoCo using a Franka Research 3 arm pushing a block across surface, without prior knowledge of its changing friction values.

</details>


### [100] [DEX-PHD: A Dexterous Portable Handheld Device for Multimodal Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35462)
*Jooyoung Hong,Noboru Myers,Obin Kwon,Yixian Yang,Joohyung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出便携式手持设备DEX-PHD，将灵巧机械手、逐指连续控制与多模态记录集成于一体，实现兼具精确本体状态采集与便携性的灵巧操作数据收集，并在人形机器人上验证开环回放可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操作数据采集方法存在取舍：基于运动链的遥操作能获得准确的机器人状态，但受限于设备与场景；基于视觉的方法便携易用，却缺乏精确的本体感知。作者希望兼顾两者优势，实现便携且具备精确关节级状态与接触信息的灵巧操作数据采集。

Method: 设计无缆便携的手持设备DEX-PHD，集成灵巧机械手、逐指连续关节级映射控制和多模态记录模块，同步采集本体感知信息、指尖接触数据与腕部相机视频；在需要多指协同的富接触任务上采集演示数据，并将数据以开环回放方式部署到实体人形机器人上进行验证。

Result: 开环回放能够保留手指运动的主要时序模式以及与任务相关的接触信息；设备可在多种环境下完成数据采集，验证了其在富接触、多指协同任务中的实用性。

Conclusion: DEX-PHD为便携式、多模态灵巧操作数据采集提供了一个实用平台，证明了关节级直接映射配合多模态记录可有效支撑演示数据的迁移部署。

Abstract: Existing manipulation data collection methods typically offer either accurate robot-state acquisition through kinematic-chain-based teleoperation or convenient, portable operation through vision-based approaches. We present DEX-PHD, a portable handheld device that combines these advantages for dexterous manipulation by integrating a dexterous robot hand, continuous per-finger control, and multimodal logging. The system records proprioceptive information, fingertip contact data, and wrist-camera video, while the operator directly controls the robot hand through joint-level mapping. Its untethered design enables data collection across diverse environments. We demonstrate DEX-PHD on contact-rich tasks requiring coordinated multi-finger manipulation and deploy the collected demonstrations through open-loop replay on a physical humanoid robot. The results show that open-loop replay preserves the main temporal patterns of finger motion and task-relevant contact. Therefore, DEX-PHD provides a practical platform for portable, multimodal dexterous manipulation data collection.

</details>


### [101] [Enhancing Deep Reinforcement Learning for Robotic Motion Planning via Task Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35494)
*Yuanzhi He,Victor Romero-Cano,Jiayu Xu,Juan David Hernández,Gualtiero Colombo*

Main category: cs.RO
