TL;DR: 提出一种结合迁移学习（仅迁移第一层critic参数）与奖励重设计（分阶段子目标）的DRL任务分解框架，显著提升障碍环境下机器人操作任务的成功率与收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真训练机器人操作策略因安全性与成本优势被广泛采用，但涉及障碍物的任务在深度强化学习下表现欠佳且收敛缓慢，需提升学习效率与鲁棒性。

Method: 构建DRL任务分解框架，融合迁移学习与奖励重设计。TL策略仅在结构相关任务间选择性迁移第一层critic参数（该层捕捉通用状态—动作表示，深层编码奖励与障碍特定的价值几何）；RR将任务分解为分阶段子目标以提供更密集的优化信号。实验基于PyBullet Panda平台，并将最优策略部署于真实Franka Emika FR3机器人做可行性演示。

Result: 障碍设置下性能一致提升：Push with obstacle达0.83±0.05，比最强基线TQC（0.77±0.06）高约7.8%；Slide with obstacle达0.95±0.02，比SAC（0.84±0.06）高约11%成功率。前向迁移使障碍任务收敛时间最多缩短70%；RR将稀疏奖励Pick-and-Place训练从约0.75M步降至0.20M步（约减少73%）。

Conclusion: 选择性浅层critic迁移与阶段化奖励分解可有效改善障碍环境下的DRL操作策略学习，兼顾最终性能与样本效率，并具备向真实机器人迁移的可行性。

Abstract: Training robotic manipulation policies in simulation is widely adopted due to safety and cost efficiency; however, tasks involving obstacles remain under-performing and often converge slowly under Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL). To address this challenge, we propose a task decomposition framework for DRL that integrates Transfer Learning (TL) with Reward Redesign (RR) to improve learning efficiency and robustness in obstacle environments. The TL strategy selectively transfers only the first-layer critic parameters across structurally related tasks, since that layer captures general state--action representations while deeper layers encode reward- and obstacle-specific value geometry; RR decomposes tasks into staged sub-objectives to provide denser optimization signals. Experiments on the PyBullet Panda platform show consistent gains in obstacle settings. On Push with obstacle, our method achieves $0.83 \pm 0.05$, outperforming the strongest baseline (Truncated Quantile Critics (TQC), $0.77 \pm 0.06$) by approximately $7.8\%$. On Slide with obstacle, performance reaches $0.95 \pm 0.02$, exceeding Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) ($0.84 \pm 0.06$) by about $11\%$ success rates. In addition to higher final performance, forward transfer reduces convergence time by up to $70\%$ in obstacle tasks, and RR shortens sparse-reward Pick-and-Place training from roughly $0.75$M to $0.20$M steps ($\approx73\%$ reduction). The best policy is deployed on a real Franka Emika FR3 robot as a qualitative feasibility demonstration.

</details>


### [102] [Preemptive Instruction Following with Resumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35576)
*Zerun Liu,Lechao Cheng,Yimin Liu,Shengeng Tang,Yaxiong Wang,Zhun Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 PIFR 基准，用于评测长时程具身导航中运行时的任务抢占与恢复能力，覆盖单次、重复与嵌套三类中断，并提供分阶段指标与轻量候选排序基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程具身导航常被更高优先级指令打断，智能体需先完成当前原子任务、执行插入任务，再从变化后的环境状态（视角与可行动作候选可能已改变）恢复原序列剩余部分；现有评测缺乏对这一能力的系统衡量，且总体成功率会掩盖中断处理与恢复环节的差异。

Method: 构建 PIFR 基准，设置单次中断、重复中断与嵌套中断等场景，并设计针对插入任务完成、原任务恢复、整条序列完成的分阶段指标；同时提出一种轻量级候选排序基线，用于改善中断后的目标重新定位（target re-grounding）。

Result: 评测显示恢复性能在不同中断条件下存在差异；总体成功率一致时会掩盖中断处理与恢复能力上的差别；所提候选排序基线可改善中断后的目标重定位表现。

Conclusion: PIFR 与基线共同构成研究长时程具身导航中中断处理与任务恢复的系统性框架。

Abstract: Long-horizon embodied navigation may be interrupted by a higher-priority instruction before the current task sequence is complete. The agent must finish its current atomic task, execute the inserted task, and resume the remaining sequence from the updated environment state, where its viewpoint and available navigation candidates may have changed. To evaluate this capability, we introduce PIFR benchmark, a novel task for runtime task preemption and resumption in embodied navigation. PIFR includes single, repeated, and nested interruption settings,with stage-specific metrics for inserted-task completion, resumption of the original task, and completion of the full sequence. Evaluations show that recovery performance varies across interruption conditions, while aggregate success can obscure differences in interruption handling and resumption. We further propose a lightweight candidate-ranking baseline to improve target re-grounding after interruption. Together, PIFR and the baseline provide a systematic framework for studying interruption handling and task recovery in long-horizon embodied navigation.

</details>


### [103] [Distributed Predictive Flocking for a Swarm of Miniature Vibration-Driven Robots in a Confined Arena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35582)
*Prajol Shrestha,Siddhant Mohapatra,Filip Novkoski,Julian Oelhaf,Nicolas Vandewalle,Ana-Sunčana Smith,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分布式预测式集群控制架构，每台微型机器人独立求解NMPC并仅与邻居交换预测轨迹；在1台bristlebot数字孪生上对比6种安全约束形式，发现look-ahead离散时间CBF表现最佳，并在多任务、近几何堆积极限的群体规模下验证了安全性与秩序可同时维持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受限场地内以无领导者、无中央规划、无全局地图的方式协调大量微型机器人是集群机器人核心难题；群体越密集，避碰成为瓶颈，能安全共存的机器人数量取决于机器人间安全约束的建模方式，因此需要系统比较不同安全约束形式。

Method: 采用分布式预测式集群（flocking）控制器架构，每台机器人求解各自的非线性模型预测控制问题，优化集群凝聚与对齐代价、机器人间与障碍物安全以及任务目标，仅与局部邻居交换预测轨迹并实时求解；在该统一架构下，于0.64 m²场地内开源振动驱动bristlebot的数字孪生上对比六种障碍函数/控制障碍函数（CBF）安全约束形式。

Result: look-ahead离散时间CBF综合表现最佳，在几乎所有测试群体规模下碰撞最少、机器人间裕度最大。将其部署于趋光、趋光环绕及无刺激凝聚三类任务，群体规模从10台直至几何堆积极限：小群体碰撞率低，接近堆积极限时碰撞陡增，而协调与凝聚仅平缓退化而非崩溃；局部对齐在所有规模下均保持较高水平，说明群体并未以安全换秩序。

Conclusion: 安全约束的建模方式决定密集群体的可共存规模，look-ahead离散时间CBF是分布式NMPC集群控制中较优的选择；所提架构具备实时性与可扩展性，可在受限场地内同时保持安全性与群体秩序，直至接近几何堆积极限。

Abstract: Coordinating many miniature robots in a confined arena, with no leader, central planner, or global map, is a core challenge for swarm robotics. As the swarm grows denser, collision avoidance becomes the limiting factor, and the number of safely coexisting robots depends on the formulation of the inter-robot safety constraint. We present a distributed predictive flocking controller in which each robot solves its own nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) problem, optimizing swarm cohesion and alignment costs, inter-robot and obstacle safety, and a task objective, while exchanging only predicted trajectories with local neighbors. Within this generalized controller architecture, solved in real time, we compare six barrier and control-barrier-function (CBF) safety formulations on a digital twin of an open-source vibration-driven bristlebot in a $0.64\,\mathrm{m^2}$ arena. A look-ahead discrete-time CBF performs best overall, with the fewest collisions and the widest inter-robot margins across nearly every swarm size tested. We deploy it on phototaxis, phototactic orbiting, and cohesion without a stimulus tasks, using swarm sizes starting from ten robots and up to the geometric packing limit. The swarm holds well: collisions stay low in small swarms, rising steeply as the packing limit approaches, while coordination and cohesion degrade gently rather than collapsing. The swarm is found not to trade safety for order, keeping both across the confined arena, with local alignment high at every swarm size.

</details>


### [104] [RoboDoctor: Training-Free Recursive Self-Improvement for Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35604)
*Heyang Yu,Haomin Li,Sihang Li,Jixin Yan,Shiyun Peng,Wanqi Zhong,Tianyu Xin,Yuan Yao,Xiaoyu Tian,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboDoctor是一个免训练框架，将机器人策略与语言模型智能体结合，在执行阶段通过诊断、接地动作和持久化经验实现适应性调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略与语言模型智能体能力互补，但完成任务还需根据物理交互的后果调整二者的使用方式；作者将"执行"视为适应的核心场所：反馈既引导当前动作，也为在固定模型权重下修正后续决策提供证据。

Method: 提出RoboDoctor免训练框架，连接诊断、接地动作与持久经验，覆盖三类修正目标：harness在策略与智能体之间分配控制权；intervention决定何时介入辅助并保留策略的有用行为；operation依据观测效果改进物理方法。关联的经验记录保存决策理由、尝试方法、结果及复用条件。

Result: 在54条RoboDojo-Sim任务条目构成的精选档案上，RoboDoctor获得五维平均分57.10，原生成功率49.44%；记录的交互展示了策略切换、选择性纠正与抓取修正等现象。

Conclusion: 结果刻画了所评估系统中的执行期适应能力；仍需受控实验以分离跨回合经验的独立贡献。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies and language-model agents provide complementary capabilities, but completing a task also requires adapting their use to the consequences of physical interaction. We study execution as a locus of adaptation: feedback guides the current action and supplies evidence for revising subsequent decisions under fixed model weights. We introduce RoboDoctor, a training-free framework that connects diagnosis, grounded action, and persistent experience across three revision targets. The harness allocates control between policy and agent; intervention determines when to assist while preserving useful policy behavior; and operation refines physical methods through observed effects. Linked experience records retain the reason for a decision, the attempted method, its outcome, and conditions for reuse. On a curated archive of 54 RoboDojo-Sim task entries, RoboDoctor achieves a five-dimension mean score of 57.10, with a native success rate of 49.44%. Recorded interactions illustrate strategy changes, selective correction, and grasp revision. These findings characterize execution-time adaptation in the evaluated system; controlled trials remain necessary to isolate the contribution of cross-episode experience.

</details>


### [105] [Context-Aware Generation and Modulation of Expressive Motion Behavior using Multimodal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35615)
*Till Hielscher,Fabio Scaparro,Kai O. Arras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种从多模态（文本、音频、视频）上下文描述自动生成并调制复杂全身表达性手势序列的方法：基于动画原理与动态运动基元（DMP）的统一数学实现，结合多模态基础模型生成参数化动作变体，并通过持续控制框架与知识蒸馏提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表达性机器人动作能改善用户参与度、亲和度与任务表现，但自动化生成并灵活调制复杂全身手势仍是难题；现有方法在灵活性、通用性、可解释性与跨形态适用性上存在不足。

Method: 用动态运动基元统一实现动画原理的数学表达；用多模态基础模型从文本/音频/视频上下文生成动作序列及参数化变体；方法具备可解释、可组合、跨机器人形态特性；将系统集成到连续控制框架中，并用知识蒸馏训练更小的模型以降低延迟、提高token效率。

Result: 用户研究（类人平台）表明，相较于非调制条件或API级条件，参与者认为该系统生成的动作与交互上下文更契合；同时展示了系统能为不同运动学结构的机器人生成表达性动作，体现其通用性。

Conclusion: 该工作将动画原理、DMP与多模态基础模型结合，实现了上下文感知、可解释、可组合且跨形态的表达性全身动作生成与调制，并通过蒸馏提升实际部署效率。

Abstract: Expressive robot motion positively impacts human-robot interaction by improving user engagement, likability, or task performance. We present a novel approach to automatically generate and modulate complex, expressive full-body gesture sequences from a multimodal (text, audio, video) context description. Based on a unified mathematical implementation of the Principles of Animation using Dynamic Movement Primitives, we use multimodal foundation models to generate such sequences along with parametric motion variations that are highly context-aware. This method extends the state of the art in terms of flexibility and generality by being interpretable, composable, and working across different robot morphologies. Moreover, we integrate the system into a continuous control framework and leverage knowledge distillation to learn a much smaller model, significantly improving token efficiency and system latency. Results from a user study with a human-like platform indicate that participants judged our system's motions to be better aligned with the interaction context than motions produced under a non-modulated or API-level condition. We also demonstrate how the system generates expressive motion for robots with different kinematics, showcasing its versatility. Paper webpage: https://gen-mod-expressive-motion-behavior.github.io/.

</details>


### [106] [Robot-Aligned 3D Gaussian Splatting for Precise Reaching with Wrist-Camera VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35626)
*Zuoxu Wang,Xiao Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的腕部相机VLA数据生成管线，用于电子废弃物拆解中的预接触精准reach，仅用1500条生成轨迹即达80.0%成功率，超过150条遥操作示教的58.7%，人工采集时间不足10分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略学习受限于机器人数据采集瓶颈，尤其在电子废弃物拆解场景：零件密集、外观相似、薄壁反光且机械结构复杂，难以构建精确的数字孪生仿真与接触模型，导致难以获得高质量的腕部相机-动作对齐数据。

Method: 用3DGS重建真实工作空间，从机器人对齐位姿渲染多样化腕部相机观测；将这些观测与平滑、结构化的reach轨迹配对，构成机器人对齐的视觉-动作监督用于VLA微调；同时在VLA策略上增加指令条件化的reach验证器（instruction-conditioned reach verifier），估计目标接近度并在物理接触前可靠终止。

Result: 在主板拆解任务上，仅用腕部相机（无需外部侧视相机）实现精准reach。以π0.5为基座，1500条生成轨迹在三类目标上达到80.0%的精准reach成功率，而150条遥操作示教为58.7%，同时主动人工采集时间少于10分钟。

Conclusion: 3DGS渲染数据可有效替代/补充真实遥操作数据，用于腕部相机VLA的预接触reach适配，在难以仿真的复杂装配场景中显著降低数据采集成本并提升成功率。

Abstract: Efficient robotic data collection remains a major bottleneck for vision-language-action (VLA) policy learning, especially for tasks requiring accurate end-effector positioning and camera-action alignment. This challenge is particularly severe in electronic-waste disassembly, where robots operate in cluttered assemblies of densely packed, visually similar components. Full digital-twin simulation is difficult because many parts are thin, reflective, or mechanically complex, making accurate object-level meshes and contact models difficult to obtain. In this work, we focus on pre-contact reaching and propose a 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)-based data generation pipeline for wrist-camera VLA adaptation. Our method reconstructs the real workspace with 3DGS and renders diverse wrist-camera observations from robot-aligned poses. These observations are paired with smooth, structured reaching trajectories to provide robot-aligned visual-action supervision for VLA fine-tuning. We further augment the VLA policy with an instruction-conditioned reach verifier that estimates target proximity and provides reliable termination before physical interaction. Experiments in motherboard disassembly demonstrate precise VLA-based reaching using wrist-camera observations alone, without an external side-view camera. With $π$0.5, 1,500 generated trajectories achieve 80.0\% precise-reaching success across three targets, compared with 58.7\% using 150 teleoperated demonstrations, while requiring less than 10 minutes of active human capture. Project website: https://001-wang.github.io/PrecisionReach-GS/

</details>


### [107] [From Language to Drives: A Multimodal Framework for Aligning Autonomous Robots with Human Purposes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35629)
*Diana Cerdas-Vargas,Alejandro Romero,Richard J. Duro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出用大语言模型（LLM）与视觉语言模型（VLM）作为"文化使能器"，通过多模态交互式对齐流程，将人类以自然语言表达的目的转化为开放式学习自主机器人的任务（mission）与内驱力（drive），从而使其行为与人类意图保持一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式学习的自主机器人能够自行生成技能与目标，但缺乏确保这些目标与人类用户意图保持相关和一致的机制，因此需要一种能引入人类文化知识与目的的对接手段。

Method: 利用LLM/VLM中蕴含的通用与文化知识，构建三层架构：语义对齐层、任务生成层、内驱力建模层；形成双向交互循环——人类输入定义高层目的，机器人借助LLM/VLM传递的多模态信息将其扩展为可执行任务，并通过反馈持续维持与人类意图的对齐。

Result: 在仿真环境与实体机器人上的实验表明，该流程能够生成有效的任务与行为，在自主机器人的交互场景中支持有效的人-智能体对齐。

Conclusion: 将LLM/VLM作为文化中介的多模态交互式对齐框架，可有效把自然语言表达的人类目的落地为机器人架构中的动机组件，为实现开放式学习自主机器人的人机对齐提供可行路径。

Abstract: This work explores a multimodal approach to the alignment of the behavior of open-ended learning autonomous robots with human purposes through the use of large language models as cultural enablers. While autonomous robots can generate their own skills and goals through interaction with the environment, they often lack mechanisms to ensure that these remain relevant and aligned to those of human users. The proposed approach makes use of the general and cultural knowledge contained in LLMs and VLMs to establish a multimodal interactive alignment process in which human purposes, expressed in natural language, are transformed into motivational components of a robot architecture in the form of robot missions and drives that guide the behavior of the robot. The system is structured into three layers for semantic alignment, mission generation, and drive modeling. This design enables a bidirectional interaction loop: human inputs define high-level purposes, robots expand them into executable missions making use of multimodal information relayed by LLMs and VLMs, and feedback maintains alignment with human intent. Experiments in simulation and with a physical robot demonstrate that the process produces valid missions and behaviors, supporting effective human-agent alignment in interactive scenarios with autonomous robots.

</details>


### [108] [Revisiting Direct Control: A Field Evaluation of Sensor Fusion Efficacy in Teleoperating Full-Scale Autonomous Vehicles via Bird's Eye View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35630)
*Stanisław Kuczma,Sławomir K. Tadeja,Michał R. Nowicki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过真实车辆实地实验（N=18）比较摄像头、原始LiDAR点云BEV与LiDAR-相机融合BEV三种遥操作可视化界面，发现融合BEV在可用性、认知负荷和任务完成时间上均最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中机器人遥操作是关键安全兜底机制，需操作员在系统失效时直接接管。既有研究多聚焦高层监督控制或仿真实验，难以反映驾驶全尺寸实车时的物理动态与感官需求，因此需要真实车辆场景下对空间数据表征方式的实证评估。

Method: 在实地实验中采用真实汽车尺寸车辆，招募18名参与者，对比三种可视化界面：(i) 标准摄像头画面基线；(ii) 原始LiDAR点云的鸟瞰图（BEV）；(iii) LiDAR与相机数据流融合的BEV。结合客观绩效数据（任务完成时间）与主观评估（感知可用性、NASA-TLX认知任务负荷、SUS系统可用性量表）进行综合分析。

Result: 与既往被动观察研究认为融合BEV可能损害态势感知的结论相反，主动控制结果显示融合BEV在感知可用性（p ≤ 0.0001）和认知任务负荷（p = 0.01，NASA-TLX为34.58/100）上显著优于标准LiDAR BEV，也显著优于纯摄像头视图（p = 0.043）；同时任务完成时间显著缩短（p = 0.0461），可用性最高（SUS 77.9/100）。

Conclusion: BEV传感器融合在最大化安全裕度的同时最小化操作员心理需求，挑战了现有遥操作设计惯例，为稳健远程驾驶系统提供了经实证验证的设计方案。

Abstract: In autonomous driving, robot teleoperation serves as a critical safety fallback mechanism, requiring operators to assume direct control in the event of system failure. While recent research has pivoted toward high-level supervisory control or simulation-based studies, these approaches often fail to capture the physical dynamics and sensory demands of driving a full-scale vehicle. To address this gap, we evaluated the impact of spatial data representation on operator performance using a real car-sized vehicle in a field experiment with $N = 18$ participants. We compared three visualization interfaces: (i) a standard camera feed baseline, with two bird's-eye views (BEV) from (ii) a raw LiDAR point cloud, and (iii) a sensor fusion of LiDAR and camera data streams. Contrary to prior passive observation studies suggesting that sensor-fusion BEV may hinder awareness, our active-control results demonstrate that such representation significantly outperforms the standard LiDAR BEV in perceived usability ($p \leq 0.0001$) and cognitive task load ($p = 0.01$, NASA-TLX score: 34.58/100) while also being significantly better than the Camera-only view ($p = 0.043$). The fused interface also led to statistically significant reductions in task completion time ($p = 0.0461$) and superior usability (SUS: 77.9/100) compared to those of the Camera-only and LiDAR-only views. By synthesizing objective performance data with comprehensive subjective assessments, we demonstrate that BEV fusion maximizes safety margins while minimizing the operator's mental demand. These findings challenge existing teleoperation conventions and provide an empirically validated approach for designing robust remote piloting systems.

</details>


### [109] [VPR-Space: A Benchmark for Visual Place Recognition in Space Analog Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35642)
*Richard Fechner,Peer Neubert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 VPR-Space 基准，用于评估视觉位置识别（VPR）在地外（月球/火星）表面场景下的能力，包含16个基于太空模拟场地的数据库-查询数据集，并提出以 SLAM 下游任务价值为导向的真值生成方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VPR 与 SLAM 方法在城市和特征丰富的自然环境中表现优异，但在地外表面这类特征稀少、范围广阔的环境中面临严峻挑战，而目前缺乏针对该类场景的 VPR 评测基准。

Method: 1）基于太空模拟场地（SAD，即类似月球/火星属性的地球环境）现有数据构建16个 VPR 数据库-查询数据集，涵盖环境、视角变化与光照条件等多类挑战；2）针对 VPR 问题的病态性，提出新的真值生成方法：不采用"位姿在固定位置与视角阈值内即视为同一地点"的判定，而是依据该匹配对下游 SLAM 任务是否有价值来判定，并通过一组先进相对位姿估计器（RPE）的集成性能生成 VPR 真值矩阵，从而避免在 SLAM 系统中进行闭环评测的开销与限制。

Result: 在新真值下，VPR 方法需准确识别出后续 RPE 能为 SLAM 提供有价值位姿估计的图像对。对九种先进 VPR 方法的评测显示仍有较大改进空间。

Conclusion: VPR-Space 填补了地外表面 VPR 评测基准的空白，并揭示了现有方法在该场景下的不足，为未来研究指明了方向；数据集预发布版本已在 GitHub 公开。

Abstract: Visual Place Recognition (VPR) is an important means for loop closure detection in visual Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM). While state-of-the-art VPR and SLAM approaches excel in urban and feature-rich natural environments, the vast and feature-poor environments of extraterrestrial surfaces like on the Moon or Mars pose severe challenges for existing approaches. This paper presents a novel benchmark, VPR-Space, to evaluate VPR capabilities on extraterrestrial surfaces. There are three contributions: (1) We collected sixteen VPR database-query datasets based on existing data from Space Analog Domains (SAD), terrestrial environments that resemble properties of the Moon or Mars. The collection includes a range of challenges regarding environment, viewpoint change, and illumination conditions. (2) To address the ill-posed nature of VPR, we propose a novel approach to generate the ground-truth information in VPR benchmarks. Instead of defining that a query and datbase image show the same place if and only if their camera poses are within fixed position and viewing direction thresholds, we propose to make the decision dependent on whether the match would be valuable in the downstream SLAM task. To avoid the overhead and restrictions of evaluating VPR in-the-loop within SLAM systems, we provide VPR ground-truth (GT) matrices based on the performance of an ensemble of state-of-the-art Relative Pose Estimators (RPE). Under this GT we require a VPR method to identify exactly those image pairs for which a subsequently applied RPE is expected to provide a valuable pose estimate in a SLAM context. (3) We evaluated nine state-of-the-art VPR approaches. The results show considerable room for improvement and indicate directions for future research. We provide a pre-release version of the dataset on github: $\texttt{https://github.com/rfechner/VPR-Space}$

</details>


### [110] [Environment Edits as a Corrective Signal for Reward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35659)
*Isaac Ngui,Courtney McBeth,Nancy M. Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出“环境编辑”作为一种补充反馈形式：保持机器人运动不变、由人直接编辑场景，从而把反馈解释唯一归因于被移动的物体；并提出 FERRET 方法从机器人修正与环境编辑轨迹中学习偏好，实验表明环境编辑能消解特征歧义并学到仅靠机器人修正无法学到的偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人修正虽能让用户调整机器人行为，但一次修正会同时改变多个特征，存在归因歧义；此外，关于物体属性以及物体之间关系的偏好无法通过移动机器人来表达。

Method: 以环境编辑作为互补反馈：编辑时保持机器人运动恒定，使编辑的唯一解释是被移动的物体；并据此提出 FERRET（Feature Learning from Environmental and Robot-Relational Edit Traces），统一从机器人修正轨迹与环境编辑轨迹中学习偏好。

Result: 实验表明，环境编辑能够消解特征之间的歧义，并学到仅凭机器人修正无法学习到的偏好。

Conclusion: 环境编辑是机器人修正的有效补充，将两类修正结合学习可提升偏好学习的可辨识性与覆盖范围。

Abstract: Robot corrections allow people to adjust a robot's behavior, but a single correction changes many features. Also, preferences about object attributes and relationships between objects cannot be expressed by moving the robot. We propose environment edits as a complementary form of feedback. The robot's motion is held constant as the human edits the scene, so the only explanation for the edit is the moved object. We introduce Feature Learning from Environmental and Robot-Relational Edit Traces (FERRET), which learns preferences from both robot and environment corrections. We show that environment edits are able to disambiguate features and learn preferences that robot corrections alone cannot.

</details>


### [111] [Continuous-Time Safe Planning and Control for Agile Quadrupeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35670)
*Gage MacLin,Mason Peterson,Camilla Tabasso,Venanzio Cichella,Joshua Migdal,Tye Brady,Taskin Padir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种实时四足机器人运动规划框架，保留完整的SE(2)全驱动模型与各向异性、航向耦合的速度约束，用复合Bernstein多项式参数化轨迹，借助凸包性质在整段规划域上严格证明无碰撞与输入有界，在仿真与硬件实验中验证了动态环境下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中的敏捷四足导航需要轨迹既动力学可行又可证明无碰撞。现有实时规划器多采用凸化简化，依赖简化运动学模型，丢弃了机器人实际可执行的运动能力；且仅在离散点上认证安全性，存在采样点之间与移动障碍物碰撞的隐患。

Method: 保留四足机器人的全驱动SE(2)模型及其各向异性、航向耦合的速度边界，不回避由此产生的非凸性；将轨迹参数化为复合Bernstein多项式，利用Bernstein多项式的凸包性质，在每个规划时域的连续全域上认证避障与输入约束；以在线重规划频率闭环求解该优化，向步态/运动控制器下发机体速度指令。

Result: 在仿真中与消融版本及外部基线方法进行了对比验证；在硬件实验中，四足机器人在未知移动障碍物之间导航，成功恢复了简化模型所排除的横向运动与航向耦合运动。

Conclusion: 结果表明，放弃凸化、直接在完整非凸模型上进行实时求解是可行的，复合Bernstein多项式提供了一种兼顾计算实时性与连续时间安全性认证的有效手段，可提升四足机器人在动态环境中的敏捷导航能力。

Abstract: Agile quadruped navigation in dynamic environments demands trajectories that are both dynamically feasible and provably collision-free. Most real-time planners achieve tractability through a convex reduction that requires simplified kinematic models, discarding motion that the robot can readily execute, and certify safety only at discretization points, leaving open possible inter-sample collisions with moving obstacles. We present a real-time motion planning framework that retains the fully-actuated $SE(2)$ model of a quadruped, with its anisotropic, heading-coupled velocity bounds, and accepts the resulting nonconvexity. By parameterizing trajectories as composite Bernstein polynomials, we exploit the convex hull property to certify collision avoidance and the input bounds over the entirety of each planning horizon. Solved at online replanning rates, the optimization runs in closed loop, commanding body velocities to the locomotion controller. We validate the approach in simulation against ablations and external baselines, and in hardware experiments on a quadruped navigating among unknown moving obstacles, recovering both lateral and heading-coupled motion that simplified models exclude.

</details>


### [112] [FISRF: Flexible Instructional Scaffolding Reflection Framework for Robotics Task Planning with Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35686)
*Qingmiao Liang,Jiatao Zhang,Runxin Zou,Yufan Song,Wei Song,Yuhua Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FISRF框架，通过智能体—导师架构估计机器人在任务规划中的最近发展区，自适应选择反思策略与导师指导，减少错误经验积累并提升纠错效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被广泛用于任务规划中的反思式纠错，但现有方法主要依赖自主探索，在与新环境交互的早期阶段容易积累错误经验，影响纠错效果。

Method: FISRF采用智能体—导师（agent–mentor）架构，借鉴教育心理学中的最近发展区（ZPD）概念，在任务规划过程中估计机器人的ZPD，并据此自适应地选择反思策略与导师指导强度。

Result: 在ALFWorld基准和真实机器人平台上的实验表明，FISRF将平均纠错轮数减少37%，冗余推理减少25.1%，整体任务成功率提升8%。

Conclusion: 引入ZPD的自适应指导机制可有效帮助规划智能体积累高质量纠错经验，在仿真与真实平台上同时提升效率与成功率。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely adopted for reflective error correction in task planning; however, existing approaches primarily rely on autonomous exploration, rendering them prone to accumulating erroneous experiences during early interactions with new environments. To address this limitation, we propose the Flexible Instructional Scaffolding Reflection Framework (FISRF), an agent--mentor architecture that estimates the robot's Zone of Proximal Development (ZPD) in task planning and adaptively selects reflection strategies and mentor guidance, thereby facilitating the accumulation of effective error-correction experience. Experiments in ALFWorld and on real-world robotic platforms demonstrate that FISRF reduces the average number of error-correction rounds by 37% and redundant reasoning by 25.1%, while improving the overall task success rate by 8%. Project webpage : https://fisrfplanning.github.io/

</details>


### [113] [TrySee: Modular Perception-Enhanced VLM Grounding for Closed-Loop Robot Task Planning in Specialized Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35687)
*Elias Hofele,Thomas Trapp,David Timmermann,Tristan Schnell,Georg Heppner,Arne Roennau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 TrySee，一个模块化闭环机器人任务规划流水线，将通用 VLM 推理与领域专用感知模块及成熟机器人组件结合，无需 VLM 微调或端到端示教即可在多个专业领域实现在线落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 在机器人高层推理与任务规划上表现优异，但在专业/特定领域中的图像理解与视觉 grounding 能力不足，而这类领域又缺乏充足的领域训练数据，难以对 VLM 进行微调或采集端到端示教。

Method: 构建 TrySee 模块化闭环规划流水线：采用固有反应式的循环式（cyclic）规划结构，引入领域专用的模块化视觉预处理以提供在线 grounding，并设计动态预处理选择机制以提升可扩展性；系统通过可替换的感知模块与运动规划组件实现自适应，不依赖 VLM 微调或端到端示教。

Result: 在 5 个差异显著的领域任务上进行了评估，其中包括真实机器人拆解任务；在棕地（brownfield）环境中的现场测试表明，该方案能够在领域训练数据有限的情况下把 VLM 规划有效集成进专业机器人系统，具有实用价值。

Conclusion: 将通用 VLM 推理与可替换的领域感知模块解耦组合，是让 VLM 规划能力落地于数据稀缺的专业机器人场景的可行路径，且闭环反馈与动态预处理选择有助于系统的鲁棒性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have shown impressive capabilities in high-level reasoning and task planning for robots. However, specialized domains remain a challenge for VLMs' image interpretation and grounding abilities. We introduce TrySee, a modular closed-loop robot task planning pipeline integrating general-purpose VLM reasoning with domain-specific perception and established robotics components. The system consists of an inherently reactive cyclic planning pipeline, augmented with domain-specific modular vision preprocessing, to provide online grounding. Finally, a dynamic preprocessing selection mechanism contributes to the scalability of the pipeline. The system requires neither VLM fine-tuning nor end-to-end demonstrations and adapts through replaceable perception modules and motion planning. The approach is evaluated on planning tasks across 5 widely different domains, including a real-world robot disassembly task. The field tests in a brownfield environment demonstrate the approach's practicality in integrating VLM planning into specialized robotic systems with limited domain-specific training data.

</details>


### [114] [SPECOLA: A Tool for Specification Driven Robot Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35730)
*Immanuel Ampomah Mensah,Hadas Kress-Gazit*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Specola，一个基于信号时序逻辑（STL）的机器人仿真行为评估工具，将仿真器状态映射为规格相关信号并实时生成、管理 STL 监视器，从而给出比成功率更细致的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真基准多以成功率作为主导性能指标，但成功率只反映任务是否完成，无法说明成功裕度有多大、成功对目标参数变化的敏感性、可能违反的约束，以及成功是否持续。

Method: Specola 提供在仿真器信号上编写规格的接口，在策略执行过程中自动创建并管理对应监视器；提供 Python API 与命令行接口以便集成到既有策略执行循环，并提供适配器接口以接入新仿真器。

Result: 已实现九个仿真环境与基准的适配器，包括 LIBERO、RoboCasa、Isaac Lab 和 RoboDojo；并通过案例研究展示了该工具所支持的多维度细粒度评估能力。

Conclusion: 以 STL 规格驱动的监视可补充成功率指标的不足，Specola 为机器人仿真评估提供了可复用的工具与适配生态，代码与适配器已开源。

Abstract: Simulation benchmarks are a useful way to measure progress in robotics; typically they provide success rate as the dominant measure of performance. However, success rate reports only whether a rollout completed the task, hiding by what margin it succeeded, how sensitive the success is to goal parameter changes, requirements it may have violated, and whether success persisted. Signal Temporal Logic (STL) provides a formal language for capturing specifications; it can be used to define requirements, and measure how close a robot behavior is to satisfying or violating them. Using STL monitors in a simulator, however, requires exposing simulator state and mapping it to specification-relevant signals. We present Specola, a tool for evaluating robot behaviors in simulation with respect to STL specifications. Specola provides an interface for writing specifications over simulator signals, and creates and manages the relevant monitors while a policy executes. We provide a Python API and command-line interface for integrating monitoring into existing policy execution loops. We provide an adapter interface for connecting new simulators, and provide adapters for nine simulation environments and benchmarks, including LIBERO, RoboCasa, Isaac Lab, and RoboDojo. We provide case studies that illustrate the nuanced evaluation that Specola enables. Code and adapters are available at https://github.com/specola-eval/specola.

</details>


### [115] [Pretraining Knows What, Simulation Teaches How: Compositional Generalization in a Sim-Trained Robot Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35817)
*Benjamin Pikus,Daniel Saunders,Mircea Mironenco,Salman Mohammadi,Mathew Mathew,Sirou Zhu,Divya Makkar,Xuhua Huang,Qinbo Li,Supreeth Narasimhaswamy,Siyuan Cen,Thomas Neff,Dejia Xu,Shyamal Buch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ZAP 通过轻量工具接口让 27B 现成视觉语言模型控制机器人，训练全程不使用真实机器人数据，真实任务成功率达 96.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略需同时具备"理解场景中的任务意图"与"生成可执行动作"两类能力，而现有 VLA 模型与调用大模型的方式在成本、速度和泛化上存在权衡。

Method: 提出 Zero-real Agent Policy (ZAP)：以预训练 27B 视觉语言模型为基础，通过简单的工具调用框架（tool harness）实现机器人控制；先用该模型引导世界理解与推理，再在简单仿真任务上经监督微调与 GRPO 强化学习教会动作控制。训练中不含任何真实机器人数据。

Result: 真实世界任务成功率 96.7%，与 GPT-6 Astra 持平，显著超过 MolmoAct2 VLA（33.3%），同时速度快 3 倍、成本低 17 倍；可泛化到全新物体、目标、策略与语言指令（试管入架并换孔、双臂空中交接试管、井字棋画 X、下国际象棋），这些均不出现在训练数据中；在未见过的 Franka 单臂本体上于 RoboLab-120 达到 55.9%，领先最强 VLA/WAM 基线 4.9 个百分点。

Conclusion: 小型现成视觉语言模型配合工具式接口与纯仿真训练，即可获得高性能、低成本且跨任务、跨本体的机器人操作策略，具备向零真实数据部署推广的潜力。

Abstract: A robot policy needs to understand what a task is asking for in a scene, and how to move the robot to accomplish that task. To achieve these two skills, we introduce Zero-real Agent Policy (ZAP), a recipe that teaches a small off-the-shelf vision-language model to control a robot via a simple tool harness. We use a pretrained 27B parameter model to bootstrap world understanding and reasoning, followed by supervised fine-tuning and GRPO on simple simulated tasks to teach it how to move the robot. Without any real-world robot data during training, ZAP reaches 96.7% success on real-world tasks, matching GPT-6 Astra in our harness and outperforming the MolmoAct2 VLA (33.3%), while running 3x faster and 17x cheaper than GPT-6 Astra. We demonstrate that ZAP also generalizes to completely new objects, goals, strategies, and language by seating a test tube in a rack (and choosing a different hole in the rack when prompted), handing a test tube between arms mid-air, drawing an X on a tic-tac-toe board, and playing chess. These goals and types of manipulation don't appear in our training data. Furthermore, ZAP also transfers to an unseen embodiment. On a single-arm Franka, ZAP reaches 55.9% on RoboLab-120, 4.9 percentage points ahead of the leading VLA/WAM baseline.

</details>


### [116] [Profiling a Frontier Agent with EBench: From Direct Control to Harness with Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35832)
*Yifei Yao,Yude Zou,Guanbo Jiao,Xing Gao,Ning Gao,Yukai Wang,Jiantong Chen,Zihou Zhu,Xinyu Li,Ningyu Yan,Shuai Wang,Jianming Ma,Haoyu Guo,Weinan Zhang,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该报告在EBench上评测前沿多模态大模型GPT-6-Astra作为机器人智能体，比较其"直接控制"与"调用后训练VLA/WAM策略"两种配置与后训练策略的能力差异与互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿MLLM无需机器人专项训练即可控制机器人，但其与后训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）及世界动作模型（WAM）策略在不同操作需求下的能力对比与交互机制尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 在EBench上，将GPT-6-Astra置于agent harness中，采用与后训练策略相同的视觉与本体感知观测协议，研究两种配置：直接生成路点命令的"直接控制"，以及可额外调用后训练VLA或WAM执行动作的"harness with policy"。

Result: 直接控制下整体性能与后训练策略相当，在短时程任务上领先，跨泛化维度性能波动最小；但在长时程任务、桌面操作和高精度接触密集任务上落后。harness with policy可融合双方互补优势，整体成功率提升至最强对比后训练策略水平并大幅降低token开销。

Conclusion: 混合配置仍低于各单独组件的逐任务上界，原因在于策略调用次优、固定指令执行缺少子目标、以及智能体恢复控制时的抓取干扰；报告刻画了前沿MLLM智能体的能力边界，并为未来agent harness设计提供改进方向。

Abstract: Frontier multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can control robots without robot-specific training, but how their capabilities compare and interact with post-trained vision-language-action (VLA) and world action model (WAM) policies across manipulation demands remains to be thoroughly evaluated. In this report, we evaluate GPT-6-Astra on EBench as the core of an agent that controls a robot through an agent harness under the same visual and proprioceptive observation protocol as post-trained policies. We study two agent configurations: direct control, in which the agent generates waypoint commands directly, and harness with policy, in which it can also invoke a post-trained VLA or WAM policy for action execution. Under direct control, the agent achieves competitive overall performance relative to post-trained policies. It leads on short-horizon tasks and exhibits the least performance variation across generalization dimensions, whereas it underperforms post-trained policies on long-horizon tasks, tabletop manipulation, and high-precision contact-rich tasks. In the harness with policy, invoking a post-trained VLA or WAM combines the complementary strengths of both components, raising overall task success to the level of the strongest compared post-trained policies while substantially reducing token cost. However, performance remains below the per-task upper bound of the standalone components due to suboptimal policy invocation, fixed-instruction execution without subgoals, and grasp disruption when the agent resumes control. Overall, our study characterizes the capability boundary of a frontier MLLM agent and suggests directions for improving future agent harness design. Project page: https://internrobotics.github.io/EBench-Astra-Tech-Report/

</details>


### [117] [Differentiable Contact Dynamics for Legged Locomotion: Policy Learning and Contact-Implicit Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35852)
*Gijeong Kim,Seungbin Oh,Dongyun Kang,Hae-Won Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于摩擦接触的平滑解析梯度，仅在反向传播中替换精确接触灵敏度，使梯度在接触模式切换时连续，用于足式机器人策略学习与接触隐式轨迹优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确接触灵敏度虽可闭式求得，但只描述当前接触模式内的变化，无法为相邻接触模式提供一阶信息，导致接触模式切换处梯度不连续，难以支撑策略学习与轨迹优化。

Method: 对 de Saxcé 修正的库仑摩擦二阶锥互补问题做中心路径松弛，平滑刻画分离、夹紧、滑动三种模式的定义条件；松弛灵敏度在由硬接触解初始化、经牛顿迭代得到的中心路径点上求值；仅替换反向传播中的灵敏度，前向硬接触求解不变，保证 rollout 可行。

Result: 灵敏度在接触模式切换过程中保持连续，并随松弛趋于零收敛到精确灵敏度；仅用 10 万样本、桌面 PC 上不到 2 分钟训练，策略即可零微调迁移到硬件，trot 策略可抗推挤并穿越碎石与湿滑地面；轨迹优化中可找到精确梯度与 Signorini 松弛梯度遗漏的接触丰富解。

Conclusion: 通过在反向传播中引入中心路径松弛的平滑接触灵敏度，可在保持前向物理可行性的同时获得跨接触模式的可用梯度，是一种轻量且可迁移到真实硬件的接触隐式优化与策略学习方法。

Abstract: This paper proposes a smooth analytic gradient for frictional contact and applies it to policy learning and contact-implicit trajectory optimization for legged locomotion. The exact contact sensitivity is available in closed form but describes changes within the current contact mode, providing no first-order information toward neighboring contact modes. The proposed gradient replaces this sensitivity only in the backward pass, leaving the hard-contact forward solve unchanged and the realized rollout feasible. A central-path relaxation of the de Saxcé-corrected second-order-cone complementarity problem for Coulomb friction smooths the conditions defining the separating, clamping, and sliding modes. The relaxed sensitivity is evaluated at a point on the central path obtained by a Newton iteration initialized from the hard-contact solution. The sensitivity remains continuous through contact-mode transitions and converges to the exact sensitivity as the relaxation vanishes. Using 100,000 samples and less than 2 min of training on a desktop PC, policy learning with the proposed gradient produces a policy that transfers to hardware without fine-tuning. On hardware, the robot under a trotting policy withstands pushes and traverses debris and slippery ground. In trajectory optimization, the proposed gradient reaches contact-rich solutions missed by exact and Signorini-relaxed gradients. Project page: https://diffcontact.github.io

</details>


### [118] [Learning Force-Reactive Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35862)
*Sihang Li,Xiaofeng Guo,Jinkun Cao,Charlie Cheng,Ruihan Yang,Chen Feng,Guanya Shi,Haozhi Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FLORE 是一个用于学习"力反应式"人形机器人移动-操作（loco-manipulation）的框架：用两台力感知 UMI 风格手持夹爪加可穿戴追踪器采集无机器人演示，训练以视觉、本体感知和测得力/力矩为输入的力反应扩散策略，输出动作与每手力指令，再由带力跟踪的混合柔顺全身控制器执行并保持平衡；真机三类富接触任务成功率 80%，位置-only 基线仅 26.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多日常任务不仅需要控制运动，还需要控制接触力；在人形机器人上，这种力往往要在双手与全身之间协调，仅靠运动学轨迹无法表达。因此需要一个能感知并反应接触力、同时保持全身平衡的移动-操作学习框架。

Method: 1）数据采集：使用两台力感知的 UMI 风格手持夹爪记录手部运动与接触力/力矩（wrench），配可穿戴追踪器记录身体运动，无需机器人本体参与采集。2）策略：力反应式扩散策略以视觉、本体感知和测得 wrench 为输入，预测动作与每只手的力指令。3）控制：混合柔顺全身控制器实现力跟踪并维持平衡；利用真实 F/T 传感器测得的力误差积分项，使每只手在指令方向上精确跟踪力、在其他方向上保持柔顺。

Result: 仿真中，控制器能在不同大小与方向的每手力指令下跟踪力，且全身运动精度损失很小。真机三类富接触任务中，力反应策略 80% 的 rollout 成功；而用同一批演示训练、同一控制器执行的位置-only 策略仅 26.7%。

Conclusion: 结果表明：把力作为策略输出、并由具备力跟踪能力的全身柔顺控制器执行，是提升人形机器人富接触移动-操作性能的关键；相比纯位置策略，力反应式方法在成功率上有显著提升。

Abstract: Many everyday tasks require controlling contact force as well as motion. On a humanoid, this force must often be coordinated across both hands and the whole body, and cannot be specified by kinematic trajectories alone. Therefore, we present FLORE, a framework for learning force-reactive humanoid loco-manipulation. We collect robot-free demonstrations using two force-sensing UMI-style handheld grippers for hand motion and contact wrenches, and wearable trackers for body motion. A force-reactive diffusion policy takes vision, proprioception, and measured wrenches as input, then predicts actions and per-hand force commands. A hybrid compliance whole-body controller with force tracking executes these commands while maintaining balance: an integral of the force error measured by real force/torque (F/T) sensors lets each hand track force accurately along the commanded direction and remain compliant along the others. In simulation, the controller tracks per-hand force commands of varied magnitude and direction, with little loss in whole-body motion accuracy. Across three contact-rich tasks on the robot, the force-reactive policy succeeds in 80% of rollouts, compared with 26.7% for a position-only policy trained on the same demonstrations and executed by the same controller. For more details, please visit our website: https://louis-leee.github.io/FLoRe/.
